Abstract: Behavior-cloned visuomotor policies can remain accurate near their training distribution yet fail when object position and camera viewpoint change together. A successful reference trajectory contains the geometry needed to transfer the same interaction, but the policy must align that geometry with the current scene and remain sensitive to it during denoising. To address these challenges, we present MemCorr-DP, a diffusion policy that lifts frozen RoMa v2 matches into explicit 3D relations between the current scene and the reference trajectory. A counterfactual paired objective assigns opposite behaviors the same physical state and noisy action while retaining reference-specific denoising targets. Mixed-condition fine-tuning then adapts the policy from ground-truth geometry to measured correspondence errors. Our strongest evaluation places the Door in the outermost position bands beyond the training support and changes the query camera by $\pm15^\circ$. Under this combined shift, MemCorr-DP achieves 96.67% closed-loop success, compared with 88.00% for a visual Transformer with the same action architecture. Objective ablations and reference interventions show that behavior responds to the selected reference, while matched controls favor the complete relation set over future motion or centroid geometry alone. These results support explicit 3D reference relations as a robust conditioning interface when spatial and viewpoint changes are compounded in the evaluated task.

</details>


### [36] [CAVEAT: Recurrent Multimodal Diffusion Planning for Mapless Aerial Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06623)
*Steven Visch,Nicolò Botteghi,Antonio Franchi,Barbara Bazzana*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出CAVEAT，一种基于扩散策略的无人机自主探索方法，利用多模态机载观测和循环内部状态生成航点序列，无需在部署策略中维护全局地图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索无需全局地图的无人机航点序列生成是否可行，即能否仅依靠多模态机载观测和固定维度的循环内部状态实现自主探索。

Method: CAVEAT采用扩散策略，以融合LiDAR、视觉和位姿特征的循环内部状态为条件，从基于地图的FUELv2专家轨迹中训练。滚动推理部分热启动连续预测，临时局部SDF提供启发式避障引导。

Result: 仿真实验评估了两种推理机制，并与生成演示的专家方法进行了对比。Flyability Elios 3上的概念验证实验展示了在未见过的室内环境中实现部分探索，以及使用独立训练策略进行目标导向视觉伺服。

Conclusion: CAVEAT证明了无需持久全局地图的扩散策略能够实现无人机自主探索，在仿真和真实室内环境中均取得了可行结果。

Abstract: Can exploratory UAV waypoint sequences be generated from multimodal onboard observations and a fixed-dimensional recurrent internal state without maintaining a persistent global map in the deployed policy? We investigate this question through CAVEAT, a diffusion policy conditioned on a recurrent internal state updated from fused LiDAR, visual, and pose features and trained from trajectories generated by the map-based FUELv2 expert. Rolling inference partially warm-starts consecutive predictions, while a temporary local signed distance field provides heuristic obstacle guidance. Simulation results evaluate both inference mechanisms and compare CAVEAT with its demonstration-generating expert. Proof-of-concept experiments on a Flyability Elios 3 demonstrate partial exploration of a previously unseen indoor environment and target-directed visual servoing using a separately trained policy.

</details>


### [37] [SkillX: Unified Multi-Skill Policy Learning for Humanoid Soccer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06718)
*Zhangchen Ye,Enxuan Ruan,Yifei Bao,Runhan Huang,Jiankun Yang,Jiakang Jin,Yixiao Huo,Pengyuan Wang,Yinan Han,Huaxing Huang,Wenhao Cui,Yiming Li,Xiaoyu Tian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SkillX 是一个统一的强化学习框架，通过单一指令条件策略实现人形机器人多种足球技能的联合学习与组合（如运球、停球、射门），在仿真和真实 Noetix E1 人形机器人上验证了多技能执行、长时域技能组合及 sim-to-real 部署的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的人形机器人运动方法多依赖任务特定的多阶段流水线，难以在单一可部署策略中联合学习和组合多种物体交互技能，缺乏统一框架来处理长期运动中的技能切换与协调问题。

Method: SkillX 集成了三个核心设计：技能特定的对抗式运动先验（skill-specific adversarial motion priors）、技能特定的评论网络（skill-specific critics）以及物体感知的时间编码器（object-aware temporal encoder），实现通过单一指令条件策略执行原子技能并在其间切换。

Result: 在仿真环境及真实 Noetix E1 人形机器人上进行了实验，展示了稳健的多技能执行能力、长时域技能组合能力以及成功的仿真到现实部署。

Conclusion: SkillX 验证了统一强化学习框架在人形足球场景中学习与组合多种物体交互技能是可行的，为部署多技能人形运动策略提供了一种有效的统一解决方案。

Abstract: Humanoid soccer is a challenging testbed for dynamic whole-body control, requiring robots to coordinate balance, locomotion, object interaction, and skill switching over long horizons. Existing humanoid sports methods often rely on task-specific multi-stage pipelines, making it difficult to jointly learn and compose multiple object-interactive skills within a single deployable policy. To address this, we present SkillX, a unified reinforcement learning framework that learns and composes multiple atomic soccer skills through a single command-conditioned policy. SkillX integrates three core designs: skill-specific adversarial motion priors, skill-specific critics, and an object-aware temporal encoder, enabling the robot to execute atomic skills and transition among them such as dribbling, trapping, and shooting. Experiments in simulation and on a real Noetix E1 humanoid demonstrate robust multi-skill execution, long-horizon skill composition, and successful sim-to-real deployment.

</details>


### [38] [OCTN: Neural OCT Representations for Robot-Guided Precision Intervention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06810)
*Ravi Prakash,Ryan P. McNabb,Patrick J. Codd,Shan Lin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OCTN是一个隐式神经表示框架，将OCT体扫描转换为连续可微分的组织强度场，支持GPU上的快速体积推理、OCT引导的机器人激光手术表面规划，以及从稀疏B-scan重建稠密体积结构，实现4倍采集加速和亚10μm表面一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原生OCT体积数据是离散采样、各向异性的，难以直接用于下游几何推理和机器人集成，需要一个连续且可微分的组织强度场表示来提升效率和可操作性。

Method: OCTN采用两阶段混合训练策略，结合体素监督与层间插值监督，将离散OCT扫描转换为连续隐式神经场；支持GPU原生存储，并通过Newton和SGD等多种优化策略实现隐式表面发现和表面约束路径规划。

Result: 在Duke TissueOCT数据集及公开数据集上，PSNR超过30dB，训练时间小于10秒，表面一致性保持10微米Chamfer距离内；GPU空间查询速度比传统CPU快43倍；稀疏采集下重建可将采集时间减少4倍。

Conclusion: OCTN提供了一种通用且高效的OCT连续表示框架，在术中引导、机器人手术和稀疏采集重建等应用中展现出强大潜力，数据集与代码已开源。

Abstract: Optical coherence tomography (OCT) offers compact, contactless, micron-scale imaging suitable for intraoperative guidance, but native OCT volumes are discretely sampled, anisotropic, and currently inefficient for downstream geometric reasoning and robot integration. We present OCTN (pronounced "octane"), an implicit neural representation framework that converts volumetric OCT scans into a continuous, differentiable, and spatially faithful tissue-intensity field. OCTN uses a two-stage hybrid training strategy that combines supervision from acquired voxels with inter-slice interpolations, preserving B-scan fidelity while improving continuity in sparsely sampled regions. For versatility, we first show that OCTN enables fast volumetric reasoning by storing the learned tissue representation natively on the GPU, supporting intensity-based spatial queries with up to 43x speedup over conventional CPU processing. We then demonstrate OCTN-enabled OCT-guided robotic laser surgery where the continuous tissue representation supports implicit surface discovery and surface-constrained path planning via multiple optimization strategies, including Newton- and SGD-based optimization. Next, OCTN enables reconstruction of dense volumetric structure from sparsely acquired B-scans, while reducing acquisition time by 4x and preserving clinically relevant structures. Across the newly generated Duke TissueOCT dataset and public OCT datasets, OCTN achieves robust, high-fidelity reconstruction with PSNR > 30 dB and training time < 10 s, while preserving surface consistency within 10 $μ$m Chamfer distance relative to baseline reconstruction. The TissueOCT dataset and code are publicly available at raprakashvi.github.io/octn

</details>

*Yilin Wang,Yiheng Feng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新型动态机器人出租车路由框架，将交通监控任务作为目标函数纳入路径规划，通过单元级时空覆盖指标和混合整数线性规划（MILP）公式，在5×5城市网格网络中验证了该框架能在不同市场渗透率下提升交通监控性能，且适当的目标权重可实现监控性能与车辆平均速度的双赢。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市交通监控对安全分析和拥堵管理至关重要，但传统基础设施传感器覆盖有限，而日益增长的机器人出租车车队可作为分布式"路过式传感器"形成协同感知环境。现有研究未将交通监控任务显式纳入机器人出租车的路径规划目标中，因此需要一种能同时兼顾载客服务和交通数据采集的车辆路由框架。

Method: 提出一个动态机器人出租车路由框架，包含三个核心组件：(1)基于单元的网络表示方法，与机器人出租车的感知能力相匹配；(2)单元级监控指标，用于量化机器人出租车的时空覆盖程度；(3)混合整数线性规划（MILP）公式，联合最小化时变旅行时间并最大化交通监控性能。在SUMO仿真环境中构建5×5城市网格网络，设置三种市场渗透率（2%、5%、10%）及多种目标权重组合进行实验评估。

Result: 结果表明，将时空网络覆盖纳入目标函数可有效提升交通监控性能。值得注意的是，在适当的目标权重下，监控性能和机器人出租车平均速度可同时得到改善，实现双赢效果。

Conclusion: 更好的网络监控有助于更准确的交通状态预测和出行能力提升，这种双赢局面可激励机器人出租车运营商自愿将其车辆作为行驶传感器参与交通监控，为网络级交通监测提供了一种创新的、可持续的解决方案。

Abstract: Urban traffic monitoring plays a critical role in safety analysis, congestion management, and incident response. The growing deployment of robotaxis creates a new opportunity for network-level traffic monitoring. Although robotaxis are primarily designed to serve passengers, they can also be leveraged as drive-by sensors to collect traffic data. Compared to conventional infrastructure sensors or probe vehicles, a fleet of robotaxis forms a cooperative perception environment, which can collectively gather spatially and temporally continuous traffic information. This paper proposes a novel dynamic robotaxi routing framework that explicitly incorporates traffic monitoring tasks as an objective. The framework introduces: (1) a cell-based network representation that aligns with sensing capabilities of robotaxis; (2) a cell-level monitoring metric to quantify spatiotemporal robotaxi coverage; and (3) a mixed-integer linear programming (MILP) formulation that jointly minimizes time-dependent travel time and maximizes traffic monitoring performance. A 5 by 5 urban grid network is built in SUMO to evaluate the framework under three robotaxi market penetration rates (2%, 5%, and 10%) with a range of objective weight combinations. Results show that incorporating spatiotemporal network coverage in the objective function can effectively improve the traffic monitoring performance. Interestingly, with appropriate weights between the two objectives, monitoring performance and robotaxi average speed can be improved simultaneously. This suggests better network monitoring leads to more accurate traffic state prediction and improved mobility. This win-win situation could incentivize robotaxi operators to contribute their vehicles as drive-by sensors for traffic monitoring.

</details>


### [40] [Diagnosing and Dynamically Filtering Occupancy World Models for Active Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06820)
*Jiahui Zhang,Gongbo Liang,Yu Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文诊断了主动建图系统中占用网络预测误差如何影响规划性能，发现仅修正假阳性或假阴性并不能一致改善最终覆盖率，且真实占用对覆盖率效率的提升远大于末端覆盖，据此提出一种动态过滤策略以改善视点选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主动建图中，预训练占用网络的预测误差会同时影响探索视点选择和机器人可达性，但现有研究缺乏对这种影响机制的深入诊断分析。

Method: 固定规划器不变，仅变化提供给规划器的占用表示（包括无补全、学习占用、去假阳性、补假阴性、真实占用），通过对比实验结果与引入动态过滤策略（保留未探索空间预测、抑制在线观察中反复不支持的占用）进行诊断。

Result: 单独修正假阳性或假阴性并不一致地提升最终覆盖率；真实占用对覆盖效率的提升远大于末端覆盖的提升，说明规划与可达性仍是重要瓶颈；动态过滤策略初步实验能引导视点选择朝向可达但本会遗漏的表面。

Conclusion: 占用精度与下游规划性能之间存在差距；仅提高占用预测准确性不足以解决主动建图的瓶颈，需要结合规划与可达性约束进行更系统的优化。

Abstract: Active mapping requires a robot to select camera viewpoints that efficiently reconstruct an unknown 3D scene. To reason about unobserved regions, recent systems use pretrained occupancy networks as world models that complete missing geometry. The predicted structure contributes to expected coverage gain and constrains feasible robot motion. Consequently, occupancy errors can change both what the robot chooses to explore and where it is able to move. We diagnose these effects by holding the planner fixed and varying only the occupancy representation provided to it. We consider planning without completion, with learned occupancy, with false positives removed by a ground truth oracle, with false negatives restored by an oracle, and with ground truth occupancy. Our experiments show that correcting false positives or false negatives alone does not consistently improve final coverage. This finding reveals a gap between occupancy accuracy and downstream planning performance. Ground truth occupancy provides a much larger improvement in coverage efficiency than in endpoint coverage, suggesting that planning and reachability remain important bottlenecks even when the geometric world model is accurate. Based on these findings, we introduce a dynamic filtering strategy that preserves predictions in unexplored space while suppressing repeatedly unsupported occupancy using online observations. Preliminary examples show that this strategy can redirect viewpoint selection toward reachable surfaces that would otherwise remain unobserved.

</details>


### [41] [ContextFlow: In-Context Flow Matching for Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06852)
*Jian Ding,Xianjie Dai,Roei Herzig,Nussair Hroub,Jinjie Mai,Dengxin Dai,Bernard Ghanem,Mohamed Elhoseiny*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出ContextFlow，一种基于条件流匹配的上下文模仿学习方法。它将演示和观测作为条件，对连续动作分布进行建模，并引入感知器风格的多模态上下文压缩器来提取紧凑的任务相关潜在表示，在LIBERO基准和真实机器人任务上均取得显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自回归上下文模仿方法需要离散化连续动作并通过next-token预测进行推理，导致早期预测误差累积，限制了对未见任务配置的泛化能力。虽然流匹配策略已被用于连续机器人控制并可缓解复合误差，但流匹配框架下的上下文模仿学习仍未被充分探索。

Method: ContextFlow采用条件流匹配模型学习连续动作分布，以演示和观测为条件进行流式动作预测，从含噪动作分布中生成鲁棒动作。同时，引入感知器风格的多模态上下文压缩器，将视觉、本体感觉和动作序列蒸馏为紧凑的任务相关潜在表示，以更好地编码多模态上下文演示。

Result: 在LIBERO基准上，ContextFlow在未见任务配置上比ICRT平均成功率高出35个百分点，与无需微调的特定任务微调VLA模型π0性能相当。在真实机器人上，它泛化到单臂和双臂任务的新配置，在新型笔开盖配置上达到40%成功率。

Conclusion: ContextFlow证明了流匹配框架在上下文模仿学习中的有效性，通过连续动作分布建模和高效多模态上下文编码，在仿真和真实机器人上均展现出强大的泛化能力，无需针对新任务进行微调即可匹配甚至超越现有方法。

Abstract: Although highly effective in vision and language domains, applying in-context learning to robotics remains challenging. Existing autoregressive in-context imitation methods discretize continuous actions and exacerbate the accumulation of early prediction errors through next-token prediction, limiting their generalization on unseen task configurations. Meanwhile, flow-matching policies have been explored for continuous robot control and can help mitigate compounding errors; however, in-context imitation learning within a flow-matching framework remains underexplored. To address these limitations, we introduce ContextFlow, a conditional flow-matching model that learns continuous action distributions for in-context imitation learning. ContextFlow conditions flow-based action prediction on demonstrations and observations, enabling robust generation from noisy action distributions. To better encode multimodal in-context demonstrations, we adapt perceiver-style multimodal context compressors that distill visual, proprioceptive, and action sequences into compact, task-relevant latent representations. On LIBERO, ContextFlow outperforms ICRT by 35 percentage points in average success rate on unseen task configurations, while matching the performance of the task-specific fine-tuned VLA model $π_0$ without any fine-tuning on unseen tasks. On real robots, it generalizes to unseen configurations of both single-arm and bimanual tasks, achieving 40% success on a new pen-uncapping configuration. Project Page: https://dingjiansw101.github.io/contextflow-page/.

</details>


### [42] [Predictive-Coding-Based Autonomous Regulation of Internally Generated and Externally Coupled Processing in Human-Robot Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06888)
*Henrique Oyama,Hiroki Sawada,Jun Tani*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种基于预测编码的在线调节机制，利用最近交互历史中累积的重建误差在预设的元先验区间之间动态切换，从而在物理人机交互过程中实时平衡内部生成预测与外部感觉证据的权重。该机制在三种交互任务中均得到验证，重建历史驱动的区间选择不仅降低了重建误差，还减少了前瞻性预测误差和机器人侧物理交互冲突。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测编码理论认为适应性行为是内部预测与外部感觉证据间的动态平衡，但具体的具身认知系统如何在持续交互中在线调节这一平衡仍不清楚。现有研究缺乏对内部生成与外部耦合处理之间调节机制的明确理解。

Method: 采用基于预测编码的变分循环神经网络（PV-RNN），其中元先验控制后验推断受学习先验动力学约束的程度。扩展该架构，提出一种在线机制，利用最近交互历史累积的重建误差在预设的元先验区间之间进行选择。在固定结构化、变化结构化和较少约束三种物理人机交互任务中进行评估。

Result: 所有任务中，较低的元先验值导致预期的后验-先验分歧增加和重建误差降低。更重要的是，重建历史驱动的区间选择还与前瞻性预测误差降低和机器人侧物理交互冲突减少相关。任务3表明，最近的感官观察可被成功容纳，而后续人类运动仍偏离模型先验生成的未来轨迹。

Conclusion: 累积的重建失配可以提供一种内源性信号，用于调节后续推断在多大程度上依赖学习的内部动力学相对于持续的感官输入，这为具身交互中的预测编码平衡监控提供了新的机制性理解。

Abstract: Predictive coding characterizes adaptive behavior as a dynamic balance between internally generated predictions and external sensory evidence, yet how an embodied cognitive system can regulate this balance online during ongoing interaction remains poorly understood. This study proposes a predictive-coding-based mechanism for regulating internally generated and externally coupled processing during physical human--robot interaction. The framework employs a predictive-coding-inspired variational recurrent neural network (PV-RNN), in which a meta-prior controls the degree to which posterior inference is constrained by learned prior dynamics. We extend this architecture with an online mechanism that uses reconstruction error accumulated over recent interaction history to select between predefined meta-prior regimes. The mechanism was evaluated across three physical human--robot interaction tasks involving fixed structured, changing structured, and less-constrained interaction. Across all tasks, lower meta-prior values produced the expected increase in posterior--prior divergence and reduction in reconstruction error. More importantly, reconstruction-history-driven regime selection was also associated with reduced prospective prediction error and robot-side physical interaction conflict, demonstrating consequences beyond the retrospective reconstruction objective itself. Task~3 further showed that recent sensory observations can be successfully accommodated while subsequent human motion still departs from the model's prior-generated future trajectory. Overall, these findings show that accumulated reconstruction mismatch can provide an endogenous signal for regulating how strongly subsequent inference relies on learned internal dynamics relative to ongoing sensory input during embodied interaction.

</details>


### [43] [Distributed Secure Learning Control for Large-scale Multirobots under Stealthy Actuator Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06896)
*Xinglong Zhang,Qingwen Ma,Cong Li,Hui Yin,Changxin Zhang,Yueying Wang,Wei Pan,Xin Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向大规模多机器人系统的分布式安全学习控制（DSLC）框架，在存在恶意隐蔽执行器攻击的情况下，通过博弈论分布式学习预测控制策略实现安全控制，并在仿真和真实轮式机器人实验中验证了其有效性和可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式学习控制虽在不确定性下具有灵活性但缺乏可证明的性能保证，而将强化学习集成到分布式模型预测控制中虽有前景，但在恶意网络攻击（特别是隐蔽攻击）下如何确保安全控制仍是关键挑战，因为分布式策略生成依赖邻居间信息交换，被攻陷的智能体可通过通信网络迅速影响其他智能体行为。

Method: 提出DSLC框架，包含两个关键特性：(i) 统一方法支持多种协调场景下的安全学习控制；(ii) 基于微分博弈的DMPC框架，采用分布式攻击者-演员-评论家（attacker-actor-critic）架构在预测区间内在线学习最优防御和攻击策略，以解析闭环形式同时生成对抗性攻击策略和对应防御策略。

Result: DSLC生成的防御策略可直接推广到不同规模、不同执行器攻击概率的多机器人系统；通过综合仿真和多个轮式机器人的真实实验，在多种控制任务中验证了方法的有效性和可扩展性。

Conclusion: 该工作为大规模多机器人在隐蔽执行器攻击下提供了可扩展的安全学习控制方案，通过博弈论视角统一了攻击与防御策略的在线学习，具备良好的泛化能力。

Abstract: Distributed learning control for multirobot systems (MRS) offers significant flexibility in presence of uncertainties but lacks provable performance guarantees. A promising direction involves integrating reinforcement learning (RL) into distributed model predictive control (DMPC), leveraging the strengths of RL in nonlinear policy design and the receding-horizon replanning capabilities of DMPC. However, ensuring secure control within such a learning framework under malicious cyber attacks, particularly stealthy ones, remains a critical challenge, because the distributed policies generation depends on information exchange among neighbors, where compromised agents can rapidly influence the behavior of others through the communication network. This article proposes a distributed secure learning control (DSLC) framework for large-scale MRS under malicious, stealthy actuator attacks. Our framework offers two key features: (i) a unified approach that enables secure learning control across various coordination scenarios and (ii) a game-theoretic distributed learning-based predictive control strategy that learns how to balance the attacker and defender through a differential-game based DMPC framework. Specifically, DSLC employs a distributed attacker-actor-critic architecture to learn the optimal defense and attack policies online within each prediction interval. Unlike numerical optimization-based controllers that calculate open-loop control sequences, our method simultaneously generates adversarial attack policies and corresponding defense policies in analytical closed-loop form. The defense policies could be directly generalized to MRS with varying scales and diverse actuator attack probabilities. The effectiveness and scalability of DSLC are validated through comprehensive simulations and real-world experiments in multiple wheeled robots via various control tasks.

</details>


### [44] [DriftParking: Trajectory Modeling via Drifting Field for End-to-End Automated Parking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06923)
*Ziyan Wang,Dong Li,Weibo Wang,Yinyin Lu,Jiayu Xie,Jiamao Gu,Dongpeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出DriftParking，一种一步式轨迹生成框架，通过重构漂移场范式实现高精度条件轨迹生成，在端到端泊车任务中达到SOTA性能，实车测试拥有97%泊车成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端泊车方法难以同时兼顾推理效率、轨迹质量和精确终点对齐；传统模仿学习目标对专家机动几何的结构化偏差提供的监督有限。

Method: 将分布级吸引替换为对配对专家轨迹的条件一对一吸引；引入以专家为中心的构造性排斥，并在收敛附近自适应衰减排斥；在端点残差空间中表述轨迹生成，分解为起点到目标基线与可学习残差，使端点对齐与排斥监督获得结构化空间。

Result: 所有评估指标上均达到最先进水平；跨多种泊车场景的闭环车载实验显示97%的泊车成功率，展现强零样本泛化能力。

Conclusion: DriftParking通过重构漂移场机制与端点残差空间表述，有效解决了高约束空间中精确终点对齐与轨迹质量之间的权衡问题，具备实际部署价值。

Abstract: Automated parking requires generating complete and executable trajectories in highly constrained spaces with low tolerance for goal pose error. Existing end-to-end parking methods struggle to jointly achieve inference efficiency, trajectory quality, and precise endpoint alignment, while conventional imitation objectives provide limited supervision on structured deviations from expert maneuver geometry. We propose DriftParking, a one-step trajectory generation framework that reconstructs the drifting-field paradigm for high-precision conditional trajectory generation. Specifically, we replace distribution-level attraction with conditional one-to-one attraction toward the paired expert trajectory, introduce expert-centered constructive repulsion, and adaptively attenuate repulsion near convergence. We further formulate trajectory generation in an endpoint-residual space by decomposing each trajectory into a start-to-goal baseline and a learnable residual, turning endpoint alignment into a representation-level structural constraint on the supervision target while providing a structured space for repulsive supervision. DriftParking achieves state-of-the-art performance across all evaluation metrics. Closed-loop on-vehicle experiments across diverse parking scenarios further show a 97% parking success rate, demonstrating strong zero-shot generalization.

</details>


### [45] [Distributed Dexterous Manipulation with Spatially Conditioned Multi-Agent Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06930)
*Sarvesh Patil*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于空间条件多智能体Transformer（MAT）的框架，用于高效学习分布式灵巧操作（DDM）系统的鲁棒控制策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式灵巧操作（DDM）作为一种新颖范式，面临着高动作空间冗余、机器人间协作以及动态物体-机器人交互等重大控制挑战，需要更高效的策略学习方法。

Method: 提出三大核心贡献：(i) 带自适应层归一化的多智能体Transformer（MAT）提升计算效率；(ii) 空间对比嵌入将Transformer嵌入与机器人的空间构型相结合；(iii) 基于MAT的行为克隆方法并使用Soft Actor Critic进行微调。此外，还提出了一种动作选择公式来分析任务性能与机器人使用数量之间的权衡。

Result: 实验表明MAT通过堆叠注意力块迭代优化动作，并验证了空间条件化在Transformer中的优势。在仿真和真实世界中展示了多种几何形状物体的长时程平面操作任务，动作选择在减少约65%机器人使用量的情况下仍能达到约1.5厘米的平均误差。

Conclusion: 空间条件化Transformer能有效学习DDM控制策略，动作选择机制可减少机器人磨损和维护成本，同时保持物体操纵能力。

Abstract: Distributed Dexterous Manipulation (DDM) is a novel paradigm that presents significant control challenges due to high action-space redundancy, inter-robot cooperation, and dynamic object-robot interactions. This paper introduces a framework based on spatially conditioned Multi-Agent Transformers (MATs) to efficiently learn robust control policies for a DDM system grounded in an array of 64 soft delta robots arranged in an 8x8 grid. Our three core contributions are: (i) an MAT with adaptive layer norm for compute efficiency, (ii) spatial contrastive embeddings to ground transformer embeddings in the spatial configuration of the robots, and (iii) an MAT-based behavior cloning method fine-tuned using Soft Actor Critic. We also propose an action selection formulation to analyze the trade-off between task performance and the number of robots utilized. Our experiments show that MATs iteratively refine their actions through the stacked attention blocks. This further informs the benefit of spatial conditioning in transformers to learn DDM policies. We demonstrate long-horizon planar manipulation tasks with objects of various geometries in simulation and real-world. Finally, we show how action selection mitigates robot maintenance by reducing wear and tear due to inter-robot collisions while maintaining the ability to manipulate objects along various trajectories in the real-world, achieving an average error of ~1.5 cm, while using ~65% fewer robots.

</details>


### [46] [Mind the Phase: Effective Rank and Representation Health in Legged Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06958)
*Felipe Tommaselli,Thiago H. Segreto,Juliano D. Negri,Ricardo V. Godoy,Marcelo Becker*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文通过策略雅可比矩阵的有效秩来研究腿式运动策略的表征，发现按步态相位条件化秩可以揭示标准架构（层归一化和残差连接）在摆动相和支撑相之间分配约两个额外维度的有效秩，而普通MLP中不存在该现象。基于此提出简单配方，可将模拟到现实的转移变得更平滑可靠，在仿真和实体Spot上均实现约3倍更低的关节抖动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在PPO非平稳训练下，浅层网络仍是腿式运动的主导架构，但对此类策略学习到的表征缺乏理解，且没有训练时信号能预测其在真实硬件上的表现，这促使作者研究隐藏的表征结构与sim-to-real行为之间的关联。

Method: 作者实证研究腿部运动策略，使用策略雅可比矩阵的有效秩作为分析工具，并将秩按步态相位进行条件化，以揭示全局秩平均会掩盖的架构结构。他们还提出一种基于这些表征特征的配方，用于改善sim-to-real迁移的平滑性和可靠性。

Result: 标准架构选择（层归一化和残差连接）在摆动相较支撑相多分配约两个维度的有效秩，而普通MLP完全没有此特征。所提出的方法在实际测试中使关节抖动降低约3倍，且该改善从仿真一致保持到物理Spot机器人上。

Conclusion: 表征健康度（representation health）可以作为一种有效的训练时视角来追踪sim-to-real的平滑性，表明通过表征分析可以预判策略在真实硬件上的行为表现，为腿式运动策略的训练与部署提供可解释的监控信号。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning has become the leading paradigm in legged locomotion, enabling complex behaviors from backflips to parkour through massively parallel simulation. Under PPO's non-stationarity, shallow networks remain the de facto architecture, supported by carefully staged curricula and environments, yet the representations these policies learn stay poorly understood, leaving no training-time signal of how they will behave on hardware. In this work, we empirically study locomotion policies through the effective rank of the policy Jacobian and show that conditioning rank on the gait phase exposes architectural structure that global rank averages away. In particular, we find that standard architectural choices, namely layer normalization and residual connections, allocate roughly two more dimensions of effective rank to swing than to stance, which is fully absent in vanilla MLPs. Building on this, we propose a simple recipe that turns these representational signatures into smoother, more reliable sim-to-real transfer. In practice, this results in roughly 3x lower joint jitter that holds from simulation onto a physical Spot, suggesting that representation health is an effective training-time lens to track sim-to-real smoothness.

</details>


### [47] [WM-Craftnet: World Synesthesia Model for Generalizable and Robust Dexterous In-Hand Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07002)
*Jie Yin,Zeyuan Zhao,Xiaojing Tan,Yang Liu,Chiyu Wang,Xinyang Gu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出WM-Craftnet，一种基于世界模型条件的灵巧手操作框架，通过学习动作条件潜动力学模型，将World Synesthesia Model作为循环任务上下文提供给非对称actor-critic策略，实现多物体旋转的鲁棒操作，并支持从九天轴物体预训练迁移到49物体下游策略学习及sim-to-real部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的触觉和视觉触觉强化学习方法在受控环境下能实现强内手旋转，但在姿态偏移、力扰动和物体变化等情况下鲁棒性显著下降，需要一种能从不完整和噪声观测中推断物体姿态、几何、接触和潜在滑动的泛化性更强、更稳健的策略。

Method: 提出WM-Craftnet框架，从本体感觉、深度、触觉和动作学习紧凑的动作条件潜动力学模型，通过多模态重建和奖励预测进行监督；不同于传统使用世界模型做潜在想象或策略优化，该方法将学习的World Synesthesia Model作为循环任务上下文提供给非对称actor-critic策略；WSM被训练从噪声深度输入重建干净的深度目标，提供去噪的几何状态用于真机部署。

Result: 在九个z轴物体上预训练的WSM可作为49物体下游策略学习的可复用先验，该上下文改善了多物体旋转能力；消融实验表明预测性世界建模、干净深度监督和触觉接触线索共同塑造了学习状态；在未见物体、扰动恢复和sim-to-real迁移方面提供了定量和定性证据。

Conclusion: WM-Craftnet证明了世界模型作为任务上下文而非直接用于策略优化的有效性，通过去噪深度重建和世界模型预训练实现跨物体泛化，为灵巧手操作的鲁棒性和可迁移性提供了新的途径。

Abstract: Generalizable and robust dexterous in-hand manipulation requires a policy to infer object pose, geometry, contact, and potential slip from partial and noisy observations. Although recent tactile and visuotactile RL methods achieve strong in-hand rotation in controlled settings, their robustness often degrades under pose shifts, force disturbances, and object variation. We propose WM-Craftnet, a world-model-conditioned framework that learns compact action-conditioned latent dynamics from proprioception, depth, tactile sensing, and actions, supervised by multimodal reconstruction and reward prediction. Rather than using the world model for latent imagination or policy optimization, WM-Craftnet uses the learned World Synesthesia Model (WSM) as recurrent task context for an asymmetric actor--critic policy. Importantly, WSM is trained to reconstruct clean depth targets from noisy depth inputs, providing a denoised geometric state for real-robot deployment. Ablations over recurrent baselines, auxiliary heads, tactile masking, and WSM modality heads show that predictive world modeling, clean-depth supervision, and tactile contact cues all shape the learned state. A WSM pretrained on nine \(z\)-axis objects serves as a reusable prior for \(49\)-object downstream policy learning. This context improves multi-object rotation, with quantitative and qualitative evidence for unseen-object, perturbation-recovery, and sim-to-real transfer.

</details>


### [48] [MEMOBench: A Process Level Memory Benchmark for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07047)
*Haiyang Sun,Haoxiao Wang,Junming Chen,Weicheng Fang,Zihao Su,Jingkun Yi,Wenyou Yi,Hao Chen,Zhou Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出MEMOBench，一个针对机器人操作中过程级记忆评估的基准，包含30个历史依赖任务、1500条专家演示和4200个可执行检查点实例，通过粗到细的语言与模拟器谓词配对，标注存储、更新、压缩三类记忆操作，并定义对应的记忆保真度指标来独立衡量记忆能力与任务成功。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略通常在当前观测主导决策的场景下评估，但机器人操作常需处理不可见的历史信息。已有记忆基准仅依赖最终任务成功率，导致记忆遗忘与操作失败被混淆，缺乏过程级别的记忆评估手段。

Method: 构建MEMOBench基准：包含30个历史依赖任务、1500条专家演示、4200个可执行检查点（来自84个模板）。每个检查点将粗到细的语言描述与模拟器谓词配对，标注存储、更新或压缩三类记忆操作。提出Memory Storage Rate、Memory Update Rate、Memory Compression Rate三个指标，从过程层面衡量记忆保真度。此外，利用检查点语言监督语义、对比和帧级记忆对齐目标，实现记忆模块的训练。

Result: 在标准和记忆增强的VLA策略上，最优的记忆模块基线平均成功率仅达31.9%，且高存储率往往伴随弱更新与弱压缩能力。利用检查点语言训练的语义、对比、帧级记忆对齐目标在不同记忆操作上带来适度提升。

Conclusion: MEMOBench提供了评估和训练两个层面的贡献：一方面作为诊断性评估套件揭示当前记忆增强策略在更新和压缩能力上的不足；另一方面提供训练监督信号，为记忆基础机器人策略的发展提供了支撑工具。

Abstract: Robotic manipulation often requires acting on information that is no longer visible, yet Vision-Language-Action policies are usually evaluated when the current observation largely determines the next action. Existing robotic memory benchmarks expose this gap, but they still rely mainly on final task success and therefore conflate forgetting with manipulation failure. We present \textbf{MEMOBench}, a benchmark for process level memory evaluation in robotic manipulation. MEMOBench includes 30 history dependent tasks, 1{,}500 expert demonstrations, and 4{,}200 executable checkpoint instances from 84 templates. Each checkpoint pairs coarse to fine language with a simulator predicate and labels one memory operation: Storage, Update, or Compression. These annotations define Memory Storage Rate, Memory Update Rate, and Memory Compression Rate, which measure memory fidelity alongside task success. Across standard and memory augmented VLA policies, the strongest memory module baseline reaches only 31.9\% average success rate, and high storage often coexists with weak update and compression. Checkpoint language also supervises semantic, contrastive, and framewise memory alignment objectives, yielding modest gains across different memory operations. MEMOBench provides a diagnostic evaluation suite and training supervision for memory grounded robotic policies. The project page is available at https://github.com/Collab-Gen/MEMOBench.

</details>


### [49] [Large Discrete Policy: Advancing Explicit Behavior Modeling with Stochastic Iterative Scoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07049)
*Zhenxin Li,Nadine Chang,Xinglong Sun,Jingde Chen,Wenhao Yao,Zi Wang,Maying Shen,Yu-Gang Jiang,Zuxuan Wu,Shiyi Lan,Jose M. Alvarez*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出大型离散策略（LDiP），一种完全离散的行为建模框架，通过从大规模物理可行候选动作词汇表中进行随机迭代评分与剪枝来提升表达能力，在自动驾驶、机器人操作等任务中优于或匹敌连续生成式策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续生成式行为策略的迭代去噪过程虽然表达力强，但难以解释且容易产生不切实际的动作。现有方法缺乏兼具表达性、合理性与可解释性的行为建模方案。

Method: LDiP采用完全离散的行为建模框架，不再扰动动作，而是通过评分空间中的随机性进行渐进式迭代评分与候选剪枝，在保留显式决策过程的同时实现精细排序和合理动作空间中的探索。

Result: 在端到端规划、闭环驾驶、机器人操作和视觉-语言-动作设置中，LDiP在自动驾驶任务中一致优于强离散与连续基线，在机器人操作任务中超越或匹敌连续生成式策略。

Conclusion: 结果表明，当离散策略配备有效的评分机制时，可以为行为建模提供一种具有表达性、合理性和可解释性的替代方案。

Abstract: Behavior policies are often formulated as continuous generative models, whose iterative denoising processes are expressive but difficult to interpret and prone to producing implausible actions. We propose the Large Discrete Policy (LDiP), a fully discrete behavior modeling framework that selects actions from a large vocabulary of physically plausible candidates. Rather than perturbing actions, LDiP improves expressivity through stochastic iterative scoring: it progressively re-scores and prunes candidates with score-space stochasticity, enabling fine-grained ranking and exploration among plausible actions while preserving an explicit decision process. Across end-to-end planning, closed-loop driving, robotic manipulation, and vision-language-action settings, LDiP consistently outperforms strong discrete and continuous baselines in autonomous driving, and exceeds or matches continuous generative policies in robotic manipulation. These results show that discrete policies, when equipped with effective scoring mechanisms, offer an expressive, plausible, and interpretable alternative for behavior modeling. Project website: https://zhenxinli.net/LargeDiscretePolicy/.

</details>


### [50] [Human-Aware Target Tracking and Navigation: Fusing Kinematic State Estimation with Structural Map Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07091)
*Sagar Gupta,Don Gideon,Seng W. Loke,Kevin Lee,Bijo Sebastian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套端到端的自主导航系统，解决自主移动机器人跟随行人在动态环境中因遮挡或传感器丢失跟踪的问题。系统融合多模态感知（深度学习视觉跟踪与2D LiDAR点聚类），并在跟踪丢失时基于预定义拓扑地图执行图搜索，预测目标可行轨迹并指导局部避障规划器，直至目标重新被视觉获取。在密集多人环境中实测，重大遮挡事件下目标重新获取成功率达71.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主移动机器人执行行人跟随任务时，动态环境中常因临时遮挡和传感器跟踪丢失导致跟随失败，现有方法缺乏有效的恢复机制。

Method: 系统包含四部分：1) 多模态感知管线，融合深度学习视觉跟踪与2D LiDAR点聚类实现目标跟踪；2) 连续状态估计器，融合感知数据与轮式里程计和IMU；3) 地图引导的恢复框架，激活基于拓扑地图的图搜索，沿行走通道传播目标轨迹并遵循左侧通行惯例，生成离散的可行未来轨迹集；4) 将地图信息预测直接输入局部避障规划器。

Result: 在真实密集多人物环境中验证，系统在长达7秒的重大遮挡事件中实现了71.4%的目标重新获取成功率。

Conclusion: 基于地图信息的空间推理能够有效缓解遮挡导致的跟踪丢失问题，使机器人能在目标暂时不可见时安全、可预测地继续跟随，直至重新获取目标视觉信息。

Abstract: Autonomous mobile robots performing person-following tasks often suffer from temporary occlusions and sensor track loss in dynamic environments. This research presents an end-to-end autonomous navigation stack that addresses target occlusion through map-informed spatial reasoning. The proposed system features a multi-modal perception pipeline, fusing deep learning-based visual tracking with 2-dimensional LiDAR point clustering to maintain high-fidelity tracking of a tagged person. A continuous state estimator integrates this perception data with wheel odometry and IMU sensors for stable localization. When the active track is lost due to occlusion, the system activates a map-based recovery framework. Leveraging a predefined topological map, the system executes a graph-based search to propagate the target's last known trajectory along structurally defined walking lanes, adhering to left-hand regional conventions. By generating a discrete set of feasible future trajectories, the robot reasons about potential structural trajectory changes, such as continuing a heading or turning at an intersection. This map-informed prediction is fed directly to the local obstacle avoidance planner, enabling the robot to continue following its target safely and predictably until the person is visually reacquired. Real-world evaluations in dense multi-person environments demonstrate the system's robustness, achieving a 71.4\% target reacquisition success rate during major occlusion events lasting up to 7 seconds.

</details>


### [51] [RoboDreamer: Anticipatory Humanoid Locomotion with Predictive State-Space Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07096)
*Zhe Li,Yangyang Wei,Xichen Yuan,Zhenzhe Zhang,Weihao Yuan,Shanghang Zhang,Jianfei Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboDreamer提出了一种两阶段教师-学生框架，通过随机连续时间掩蔽和下一观测一致性来训练人形机器人运动控制策略，在观测缺失时仍能利用历史信息维持稳定运动，并在IsaacLab、MuJoCo和Unitree G1上验证了效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人运动控制需要在不完美感知条件下保持稳定，同时利用时间上下文生成连贯运动，现有方法在观测缺失时缺乏鲁棒性。

Method: 采用两阶段教师-学生蒸馏框架：先训练教师策略于干净观测，再让学生在掩蔽最近观测的条件下蒸馏，迫使策略从历史推断缺失的当前信息；推理时复用同一掩蔽接口实现隐式闭环动作精化和可选的多步动作分块；采用Mamba作为时序骨干网络。

Result: 消融实验表明掩蔽/蒸馏贡献了大部分性能提升，Mamba进一步改善了跟踪精度且满足实时延迟要求；在IsaacLab、MuJoCo仿真和Unitree G1实体机器人上均实现了在观测掩蔽下的鲁棒运动跟踪和成功的真实世界部署。

Conclusion: 证明了将时间掩蔽与教师-学生蒸馏结合是提升人形机器人在感知受限条件下运动鲁棒性的有效途径，且该方法具备实际部署可行性。

Abstract: Humanoid locomotion requires control policies that remain stable under imperfect sensing while exploiting temporal context for consistent motion. We present RoboDreamer, a two-stage teacher--student framework that combines next-observation consistency with randomized continuous temporal masking. A teacher is first trained on clean observations, and a student is then distilled under masked recent observations, encouraging the policy to infer missing current information from history. At inference, the same masking interface is reused for implicit closed-loop action refinement and optional multi-step action chunking. Mamba is used as the temporal backbone, while matched ablations show that masking/distillation provides a substantial part of the gain and Mamba contributes additional tracking improvements with real-time latency. Experiments in IsaacLab, MuJoCo, and on a Unitree G1 demonstrate robust motion tracking under observation masking and successful real-world deployment.

</details>


### [52] [Eventually Optimal and Scalable Multi-Agent Planning for Block Cave Mining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07109)
*Christopher Leet,Paolo Forte,Uwe Köckemann,Henrik Andreasson,Sven Koenig*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对地下矿山的自动驾驶车队协调问题，形式化了Block Cave Mining (BCM)问题并通过SAMM/SAMMS求解器实现近最优吞吐量优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地下采矿自动化有望提升安全性和效率，但动态矿山环境中多个自主车辆的协调在优化和运动规划上具有重大挑战。

Method: 提出SAMM，一个通过混合整数线性规划公式联合集成任务分配、调度和路径规划的最终最优任意时刻求解器；以及SAMMS，通过将问题分解为较短规划子周期以牺牲最优性换取效率的变体。

Result: 在逼真的工业矿山场景下，SAMMS实现了接近最优的吞吐量，并能有效扩展到更大的车队规模和矿山布局。

Conclusion: SAMMS在可扩展性和吞吐量性能之间取得了良好的平衡，适合实际大规模地下矿山部署。

Abstract: Automation in underground mining has the potential to significantly enhance safety, operational efficiency, and sustainability. However, effectively coordinating fleets of autonomous vehicles in dynamic mine environments introduces substantial challenges in both optimization and motion planning. To address these challenges, we introduce and formalize the \emph{Block Cave Mining (BCM)} problem, which focuses on computing a transport plan that maximizes ore throughput while satisfying draw ratio constraints. To solve this problem, we propose SAMM, an eventually optimal anytime solver that jointly integrates task assignment, scheduling, and path planning via a mixed-integer linear programming formulation. To improve scalability, we also introduce SAMMS, a variant of SAMM that trades optimality guarantees for efficiency by decomposing the problem into shorter planning subcycles. Experimental evaluations using realistic industrial mine scenarios demonstrate that SAMMS achieves near-optimal throughput and scales effectively to larger fleets and mine layouts.

</details>


### [53] [From LLM-Generated Specifications to Learned Quadruped Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07111)
*Merve Atasever,Keyan Azbijari,Cagan Bakirci,Alfredo Reina Corona,Tolga Izdas,Richard Yang,Erdem Biyik,Jyotirmoy V. Deshmukh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究利用大语言模型（LLM）自动生成参数化信号时序逻辑（PSTL）规范，以替代人工设计奖励函数来训练四足机器人运动策略。给定自然语言目标，GPT-5.5和Qwen 3.6独立生成STL模板，通过专家轨迹实例化参数并筛选一致性规范，最终转化为平滑奖励函数并使用PPO在MJX中训练。实验表明，步态感知的Qwen 3.6规范在所有测试速度下实现100%生存率和命令成功率，优于手工奖励和Text2Reward等方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习训练四足机器人运动策略依赖大量手工设计的奖励函数，工程量大且难以判断局部奖励能否诱导期望的全局行为。虽然STL等形式化规范能提供更可解释的奖励，但编写STL规范本身仍需要领域专业知识。研究者希望通过LLM的语义理解能力自动生成STL规范，降低奖励设计门槛。

Method: 给定自然语言运动目标和受限规范语法，使用GPT-5.5和Qwen 3.6独立提出面向命令跟踪、安全性和步态结构的STL模板；利用专家轨迹实例化PSTL模板参数并仅保留与专家行为一致的规范；将筛选后的规范转化为平滑有限历史奖励函数，用于PPO策略训练。评估涵盖步态感知（指定walking-trot、trot、bound）和步态无关（接触模式自然涌现）两种设置。

Result: 步态感知的Qwen 3.6规范在0.3-2.1 m/s所有测试速度下实现100%生存率和命令成功率，并在高速下匹配目标步态；而Text2Reward在≥1.9 m/s时两个指标均为0%。实验对比了手工奖励、Text2Reward式LLM奖励代码和专家切换oracle。

Conclusion: LLM能够自动生成可用的STL规范用于四足机器人运动策略训练，在高速场景下显著优于Text2Reward等基线方法，验证了将LLM语义理解与形式化规范结合用于机器人奖励设计的可行性。

Abstract: Quadruped robot locomotion policies are often trained using reinforcement learning, which in turn relies heavily on hand-crafted reward functions. Designing reward functions requires substantial manual engineering, and it is often unclear which local rewards will induce the desired global behavior. Shaped rewards from formal specifications in languages like Signal Temporal Logic (STL) can make rewards more interpretable, but writing STL specifications itself still requires domain expertise. We study whether large language models (LLMs) can fill this gap by generating Parametric Signal Temporal Logic (PSTL) specifications that are subsequently used for policy learning. Given a natural language locomotion objective and a constrained specification grammar, GPT-5.5 and Qwen 3.6 independently propose STL templates for command tracking, safety, and gait structure. We instantiate the parameters of the generated PSTL templates using expert trajectories and retain only specifications that are consistent with demonstrated expert behavior. The resulting specifications are then transformed into smooth, finite-history reward functions and used to train a quadruped locomotion policy with Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) in MuJoCo XLA (MJX). We evaluate both \emph{gait-aware} and \emph{gait-agnostic} settings. The former specifies walking-trot, trot, and bound regimes, while the latter allows contact patterns to emerge from the task objective. We compare against hand-engineered rewards, Text2Reward-style LLM-generated reward code, and an expert-switching oracle. Gait-aware Qwen 3.6 specifications achieved 100\% survival and command success across all tested speeds (0.3--2.1 m/s) and matched the target gait at high speeds, whereas Text2Reward achieved 0\% for both metrics at $\geq 1.9$ m/s. Videos: https://stl-locomotion.github.io/

</details>


### [54] [MAC-I$^2$: Learned Metrics-Aware Covariance for Robust Visual-Inertial Fusion in Initialization and Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07116)
*Xiang Fei,Yuheng Qiu,Can Xu,Yutian Chen,Ruogu Li,Xingxing Zuo,Wenshan Wang,Sebastian Scherer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出MAC-I²，通过为视觉和惯性两种模态学习度量感知的协方差（metric-aware covariance），实现鲁棒的视觉-惯性融合，显著提升VI初始化与校准系统的准确性和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-惯性融合方法使用预定义的固定不确定性来融合相机和IMU测量，无法反映每种模态在局部场景中的实际可靠性，在光照变化、动态物体和无纹理区域等挑战性环境下性能不佳。

Method: 1) 在视觉侧，将学习到的特征匹配不确定性传播为位姿协方差用于融合；2) 在惯性侧，设计带可学习初始协方差的IMU模型，并提出在保留训练子集上的专门微调策略，使预测的协方差能在未见序列上具有度量感知能力。

Result: 在EuRoC和VBR数据集上，MAC-I²显著优于现有方法：EuRoC上初始化成功率99.9%，重力误差和速度误差相比最强基线分别降低约60%和42%；在VBR挑战性序列上成功率保持80%，而VINS-Mono等基线方法低于10%。

Conclusion: 通过学习度量感知的协方差使视觉和IMU能够依据各自实际可靠性进行竞争融合，而非依赖预定义的不确定性，为实现鲁棒的VI融合提供了新思路，尤其对VI系统的初始化和校准这一前提环节有显著提升。

Abstract: Visual-Inertial (VI) fusion is fundamental to accurate and robust state estimation, where camera and IMU measurements are combined according to their respective uncertainties. Existing methods, however, fuse the two modalities with predefined uncertainties, regardless of how reliable each is in the local context, and thus often struggle under challenging environments involving illumination changes, dynamic objects, and textureless regions. In this paper, we present MAC-I$^2$, which achieves robust VI fusion through learned metric-aware covariance for both modalities, so that vision and IMU compete on their own merits rather than relying on predefined uncertainties. Here, metrics-aware means that each predicted covariance faithfully reflects the actual magnitude of the corresponding measurement noise. On the visual side, we propagate learned feature-matching uncertainties into pose covariances for the fusion. On the inertial side, motivated by the observation that integration error accumulates sharply at the early stage and grows slowly afterward, we design a learned IMU model with a learnable initial covariance, and propose a dedicated fine-tuning strategy on a held-out training subset to enable the metrics-aware covariance on unseen sequences. As a showcase, we build a VI initialization and calibration system, since accurate and robust initialization and calibration are the prerequisite for any reliable VI system. Experiments on EuRoC, and VBR show that MAC-I$^2$ substantially outperforms existing methods: it achieves a 99.9% initialization success rate on EuRoC, reducing gravity and velocity errors by about 60% and 42% over the strongest baseline, and maintains 80% success rate on challenging VBR sequences where baseline methods such as VINS-Mono drop below 10%.

</details>


### [55] [Beyond Task Success: Stage-Wise Reliability of World Model Planning under Sensing Degradation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07126)
*Geonmyeong Lee,Byoung-Tak Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究对世界模型规划器施加10种视觉和时间感知退化，在50个相同任务上追踪其影响在表征、未来预测、规划器偏好和物理结果各阶段的变化。发现退化影响的相对大小在各阶段不一致，时间退化模式亦因损坏信息位置和规划器实际暴露程度而不同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型规划中感知输入需经过编码器和预测器才能影响规划决策，仅依赖最终任务成功无法揭示感知干扰在管道中何处衰减或持续存在，因此需要分阶段诊断。

Method: 在相同50个任务上对世界模型规划器应用10种视觉和时间感知退化，采用配对评估方式，跟踪退化在表征、未来预测、规划器偏好和物理结果四个阶段的效应；并在另一操纵任务和不同世界模型上进行二次验证。

Result: 退化影响的相对大小未在阶段间保持一致：大的表征偏移可能在下游衰减，而小的初始偏移可能持续到结果；内部响应排序与物理结果排序不直接匹配。时间退化即使在观测历史整体变化相似时，也会因损坏信息位置和规划器实际暴露程度而显著不同，该非均匀阶段响应在二次评估中也得到验证。

Conclusion: 阶段式诊断能够识别感知扰动在管道中何处衰减或持续，有助于优先安排后续模型验证和感知缓解措施。

Abstract: In world model planning, sensing inputs pass through an encoder and predictor before affecting planner decisions, so final task success alone cannot reveal where sensing disturbances attenuate or persist in the pipeline. We apply 10 visual and temporal sensing degradations to a world model planner and track their effects across representation, future prediction, planner preference, and physical outcome using paired evaluation on the same 50 tasks. The relative impact of degradations was not preserved across stages: large representation shifts could attenuate downstream, while smaller initial shifts could persist to the outcome, and internal-response ordering did not directly match physical-outcome ordering. Temporal degradations also showed distinct patterns: even with similar overall changes in observation history, responses differed substantially with the location of corrupted information and the planner's actual exposure. This non-uniform stage-wise response was also observed in secondary evaluations with another manipulation task and a different world model. Stage-wise diagnosis can therefore identify where sensing disturbances attenuate or persist and help prioritize subsequent model verification and sensing mitigation.

</details>


### [56] [EquiGQNet: Fast Grasp Quality Evaluation via Shared Equivariant Point Cloud Encoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07145)
*Sungwon Seo,Jaeseog Won,Jiyou Shin,Youngjin Seo,Hyunjun Kim,Seokmin Yoon,Tuan Luong,Hyungpil Moon*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出EquiGQNet，一种高效的6-DoF抓取质量评估器，通过SO(3)等变编码与中期动作融合机制，兼顾早期融合在抓取相对几何上的优势与后期融合在特征共享上的效率，显著加速规划时间并提升真实场景抓取成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有抓取评估方法存在两难：早期融合方法可捕获每个抓取候选的局部物体几何，但需重复编码场景导致效率低下；后期融合方法虽共享场景表征但可能丢失抓取相关的局部几何信息。如何在保持效率的同时兼顾两者优势是待解决的核心问题。

Method: EquiGQNet包含两个关键设计：(1)针对抓取姿态，采用SO(3)等变的"先编码后旋转"方案替代早期融合中需旋转并重编码点云的操作，从共享场景编码中直接获得与抓取对齐的几何特征；(2)针对抓取位置，提出中期动作融合（MAF），在全局聚合前将抓取位置注入中间特征，保留各候选的局部几何信息。

Result: 在仿真中，EquiGQNet抓取性能与早期融合基线相当，在几何复杂、可抓取区域有限的物体上显著优于后期融合；CEM规划时间从3.31s降至0.48s，较早期融合提速6.9倍。在真实家庭物体整理实验中，抓取成功率达95.2%，每小时230次拾取，优于后期融合的153次和早期融合的170次。

Conclusion: EquiGQNet通过SO(3)等变编码和中期动作融合，成功兼顾了早期融合的几何感知能力与后期融合的共享表征效率，在保持高抓取质量的同时大幅降低规划耗时，为单视角深度图像下的杂乱桌面6-DoF抓取提供了高效且实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Planning six-degree-of-freedom (6-DoF) grasps for unseen objects in cluttered tabletop scenes from a single-view depth image requires accurate and efficient evaluation of diverse grasp candidates. Existing early-fusion methods capture local object geometry relative to each grasp candidate but repeatedly encode the scene, whereas late-fusion methods reuse a shared scene representation but may lose this grasp-relative local geometry. We propose EquiGQNet, an efficient 6-DoF grasp quality evaluator that combines the strengths of both approaches. For grasp orientation, EquiGQNet replaces the early-fusion operation of rotating and re-encoding the point cloud for each grasp candidate with an SO(3)-equivariant encode-once-then-rotate scheme, yielding grasp-aligned geometric features from a shared scene encoding. For grasp translation, Mid-level Action Fusion (MAF) injects the grasp position into intermediate features before global aggregation, retaining local geometry relative to each candidate. We evaluate EquiGQNet in two grasp planning pipelines: Cross-Entropy Method (CEM)-based continuous grasp search and candidate ranking with a pretrained generative planner. In simulation, EquiGQNet achieves grasping performance comparable to the early-fusion baseline and substantially outperforms late fusion on objects with complex geometry and limited graspable regions, while reducing CEM planning time from 3.31s to 0.48s, a 6.9x speedup over early fusion. In real-world household-object decluttering, EquiGQNet achieves a 95.2% grasp success rate and 230 picks per hour, versus 153 and 170 for early- and late-fusion baselines. Code is available at https://equigqnet.github.io/.

</details>


### [57] [State-of-the-Art in Learning-by-Demonstration with Passive Observation for Industrial Assembly Automation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07165)
*David Koetter,Oliver Petrovic,Christian Brecher*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文对工业装配中的被动学习示范（LbD）进行了系统性文献综述，重点关注在单次示范条件下机器人的感知架构与示范泛化能力，识别出向物体中心感知的转变趋势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多品种、小批量的敏捷制造环境中，LbD是实现直观机器人编程的关键技术，但目前缺乏对工业装配中被动LbD系统（尤其是单次示范方法）的系统性综述来指导技术选择与发展方向。

Method: 采用系统性文献综述方法，聚焦被动LbD在工业装配中的应用，从感知架构和示范泛化两个维度进行分析，特别关注仅需单次示范的一击（one-shot）方法。

Result: 研究发现系统向物体中心感知（object-centric perception）方向转变，这一转变使学习到的操作基元能够以最少的训练量迁移到新的产品变体中。

Conclusion: 物体中心感知是当前LbD系统适应新产品变体的关键趋势，有助于在有限数据条件下实现泛化，支持高混合低批量制造场景下的机器人灵活性。

Abstract: Learning-by-Demonstration (LbD) enables intuitive robot programming by capturing expert skills, which is crucial for agility in high-mix, low- volume manufacturing. This systematic literature review analyzes passive LbD for industrial assembly processes, focusing on the perception architecture and the generalization of the perceived demonstration. We specifically investigate one-shot approaches where only a single demonstration is required. The review evaluates how systems adapt to new assemblies using this limited data. We identify a shift towards object-centric perception, allowing learned primitives to be transferred to new product variants with minimal training.

</details>


### [58] [Phase-and-First-Arrival VLM Feedback for Sparse-Reward Reinforcement Learning in Surgical Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07211)
*Wanli Liuchen,Fangyuan Wang,Bin Li,Anqing Duan,Yunhui Liu,Peng Zhou,David Navarro-Alarcon*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为"阶段与首次到达反馈"（phase-and-first-arrival feedback）的方法，通过VLM对每个录制片段进行单次查询，识别任务中视觉可验证的最远阶段及其首次到达时间，从而在稀疏奖励强化学习中实现部分行为重用和时间信用定位。作者在SurgPhaseBench基准上验证了该方法，在五个模拟任务中平均成功率达到75.2%，优于基于CLIP奖励的52.1%，并在硬件上验证了其支持自主物块拾取和滑动恢复的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏结果反馈限制了机器人从复杂操作失败尝试中学习的能力。多阶段手术失败尝试中可能包含值得复用的抓取、抬起或转移等行为，但稀疏奖励强化学习将此类尝试坍塌为相同结果，标量VLM评分也无法揭示哪些进展应获得信用及何时获得，因此需要更细粒度的反馈机制。

Method: 提出阶段与首次到达反馈机制：对每个记录的episode执行一次VLM查询，以识别视觉上已验证的最远任务阶段以及该阶段被首次到达的时间点，使学习器能够复用部分行为并定位信用。在此基础上构建了SurgPhaseBench基准套件，涵盖刚体和可变形任务，在仿真与硬件平台上进行评估。

Result: 在五个模拟任务中，该方法达到75.2%的平均成功率，而使用相同视觉输入的基于CLIP的奖励方法仅为52.1%。优势在仅改变反馈表征时依然存在。硬件平台上，该方法支持自主物块拾取和滑动恢复。

Conclusion: 轨迹级别的视觉监督能够在保留部分进展信息的同时，提供稀疏奖励控制所需的时间信用，为解决复杂多阶段操作任务中的学习效率问题提供了有效途径。

Abstract: Sparse outcome feedback limits what robots can learn from unsuccessful attempts at complex manipulation. Failed multi-stage surgical attempts can contain grasps, lifts, or transfers worth reusing. In sparse-reward reinforcement learning, terminal rewards collapse such attempts to the same outcome, while scalar vision-language model (VLM) ratings reveal neither what progress merits credit nor when it occurred. We introduce phase-and-first-arrival feedback: one VLM query per recorded episode identifies the furthest visually verified task phase and when that phase is first reached, allowing the learner to reuse partial behavior and localize credit. We instantiate it in SurgPhaseBench, a phase-structured suite spanning rigid and deformable tasks, and evaluate it in simulation and hardware. Across five simulated tasks, our method reaches 75.2% mean success, compared with 52.1% for a reward based on Contrastive Language-Image Pre-training (CLIP) using the same visual input; the advantage persists when only the feedback representation changes. On hardware, the same record supports autonomous block picking and slip recovery. Together, these results show that trajectory-level visual supervision can preserve partial progress while providing the temporal credit needed for sparse-reward control.

</details>


### [59] [Generalizable 6D Pose Estimation of Textureless Objects with Planar-based Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07231)
*Jie Lu,Hengtan Zhang,Li Gong,Pengpeng Wang,Xianjia Yu,Jinxiang Deng,Tomi Westerlund,Zhongxue Gan,Lirong Zheng,Zhuo Zou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出PG-Pose框架，利用平面高斯溅射重建和几何驱动优化，实现对无CAD模型的无纹理物体6D姿态估计，在OnePose-LowTexture数据集上取得94.2%的ADD(S)@0.1d精度，相比SOTA方法提升2.1%，并成功部署于双轴工业机器人完成抓取任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无纹理物体缺乏外观特征且没有先验CAD模型时，6D姿态估计仍面临重大挑战。现有通用方法在低纹理物体上性能受限，主要因为底层表示中几何约束不足。

Method: 提出PG-Pose框架，包含两个阶段：(1)离线表示提取阶段，从已知姿态的多视角参考RGB图像中提取三种表示，利用平面高斯溅射(PGS)重建3D高斯表示并渲染高保真深度图进行反投影生成3D点云；(2)在线姿态推理阶段，通过2D-3D对应匹配估计初始姿态，再经PGS-Refiner进行迭代姿态优化。

Result: 在OnePose-LowTexture数据集上平均精度达到94.2% ADD(S)@0.1d，相比基于GS的SOTA方法平均精度提升2.1%。此外，成功部署于双轴工业机器人，实现了对未见物体的抓取任务。

Conclusion: PG-Pose通过平面高斯溅射重建与几何驱动姿态优化的结合，有效解决了无纹理物体在无CAD模型条件下的6D姿态估计难题，并为工业机器人抓取场景提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Estimating the 6D pose of textureless objects without prior CAD models remains a critical challenge due to the lack of appearance features. While recent generalizable approaches alleviate the dependence on object-specific models, their performance on low-texture objects is often limited by insufficient geometric constraints in the underlying representations. In this work, we propose PG-Pose, a geometry-aware framework combining Planar-based Gaussian Splatting (PGS) reconstruction and Geometry-driven pose optimization. In the offline representation extraction stage, three distinct representations of the object are extracted from multi-view reference RGB images with known poses. PG-Pose reconstructs a 3D Gaussian representation and renders high-fidelity depth maps to generate 3D point clouds through back projection. In the online pose inference stage, the initial pose of the input image is estimated by 2D-3D correspondence matching between the input image and the reconstructed 3D point clouds, followed by a PGS-Refiner for iterative pose optimization. Evaluations on the OnePose-LowTexture datasets, PG-Pose achieves an average accuracy of 94.2% ADD(S)@0.1d, with a 2.1% improvement average accuracy compared with the state-of-the-art (SOTA) GS-based approach. To further demonstrate the effectiveness of PG-Pose for industrial robots in grasping tasks, we deploy it on a dual-arm industrial robot and successfully realize the grasping task on an unseen object.

</details>


### [60] [Singularity-Free Guiding Vector Fields on SO(3) with Designer-Specified Progression Behavior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07269)
*Jesus Bautista,Hector Garcia de Marina*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在特殊正交群SO(3)上的无奇异性引导向量场(SF-GVF)，用于姿态路径跟随。该方法将欧几里得空间的SF-GVF构造提升到SO(3)上，结合增广状态法与李群几何，得到闭式几何引导律。该场定义于SO(3)的稠密开子集上，仅排除测度为零的对径集。同时将沿路径的推进行为形式化为设计者指定的函数，且在SO(3)上ω=0的条件通常是物理可接受的，这使得推进行为成为SO(3)上结构性的设计自由度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有引导向量场方法多局限于欧几里得空间，缺乏在SO(3)上处理姿态路径跟随的框架。此外，欧几里得设置中速度v=0的条件排除了具有最小速度约束的载体，需要发展一个能处理SO(3)上的姿态路径跟随且无奇异性的引导框架。

Method: 该论文的方法包括：(1)将欧几里得SF-GVF构造通过增广状态法与李群内在几何相结合，提升到SO(3)上，获得闭式几何引导律；(2)该场定义在SO(3)的稠密开子集上，仅排除测度为零的对径集，反映了SO(3)上连续全局稳定的拓扑障碍；(3)无需逐步优化，直接产生so(3)中的控制输入作为机体角速度；(4)将推进行为形式化为设计者指定的函数ν(ξ)，在双不变黎曼度量下建立结构结果。

Result: 该框架在自交路径上的仿真中得到验证，涵盖恒定推进行为和点收敛推进行为两种情形。结构结果在双不变黎曼度量下建立，对路径、推进和李雅普诺夫增益的选择具有一致性。

Conclusion: 该框架揭示了SO(3)上推进行为作为设计自由度的独特结构优势——这是欧几里得设置中不具备的。与欧几里得情形中v=0排除最小速度约束载体不同，SO(3)上的ω=0条件对大多数具有主动姿态控制的平台在物理上是可接受的，从而在姿态路径跟随中提供了更大的设计灵活性。

Abstract: This paper develops a singularity-free guiding vector field (SF-GVF) for path following on the special orthogonal group SO(3). First, we lift the Euclidean SF-GVF construction to SO(3), integrating the augmented-state approach with the intrinsic Lie-group geometry and obtaining a closed-form geometric guidance law whose integral curves converge to a designer-specified attitude path. The field is defined on a dense open subset of SO(3), excluding only the measure-zero antipodal set - a manifestation of the topological obstruction to continuous global stabilization on SO(3). The construction requires no per-step optimization and produces a control input intrinsically in so(3) as body angular rates. Second, we formalize the progression behavior along the path as a designer-supplied function ν(ξ), promoting the parametric speed from an implicitly resolved degree of freedom to a first-class design specification. In contrast to the Euclidean condition v = 0, which excludes vehicles with minimum-speed constraints, the corresponding condition ω= 0 on SO(3) is physically admissible for most platforms with active attitude control, making the progression behavior a design freedom structurally available on SO(3) but absent in the Euclidean setting. The framework's structural results are established under a bi-invariant Riemannian metric and hold uniformly across choices of path, progression, and Lyapunov gain. The framework is illustrated in simulation on self-intersecting paths under both constant and point-convergence progression behaviors.

</details>


### [61] [LightSplat: Real-Time High-Fidelity 3D Gaussian SLAM with Loop Closure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07274)
*Junze Bao,Ye Gao,Yiming Huang,Xiaolong Yu,Chen Dong,Qing Gao,Wei Wang,Jinhu Lü*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出LightSplat，一种基于混合表征的RGB-D SLAM框架，结合局部稀疏特征用于快速鲁棒跟踪与双线程后端渐进构建稠密高斯子图，并通过特征加速的3DGS配准实现在线回环检测与位姿图优化，在多个数据集和真实机器人平台上实现了接近最先进的建图质量和适配实际相机运动的能力，平均帧率达到8 FPS，为高保真3DGS在真实环境中的部署提供了高效鲁棒的基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于3DGS的SLAM系统虽然展现了稠密三维场景重建的潜力，但在实际部署中面临运行性能和地图适应性方面的严重不足，无法满足真实世界应用的严格要求。

Method: LightSplat采用混合表征的RGB-D SLAM框架：1) 利用局部稀疏特征实现快速鲁棒的跟踪；2) 采用双线程后端逐步构建稠密高斯子图；3) 通过特征加速的3DGS配准实现在线回环检测；4) 通过位姿图优化提升全局地图一致性；5) 支持在线高保真高斯地图重建。

Result: 在多个数据集和真实机器人平台上进行的大量实验表明，该方法实现了接近最先进的建图质量，能够适应实际相机运动，并保持平均8 FPS的运行帧率。

Conclusion: LightSplat为高保真3DGS在真实环境中的应用提供了高效且鲁棒的部署基础，兼顾了重建精度与运行效率。

Abstract: SLAM systems based on 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) have recently demonstrated promising reconstruction accuracy for dense 3D scene representations. However, current 3DGS systems struggle to meet the strict demands of real-world deployments due to severe limitations in operational performance and map adaptability. To this end, we propose LightSplat, a hybrid-representation RGB-D SLAM framework. It synergizes local sparse features for robust and fast tracking with a dual-thread backend that progressively constructs dense Gaussian submaps. Crucially, we enable online loop closure through feature-accelerated 3DGS registration, refining overall map consistency through pose graph optimization. Ultimately, LightSplat achieves the online reconstruction of high-fidelity Gaussian map. Extensive experiments on multiple datasets and real-world robotic platform demonstrate that our method achieves near state-of-the-art reconstruction quality and the capability to accommodate practical camera motions, maintaining an average framerate of 8 FPS. Overall, LightSplat provides an efficient and robust foundation for deploying high-fidelity 3DGS in real-world environments.

</details>


### [62] [How Long Until Your Robot Ignores You? A Safety Benchmark for LLM Orchestrators in Human-Humanoid Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07288)
*Aulon Bajrami,Mohamed Elshamouty,Werner Kraus*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文首次提出面向人机协作场景下LLM编排器的安全基准测试环境，基于MCP架构和ISO 10218-2:2025安全规范，定义了5个可测试的安全不变量和四级合规分类法，并通过三层评估管线（文本提示、仿真传感-执行器回路、物理验证）对多个云后端和本地模型进行了系统测试。初步结果显示：模型家族决定安全底线（Claude和Gemini几乎零违规，GPT-4o-mini每会话最多13次违规）、上下文管理效果因模型而异（可减少多数模型的行为问题但反而使GPT-4o-mini违规翻倍）、以及不同模型在合规策略上存在显著差异（Gemini表现出独特的比例合规行为）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM正越来越多地被用于通过自然语言接口编排机器人行为，但目前缺乏评估其在人机协作中作为安全感知决策者可靠性的基准。与确定性安全系统不同，LLM编排器表现出从过度合规到完全违规的合规光谱，因此需要专门的基准环境来衡量其安全性。

Method: 构建了基于Model Context Protocol (MCP)架构的安全基准环境，其安全不变量以ISO 10218-2:2025保护措施为基础。定义了5个安全不变量、四级合规分类法（正确合规、过度合规、不足合规、完全违规）以及三层评估管线（文本提示、仿真传感-执行器回路、Unitree G1 EDU物理验证）。在40个100回合会话中对3个云后端（Claude Haiku 4.5、GPT-4o-mini、Gemini 2.5 Flash）和1个本地开放权重基线（qwen3:8b）进行测试，比较了全上下文和滑动窗口预算两种条件。

Result: (1) 模型家族决定安全底线：Claude和Gemini保持接近零违规，而GPT-4o-mini每会话最多13次违规；(2) 上下文管理与失败轴分离，使所有云后端的平均行为问题减少42-57%，但使GPT-4o-mini的违规几乎翻倍（从3.8增至7.2次/会话）；(3) 比例合规行为（将运动速度限制在规则规定的最大值而非拒绝）仅在Gemini中一致出现。初步仿真层复现了模型排名和GPT-4o-mini的失败模式反转现象。仿真和物理层仍在进行中。

Conclusion: LLM编排器在人机协作中的安全性高度依赖于模型选择，不同模型安全底线差异显著；上下文管理策略对安全的影响具有模型依赖性，并非所有模型都能从上下文优化中获益；物理/仿真验证层对结论的推广至关重要，当前层1结果显示了初步但重要的安全差异格局。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly employed to orchestrate robot behavior through natural-language interfaces, yet no benchmark exists to evaluate their reliability as safety-aware decision makers in human-humanoid collaboration. Unlike deterministic safety systems that enforce binary allow/deny decisions, LLM-based orchestrators exhibit a compliance spectrum ranging from overcompliance (refusing safe actions) to full safety violations. This paper introduces the first safety benchmarking environment for LLM orchestrators in human-humanoid collaboration, built on a Model Context Protocol (MCP)-based architecture with safety invariants grounded in ISO 10218-2:2025 protective measures. The benchmark defines five testable safety invariants, a four-level compliance taxonomy (correct compliance, overcompliance, undercompliance, full violation), and a three-layer evaluation pipeline (text prompting, simulated sensor-actuator loops, and physical validation on a Unitree G1 EDU humanoid). We report Layer-1 results: three cloud backends (Claude Haiku 4.5, GPT-4o-mini, Gemini 2.5 Flash) and a local open-weights baseline (qwen3:8b) across 40 100-turn sessions under full-context and sliding-window budget conditions, while the simulation and physical layers remain ongoing. We find that (1) model family determines the safety floor, as Claude and Gemini remain at or near zero violations while GPT-4o-mini commits up to 13 per session, (2) context management dissociates two failure axes, reducing mean behavioral issues by 42-57% for every cloud backend while nearly doubling GPT-4o-mini's violations (3.8 to 7.2 per session), and (3) proportional compliance, clamping movement speed to the rule-specified maximum rather than refusing, emerges consistently only in Gemini; the preliminary simulation layer reproduces the model ranking and the GPT-4o-mini failure-mode inversion.

</details>


### [63] [PV-WM: A Heterogeneous Micro-Macro World Model for Articulated Pedestrian-Vehicle Co-Rollout](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07328)
*Haozhuang Chi,Jingsong Liang,Ziying Song,Lei Yang,Shihao Li,Haoruo Zhang,Chen Lv*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出PV-WM，一个基于历史观测的局部行人-车辆联合预测世界模型。它在统一异构状态中循环推进行人根部运动和15关节姿态以及车辆运动状态，相比单次预测器和模块化专家系统，在各项误差指标上均有显著改进，同时参数更少、计算开销更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 局部行人-车辆预测涉及异质物理尺度：行人兼具根部移动和关节运动，而车辆是带方位和边界的刚体。现有路况智能体预测器通常忽略行人姿态，姿态预测器又无法覆盖车辆未来状态，缺乏一个能统一处理这类异构状态的预测框架。

Method: PV-WM采用循环世界模型架构，在同步的异构状态表示中，循环地推进三类信息：行人根部运动、15关节关节姿态（articulation）以及学习的车辆状态。生成的行人块和车辆块作为下一个循环边界的输入；车辆边界框由预测的中心和航向角结合观测到的尺度重建；每次状态转移后重新计算行人与车辆的几何关系。

Result: 相比单次完整状态预测器，循环执行使Root ADE降低12.7%、MPJPE降低14.8%。在824个对齐Waymo场景中（其中797个有有效车辆未来支持），相对于验证集精选的模块化专家系统，Root ADE降低5.2%、MPJPE降低7.6%、行人-车辆距离误差降低11.9%、有向框最近接近误差降低5.8%。单网络模型参数减少57.1%，平均FLOPs降低96.5%，实测p95延迟降低25.5%。

Conclusion: PV-WM在统一模型中实现了异构未来状态（行人姿态+车辆状态）的联合预测，同时保持类型特定的物理动力学特性，验证了循环世界模型结构在此类跨尺度任务中的有效性与效率优势。

Abstract: Local pedestrian-vehicle forecasting spans heterogeneous physical scales: pedestrians combine root locomotion with articulated motion, whereas vehicles are rigid bodies described by kinematic state and oriented extent. Existing road-agent forecasters typically omit pedestrian articulation, while pose forecasters leave vehicle futures outside the learned rollout. We introduce PV-WM, a history-only world model over structured post-perception tracks. It recurrently advances pedestrian root motion, 15-joint articulation, and learned vehicle states within a synchronized heterogeneous state. The generated pedestrian and vehicle chunks supply the next recurrent boundary; vehicle boxes are reconstructed from predicted center and heading with observed extent, and P-V geometry is recomputed after every transition. Relative to a matched one-shot complete-state predictor, recurrent execution reduces Root ADE by 12.7% and MPJPE by 14.8%. Feedback interventions show that later predictions depend on the content, temporal order, and pedestrian identity of generated articulation. Across 824 aligned Waymo contexts, with 797 providing valid future vehicle support, PV-WM reduces Root ADE by 5.2%, MPJPE by 7.6%, P-V distance error by 11.9%, and oriented-box closest-approach error by 5.8% relative to a validation-selected Modular Specialist. The single-network model uses 57.1% fewer parameters, 96.5% lower average FLOPs per local scene, and 25.5% lower measured p95 latency. PV-WM unifies this heterogeneous future state while preserving type-specific pedestrian and vehicle dynamics.

</details>


### [64] [D3ARC: Time-Critical Distributed Disaster Detection for Asynchronous Cooperative Multi-Robot Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07350)
*Nikolaos Koursioumpas,Lina Magoula,Nancy Alonistioti,Ramin Khalili*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出D3ARC，一个异步分布式分层框架，用于时效性和可靠性强的野火检测。该框架整合多个机器人代理，在不确定性下通过分布式感知、共享态势感知和协调行动协同工作，在仿真中实现了高达94%的任务成功率与89.4%的检测置信度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 气候变化加剧了自然灾害的严重性和不可预测性。传统野火监测方法受覆盖范围、成本和人员风险限制，亟需自主、自适应的监测解决方案。

Method: D3ARC采用异步分布式分层架构：远程控制器异步决定每个机器人的运动，各机器人代理负责环境感知和野火检测的执行决策。框架整合了安全导航、覆盖效率、协作与可靠性机制，并引入前瞻性能力，使代理能在执行前评估候选策略以预测未来。

Result: 通过真实机器人仿真、消融研究和基线对比进行评估，整体任务成功率高达94%，检测置信度为89.4%。

Conclusion: D3ARC框架展示了多机器人协作在时间紧迫的野火检测场景中的有效性，通过异步分层协调和前瞻性策略评估，显著提升了任务成功率与检测可靠性。

Abstract: Climate change is increasing the severity and unpredictability of natural disasters. In time-critical crises such as wildfires, traditional monitoring practices remain limited by coverage, cost, and personnel risk, paving the way for autonomous and adaptive monitoring solutions. Within this context, this paper introduces D3ARC, an asynchronous distributed hierarchical framework for time-aware and reliable wildfire detection. D3ARC integrates multiple robotic agents that cooperate under uncertainty through distributed perception, shared situational awareness and coordinated actions. A remote controller asynchronously decides upon each robot's motion, while each robotic agent senses the environment and decides where and how to execute the wildfire detection. All robotic operations require time, and as time progresses, wildfires continue to spread, reducing the opportunity for early intervention. As such, all agents share a common objective: to detect a wildfire with a certain performance threshold as fast as possible and within a time limit. D3ARC integrates mechanisms for safe navigation, coverage efficiency, cooperation and reliability. It introduces a forward-looking capability that allows agents to anticipate the future by evaluating candidate strategies before execution. The framework is evaluated through realistic robotics simulations, ablation studies, and baseline comparisons, achieving an overall mission success up to 94% with 89.4% detection confidence.

</details>


### [65] [OpenWAM: An Open, Modular Exploration Towards Systematic World-Action Model Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07398)
*Yuran Wang,Siqiao Huang,Mingleyang Li,Chenhao Zhang,Jiaqi Liang,Weiyang Jin,Yue Chen,Xuemin Chi,Donghao Zhou,Qize Yu,Yu-Kai Wang,Yuhan Rui,Shenzhe Yao,Zhen Yuan,Zhenhao Shen,Kefei Zhu,Zijie Zhu,Ning Gao,Xiaowei Chi,Guanqi He,Shanghang Zhang,Hao Dong,Lin Shao,Hang Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OpenWAM是一个开放研究栈，将世界-动作预训练转化为可控实验程序，通过分解设计空间、控制实验提炼三个原则，最终构建OpenWAM-α在仿真和真实世界基准上取得卓越表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界-动作模型系统是单体式的，生成骨干、视觉表征、架构、信息流、推理过程和训练数据紧密耦合，导致难以确定哪些设计选择重要及其原因。作者希望解决这一可解释性和可控性问题，将世界-动作预训练转化为可控的实验程序。

Method: 提出OpenWAM-Infra将WAM设计空间分解为可组合模块，统一训练、推理、部署和评估；在此基础上通过OpenWAM-Study进行三方面受控实验（继承什么、世界与动作学习的交互、协同如何扩展），提炼三条设计原则；将这些原则组合构建OpenWAM-α模型，使用约6400小时的自我中心人类和机器人数据进行预训练。

Result: 在八个仿真基准和真实机器人实验中（涵盖单臂、双臂操作和灵巧手等多种形态），OpenWAM-α表现出持续优秀的性能，从仿真到真实世界均保持顶尖水平。

Conclusion: 上游知识通过足够强的生成骨干和紧凑的信息丰富潜空间传递；世界-动作协同需要专门的动作容量、显式的世界到动作信息流和同步联合去噪；具身预训练主要提升域外泛化能力，单阶段协同训练自我中心和机器人数据可整合世界覆盖与动作接地。

Abstract: World-Action Models inherit world knowledge from video-generative priors, and channel it into executable control signals through embodied experience. Existing systems, however, are monolithic: the generative backbone, visual representation, architecture, information flow, inference procedure, and training data are tightly coupled, obscuring which design choices matter and why. We introduce OpenWAM, an open research stack that turns world-action pretraining into a controlled experimental program. OpenWAM-Infra factorizes the WAM design space into composable modules with unified training, inference, deployment, and evaluation. On this substrate, OpenWAM-Study examines three questions through controlled experiments: what to inherit, how world and action learning interact, and how their synergy scales; and distills three principles: upstream knowledge transfers through a sufficiently capable generative backbone and a compact, information-rich latent space; world-action synergy requires dedicated action capacity, explicit world-to-action information flow, and synchronized joint denoising; and embodied pretraining principally improves out-of-domain generalization, with one-stage co-training over egocentric and robot data integrating world coverage and action grounding. Composing these principles, we build OpenWAM-α, an open WAM pretrained on roughly 6,400 hours of egocentric human and robot data and evaluated across simulation and real-world benchmarks. Across the eight simulation benchmarks and the real-robot experiments, which together span embodiments from single-arm and bimanual manipulation to dexterous hands, OpenWAM-α delivers consistently excellent performance, sustaining its top-tier standing from simulation to the physical world. We release the full stack, including infrastructure, evaluation protocols, pretrained models, and data recipes, to facilitate future research.

</details>


### [66] [CALM: Configuration-Aware Human Intervention Boundaries During Robot Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07430)
*Xinting Gao,Sipu Zhu,Weimin Zhuang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本论文研究了机器人身体构型如何影响人类在机器人接近过程中的干预行为。通过41名参与者的组内实验，测量了四种人形手臂构型和两种空间尺度下的停止距离、主观舒适度和眼动反应。结果显示，全臂前伸使停止距离增加约31-36厘米；空间尺度主要影响舒适度和瞳孔反应。作者提出构型感知极限模型（CALM），将停止距离分布转化为依赖于构型的人群覆盖边界，并在路径规划分析中证明重配置可使原本无法实现的接近目标成为可能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究尚未充分探索机器人身体构型如何影响人类在机器人接近过程中的干预行为，这影响了人机交互安全性和舒适性的理解与建模。

Method: 采用组内设计，招募41名参与者，在四种人形手臂构型和两种空间尺度下测量最终停止距离、主观舒适度及探索性眼动反应；提出构型感知极限模型（CALM），将停止距离分布转换为依赖于构型的人群覆盖边界，并在一维路径规划分析中进行了验证。

Result: 全臂前伸相比手臂下垂使停止距离增加约31-36厘米；空间尺度主要影响舒适度和瞳孔反应但未检测到停止距离的显著变化。CALM估计的80%覆盖率边界范围为0.88至1.47米。在相同名义点状20%干预概率约束下，重配置使1.10米接近目标从不可达变为可达。

Conclusion: 身体构型应被视为路径规划中的一个变量，同时需要区分物理安全、行为干预和主观成本三个不同维度来进行综合考量。

Abstract: How robot body configuration shapes human intervention during approach remains underexplored. We conducted a within-participants study with 41 participants, measuring final stopping distance, subjective comfort, and exploratory eye-tracking responses across four humanoid arm configurations and two spatial scales. Full forward arm extension increased stopping distance by approximately 31-36 cm relative to arms-down. Spatial scale primarily affected comfort and pupil responses without a detectable stopping-distance shift. We introduce the Configuration-Aware Limit Model (CALM), which translates stopping-distance distributions into configuration-dependent population-coverage boundaries. Estimated boundaries at 80% coverage ranged from 0.88 to 1.47 m. In an illustrative one-dimensional planning analysis, reconfiguration enabled a 1.10 m approach goal that was unreachable with arms remaining fully extended under the same nominal pointwise 20% intervention-probability constraint. These findings support treating body configuration as a planning variable while distinguishing physical safety, behavioral intervention, and subjective cost.

</details>


### [67] [Open-Set Ego-Noise Separation for Legged-Robot Audition via Annotation-Free Adaptation and Pretrained-Model Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07440)
*Koki Shoda,Jun Younes Louhi Kasahara,Aoba Koyanagi,Qi An,Atsushi Yamashita*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个用于腿式机器人听觉的开集自我噪声分离框架，通过无标注适应和预训练模型迁移，无需人工标注或单独录制噪声数据即可去除机器人自身噪声并保留环境声。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 腿式机器人行走会产生脚步冲击、关节间隙咔嗒声和电机噪声等自我噪声，严重污染录音信号，影响机器人利用声学感知获取环境信息的能力。

Method: 框架包含两部分：1) RecurGraph通过嵌入质心聚合和图传播从未标注录音中自动选择自我噪声主导片段，并将其与大规模声音事件数据集中的多样环境声混合，生成配对监督数据；2) Transfer-DiT将通用零样本神经分离器迁移适配到目标机器人上，实现开集自我噪声分离。

Result: 在双足和四足机器人上的实验表明，该方法在片段选择可靠性、分离质量和下游任务性能上均优于基线分离器，验证了无需单独录制噪声或人工标注即可实现无标注适应的可行性。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了腿式机器人开集自我噪声分离可以通过注释自由适应和预训练模型迁移来实现，为机器人声学感知在真实场景中的应用提供了可行路径。

Abstract: This paper proposes an open-set ego-noise separation framework for legged-robot audition via annotation-free adaptation and pretrained-model transfer. The framework removes robot-specific ego-noise while preserving environmental sounds whose classes are not specified in advance. Acoustic sensing provides cues about a robot's surroundings beyond the visual field, but walking-induced ego-noise from footstep impacts, joint-backlash rattling, and motor noise severely contaminates the recordings. The framework first uses RecurGraph to select ego-noise-dominant clips from the unlabeled recordings by aggregating clip embeddings into an embedding centroid and propagating scores over an audio-embedding graph. The selected clips are mixed with diverse environmental sounds from a large-scale sound-event dataset to provide paired mixture--target supervision for open-set separation. Transfer-DiT then adapts a general-purpose zero-shot neural separator to achieve high-fidelity open-set ego-noise separation for the target robot. Experiments with bipedal and quadrupedal robots show reliable clip selection and improvements in separation quality and downstream task performance over baseline separators. These results demonstrate the feasibility of annotation-free adaptation without separately recorded ego-noise-only data or manual clip-level annotations.

</details>


### [68] [SMaRT-Tug: Structured Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning for Physics-Based Tugboat-Barge Collaborative Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07445)
*Junkai Lu,Jiadong Zhao,Jiacheng Zhang,Wenqi Zhao,Hao Gen Chia,Qun Shen Png,Germaine Ee,Chengyang He,Yifeng Zhang,Nathanael Tan,Guillaume Sartoretti*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于物理的GPU加速仿真与学习框架，用于多拖船协同操纵大型船只的任务。该框架包含定制浮力模型、波浪建模和水动力阻力，并支持大规模多智能体训练。研究者使用带有结构化控制先验（SCP）的分散式MAPPO策略进行训练，在直线航行、转弯和减速任务上优于PID控制器和集中式PPO基线，并展示了对更恶劣海况、高级机动以及三到四艘拖船团队的零样本泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主拖船在港口物流和船舶操纵中至关重要，但多拖船协同推送因耦合水动力学、低阻力、强环境扰动、欠驱动驳船动力学和接触丰富的交互而具有挑战。传统控制方法依赖简化模型和固定配置，适应性有限；学习方法的瓶颈在于缺乏可扩展且物理真实的训练环境。

Method: 构建一个基于物理的GPU加速仿真与学习框架，集成了定制浮力模型、波浪建模和水动力阻力，支持大规模多智能体训练。在该仿真器中训练结合结构化控制先验（SCP）的分散式MAPPO策略，以提高训练稳定性并维持可行的推送配置。

Result: 在直线航行、转弯和减速任务中评估策略，分散式框架相比PID控制器和集中式PPO基线表现出更可靠和精确的操纵性能。此外，该策略实现了对更恶劣海况和高级机动的零样本泛化，并展示了从双拖船扩展到三至四艘拖船团队的零样本规模扩展能力。

Conclusion: 该研究展示了所提出的物理仿真与强化学习框架在协同拖船操纵中的有效性，其分散式策略不仅优于传统控制方法和集中式基线，还展现出优秀的泛化性和可扩展性，为自主海事操作提供了可行的技术路径。

Abstract: Autonomous tugboating is central for automating maritime operations such as port logistics and vessel maneuvering, where multiple tugboats must cooperatively transport/manipulate a larger vessel. Collaborative pushing in this setting is challenging due to coupled hydrodynamics, low resistance, strong environmental disturbances, underactuated barge dynamics, and contact-rich interactions. Conventional control methods often rely on simplified models and fixed configurations, which limit their adaptability, while learning-based approaches are constrained by the lack of scalable and physically realistic training environments. We address these challenges by introducing a physics-based, GPU-accelerated simulation and learning framework for collaborative tugboat manipulation. Our simulator incorporates a customized buoyancy model, wave modeling, and hydrodynamic resistance, and supports large-scale multi-agent training under marine dynamics. In this simulator, we train a decentralized MAPPO (Multi-Agent PPO) policy augmented with a structured control prior (SCP) to improve training stability and maintain feasible pushing configurations. We evaluate our learned policy on straight-line transit, turning, and deceleration tasks, where we show that our decentralized framework yields more reliable and accurate maneuvering performance compared to a PID-based controller and a centralized PPO baseline. We further demonstrate zero-shot generalization to more challenging sea states and advanced maneuvers, as well as zero-shot scalability to larger teams of three and four tugboats despite training with only two agents.

</details>


### [69] [Measuring Language Transfer in Robot Policies: Adding Greek to a Cosmos3 Vision-Language-Action Policy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07470)
*Ayoub Kirouane,Georgios Giaples,Christos Petrocheilos*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文研究将希腊语添加到开源视觉-语言-动作机器人基础模型堆栈中，仅使用机器改写的指令且不改变架构。核心发现是度量问题比翻译更具挑战性：多项看似合理的评估工具会产生虚假结论。双语训练比单语希腊语训练更优，能持续超越对照基线6.7-7.1分，达到英语性能的约五分之二。论文提出两个实际要求：先建立保证的空值基线再信任度量，以及跨种子复制低资源语言实验结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人基础模型主要在英语环境下训练和评估，大多数语言缺乏机器人演示语料库，因此需要探索在低资源语言（如希腊语）下进行机器人策略本地化的可行方法及可靠评估手段。

Method: 在开源视觉-语言-动作堆栈中添加希腊语支持，仅使用机器改写的指令，不改变架构。设计了一个具有三个种子的九十任务判别性套件进行系统评估，比较多语言文本塔、仅希腊语训练和双语训练等多种配置，并对比多种评估度量的可靠性。

Result: 多语言文本塔在无希腊演示时仍停留在错误指令基线；仅希腊语训练最多超出对照2.7分；双语训练持续超出对照6.7-7.1分，达到英语性能约五分之二。策略会过拟合翻译者的措辞，七种措辞训练可将此惩罚减半。从语言适配的世界模型热启动和解冻文本塔均会降低性能。

Conclusion: 低资源语言机器人策略本地化需要两个实际要求：在信任任何度量前先构建可保证的空值基线，以及跨种子复制低资源语言实验结果。

Abstract: Robot foundation models are trained and evaluated predominantly in English, and robot demonstration corpora do not exist for most languages. We study the addition of Greek to an open vision-language-action stack using only machine-rephrased instructions and no architecture changes. The main challenge is measurement rather than translation.
