Several plausible instruments produce false conclusions: a color-histogram metric rewards noise, a single-goal benchmark scores 84.6% under correct Greek and 82.6% under deliberately wrong instructions, training loss fails to predict Greek success, and single-run comparisons are dominated by seed variation. On a discriminative ninety-task suite with three seeds per arm, a multilingual text tower without Greek demonstrations remains at its wrong-instruction floor, while Greek-only training exceeds its control by at most 2.7 points. Bilingual training yields a consistent 6.7-7.1 point margin over its control and reaches about two fifths of English performance. The policy also overfits the translator's phrasing; training on seven phrasings per task approximately halves this penalty. Warm-starting from a language-adapted world model and unfreezing the text tower both degrade performance. The results support two practical requirements for low-resource robot-policy localization: build a guaranteed null before trusting a metric, and replicate low-resource-language results across seeds.

</details>


### [70] [Wearable Multimodal Human-Machine Interface for Integrated Hand Intentions Decoding in Dynamic Teleoperation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07495)
*Jiaxuan Li,Yinshi Wu,Xiao Zhang,Hongyu Wang,Renzhen Le,Zhenzhi Ying,Liming Shu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为MI-DHMI的多意图解码人机接口，将高通量sEMG传感器与手部和前臂安装的IMU相结合，在不受约束的上肢运动条件下实现手势识别、抓握力估计和手部姿态解码的多意图同步解码，手势识别准确率超过97%，抓握力估计R²=0.95，并通过在线实验验证了其在倒水任务和物体抓取等任务中的优越性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在普遍存在的遥操作环境中，光学条件常具有挑战性，需要一个既具备可穿戴性又能精准解码手部多种意图（手部姿态、手势和抓握力）的接口。然而现有接口往往无法同时满足多样意图解码与可穿戴性的需求，在两者之间存在妥协。

Method: 开发了MI-DHMI接口，集成高通量表面肌电（sEMG）传感器与手部/前臂安装的惯性测量单元（IMU），并提出统一的多意图同步解码框架。该框架采用多模态深度学习与低噪声硬件设计，选择性聚焦与手指运动真正相关的sEMG成分，减少无约束上肢运动中sEMG变异性带来的解码误差。

Result: 在无约束腕部和前臂运动条件下，手势识别准确率超过97%，抓握力估计R²=0.95，手部姿态解码与真实手部姿态一致，性能优于基线设备和算法。消融研究验证了解码框架的有效性，两项在线实验（倒水任务和物体抓取）证实了其在高稳定性任务中的优越性能。

Conclusion: 该接口提供了一种全新的全可穿戴多意图解码系统方案，为无处不在的遥操作提供了有效支持，推动了人机交互研究的发展。

Abstract: Under ubiquitous teleoperation environments with optically challenging conditions, an interface for tele-operated grasping that combines wearability with precise decoding of hand intentions (hand pose, gestures, and grasping force) is essential. Yet, existing interfaces often fall short in meeting these demands, compromising either the diversity of multiple intentions decoding or wearability. To address this, we developed a novel Multiple Intentions Decoding Human-Machine Interface (MI-DHMI) that integrates high-throughput surface electromyography (sEMG) sensors with hand-mounted and forearm-mounted inertial measurement units (IMUs). The developed interface is supported by a unified framework for simultaneous multiple intentions decoding. By employing multimodal deep learning and hardware design with a low noise floor, the decoding framework selectively focuses on the sEMG components that are genuinely associated with finger movements. This effectively reduces decoding errors caused by sEMG variability during unconstrained upper-limb motions, thereby significantly enhancing robustness. Even under unconstrained wrist and forearm motion, the interface achieves a gesture recognition accuracy exceeding 97%, grasping force estimation with $R^2 = 0.95$, and hand pose decoding consistent with the actual hand pose, outperforming baseline devices and algorithms. Ablation studies further validate the effectiveness of the proposed decoding framework. Finally, two online experiments were conducted to validate the device, demonstrating its superior performance in high-stability tasks, including a pouring task and object grasping. The developed interface provides a new solution of a fully wearable, multiple intentions decoding system, offering effective support for ubiquitous teleoperation and contributing to the advancement of human-machine interaction research.

</details>


### [71] [Functional-SLAM: Interaction-Aware Mapping with Online Functional Scene Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07497)
*Xinggang Hu,Chenyangguang Zhang,Zihan Zhu,Ruida Zhang,Xiangkui Zhang,Xiangyang Ji*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Functional-SLAM，首个在在线SLAM状态中持续递归维护功能场景图的框架，通过锚点关键帧几何、功能上下文约束、时间关系累积等功能，实现在实时探索中构建稳定的功能地图，同时显著提升运行效率和位姿估计精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SLAM系统缺乏细粒度机器人交互所需的功能关系建模能力；虽然功能3D场景图可以表示物体与交互元素间的关系，但现有方法依赖离线重建，无法满足真实世界探索中的实时交互需求。

Method: 将功能场景图作为在线SLAM状态进行持续递归维护，具体包括：(1) 采用锚点关键帧几何加功能上下文约束进行持久节点维护；(2) 通过时间关系累积多帧证据以提交稳定的功能边；(3) 在重复外观或纹理退化场景中，用功能拓扑补充视觉回环候选。

Result: 实验表明Functional-SLAM能够在线高效构建稳定的功能地图，相比离线方法大幅提升运行时间，同时保持高度竞争性的精度；与同类SLAM系统相比，通过功能拓扑辅助回环闭合进一步提升了位姿估计精度。

Conclusion: Functional-SLAM成功将功能场景图建模引入在线SLAM框架，解决了离线重建在实时交互场景中的局限性，实现了运行效率与精度的双赢，代码已开源。

Abstract: Existing SLAM systems lack modeling of the functional relations required for fine-grained robotic interaction. Functional 3D scene graphs can represent relations between objects and interaction elements, but existing methods rely on offline reconstruction, making them inadequate for real-time interaction in real-world exploration. To address this limitation, we propose Functional-SLAM, the first framework that continuously and recursively maintains a functional scene graph as an online SLAM state. The framework combines anchor-keyframe geometry with functional-context constraints for persistent node maintenance, accumulates multi-frame evidence through temporal relations to commit stable functional edges, and supplements visual loop-closure candidates with functional topology in scenes with repetitive appearance or degraded texture. Experiments show that Functional-SLAM efficiently constructs stable functional maps online, substantially improving runtime over offline methods while maintaining highly competitive accuracy. Compared with peer SLAM systems, it further improves pose estimation accuracy through functional-topology-assisted loop closure. The code is publicly available at https://github.com/Hbelief1998/Functional-SLAM-CoRL_2026.

</details>


### [72] [CosmoH2G: A Hand-to-Gripper Transfer Dataset and Baseline Method for Object Manipulation with Complex Spatial Movements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07498)
*Hongxiang Zhao,Mutian Xu,Zeyu Jin,Yiming Hao,Shuguang Cui,Xiaoguang Han*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种从人手演示到机器人夹爪的跨域迁移框架，通过大规模配对数据采集和两阶段生成策略，解决了复杂空间操作任务（如旋转、翻转等）中的轨迹生成与累积漂移问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的人手到夹爪迁移方法仅适用于简单平面任务，难以处理涉及旋转或翻转等复杂空间运动。同时，简单的端到端全轨迹生成方法在复杂动力学下会因微小偏差累积而导致失败，亟需更鲁棒的方案。

Method: （1）构建可扩展的数据采集流程，基于严谨协议收集人手-夹爪配对演示，形成包含6,189个片段、1,254个物体的大规模配对数据集；（2）提出两阶段框架：第一阶段预测稀疏夹爪关键帧（起始和终止位姿）以简化映射目标，第二阶段在这些关键帧条件下生成完整连续动作序列；（3）学习夹爪姿态的同时，基于抓取启发式和运动学一致性对平移量进行后优化，以缓解累积漂移。

Result: 在仿真和真实机器人实验中，该方法能够实现复杂空间操作任务的稳定、精确人手到夹爪迁移，性能显著优于传统基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过细粒度手部姿态引导的隐式数据驱动方法，结合两阶段关键帧生成与后优化策略，可有效解决复杂空间操作的跨域迁移难题，为机器人学习提供了一条成本效益高的路径。

Abstract: Transferring human hand demonstrations to robotic grippers has recently emerged as a cost-effective solution for robot learning. However, existing methods are largely confined to simple, planar tasks and fail to handle complex spatial movements (e.g., intricate trajectories involving rotations or flips) that are essential for robot manipulation. Motivated by this gap, we adopt an implicit, data-driven approach guided by fine-grained hand-pose motions. To this end, we introduce a scalable acquisition pipeline to collect hand-gripper paired demonstrations, governed by a rigorous protocol that prioritizes motion complexity and leverages a handheld gripper for seamless action mimicry. This yields a large-scale paired dataset comprising 6,189 episodes across 1,254 unique objects, exhibiting significantly higher spatial complexity than existing benchmarks. However, learning such complex mappings remains challenging. We observe that naive end-to-end generation of full gripper pose sequences is insufficient, as minor trajectory deviations compound rapidly under intricate dynamics. To address this, we propose a two-stage framework: Stage I predicts sparse gripper keyframes (initial and terminal) to simplify the mapping objective, while Stage II generates the full continuous action sequence conditioned on these keyframes. Furthermore, to mitigate cumulative drift, we keep the gripper's orientation being learned while post-optimizing its translation based on the grasping heuristic and kinematic consistency. In both simulation and real-robot experiments, our framework enables stable and precise hand-to-gripper transfer of complex spatial manipulations, significantly outperforming traditional baselines. Project page: https://cosmoh2g.github.io.

</details>


### [73] [Generation of Vectorized Maps Beyond Vehicle View](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07511)
*Clara Gomez,Alberto Jaenal,Antonio Artuñedo,Jorge Godoy,Jorge Villagra*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出"超视距矢量化地图生成"这一新问题，旨在基于车载传感器感知范围内的矢量化地图（视内地图），预测生成车辆前方视野之外的地图延续。作者提出首个针对该任务的方法BeyondFormer，并构建了首个专用数据集进行验证，结果表明该方法在多种场景下表现稳定，为自动驾驶地图预测开辟了有前景的方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统高精地图构建成本高昂且更新受限；现有的在线矢量化地图生成方法仅依赖车载传感器，受限于传感器视野范围，无法重建足够远的车辆前方地图，难以满足安全规划需求。

Method: 提出BeyondFormer模型，这是首个面向超视距（beyond-view）矢量化地图生成任务的方法。给定车载传感器感知范围内的矢量化地图（in-view），生成车辆前方视野之外的合理地图延续。同时作者创建了该任务的第一个专用数据集用于评估。

Result: 在多种不同驾驶场景下，BeyondFormer表现出一致的性能，证明了基于学习方法的地图预测在自动驾驶中具有良好前景。实验验证了该任务的可行性。

Conclusion: 本文验证了超视距矢量化地图生成这一任务的可行性，并提供了对方法局限性的深入讨论以及将其扩展到更复杂驾驶条件的关键未来研究方向。

Abstract: Autonomous driving relies on High Definition (HD) maps for safe navigation. Traditional HD maps construction is costly in hardware, data and human resources, which together with its update limitations hinders scalability. Recent works have proposed online alternatives for HD vectorized mapping from onboard sensors. However, sensor field of view is limited, and the range of the reconstructed maps ahead of the vehicle is insufficient for safe planning. This paper aims to address this limitation by proposing the novel beyond-view vectorized map generation problem: given vectorized maps of the area sensed by the vehicle (in-view), to generate plausible map continuations. To experimentally assess its feasibility, we propose BeyondFormer, which, to the best of out knowledge, is the first work designed towards beyond-view map generation. Given the novelty of the problem, we generate the first dataset specifically designed for it and evaluate the proposed approach. The results demonstrate consistent performance across diverse scenarios, establishing learning-based methods as a promising direction for map forecasting in autonomous driving. Beyond demonstrating the feasibility of the task, we provide an extensive discussion of the method's limitations and identify key future research directions for scaling it to more complex driving conditions. Code is available at https://git-autopia.car.upm-csic.es/beyondformer.

</details>


### [74] [P$^2$Calib: Utilizing Pattern Priors for LiDAR-Camera Extrinsic Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07516)
*Xiangcheng Hu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出P²Calib，一种利用目标板CAD模型中的图案先验（已知孔半径和四孔刚性矩形布局）来改进LiDAR-相机外参标定精度的方法。在模拟和真实数据集上，联合配准残差分别降低90%和82%，留出重投影误差分别降低96%和77%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在广泛采用的四孔标定流程中，标定精度受限于LiDAR侧孔中心提取的瓶颈，该步骤受到稀疏角覆盖和混合像素污染的影响，导致标定精度不佳。

Method: 首先，将已知孔半径作为拟合约束，防止在稀疏角覆盖下中心估计退化；其次，利用四孔的刚性矩形布局作为全局一致性约束，纠正跨孔的残差误差。两种先验被集成到一个交互式标定工具中，提供完整的标定流程。

Result: 在模拟数据集上，联合配准残差降低90%，留出重投影误差降低96%；在真实数据集上，联合配准残差降低82%，留出重投影误差降低77%。

Conclusion: 利用目标板CAD模型提供的图案先验可以有效突破LiDAR侧孔中心提取的精度瓶颈，显著提升四孔管线的外参标定精度，且代码和数据集将公开发布以促进后续研究。

Abstract: Target-based LiDAR-camera extrinsic calibration is a prerequisite for multi-sensor fusion in robotics. However, in the widely adopted four-hole pipeline, calibration accuracy is bottlenecked by LiDAR-side hole-center extraction, which suffers from sparse angular coverage and mixed-pixel corruption. This paper presents P$^2$Calib, which exploits pattern priors, geometric constraints specified by the CAD model of the target board, to improve calibration accuracy. First, we incorporate the known hole radius as a fitting constraint to prevent center estimates from degrading under sparse angular coverage. Building on the improved hole estimates, we further enforce the rigid rectangular layout of the four holes as a global consistency constraint to correct residual errors across holes. Both priors are integrated into an interactive calibration tool that provides a complete extrinsic calibration pipeline. Experiments on simulated and real datasets show that P$^2$Calib lowers the joint registration residual by 90\% and 82\% and the held-out reprojection error by 96\% and 77\% over the baseline. Code, https://github.com/JokerJohn/P2Calib.git, and data will be released to facilitate future research.

</details>


### [75] [PhysReal: Learning Real-World Deformable Object Physics via Hybrid Constitutive Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07532)
*Yinan Deng,Jianqiao Song,Yisi Zhang,Yuhan Wang,Jiahui Wang,Yufeng Yue*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PhysReal是一种视频驱动的框架，用于学习真实可变形物体的物理动态，整合了空间变化的混合专家-神经本构模型与可微MPM模拟器和3DGS渲染器，并通过渐进式课程学习从稀疏视觉观测中识别全局材料属性、空间变化局部参数和神经本构残差，在动态重建和未来状态预测方面表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界可变形物体的动力学往往源于复杂的、空间异质的材料响应，现有的物理建模方法难以准确捕捉这种空间不均匀性，因此从视觉观测中学习物理上合理的动态过程仍然是交互式世界模型和具身智能体的关键挑战。

Method: PhysReal框架包含三个核心组成部分：(1) 空间变化的混合专家-神经本构模型——解析专家模型提供可解释的物理先验，神经本构残差捕捉超出预定义公式的材料响应，空间分布补丁参数化本构场以实现连续表征；(2) 可微MPM模拟器和3DGS渲染器作为物理与视觉重建的桥梁；(3) 渐进式课程策略，依次优化全局材料属性、空间变化局部参数和神经本构残差，并辅以运动与掩码监督。

Result: 在多种可变形物体交互场景上的大量实验表明，PhysReal在动态重建和未来状态预测方面取得了优于现有方法的性能，并展现出对下游机器人应用的强大潜力。

Conclusion: PhysReal通过结合物理先验与神经网络的混合本构建模、空间连续参数化以及渐进式优化课程，有效解决了从稀疏视觉观测中学习真实可变形物体物理动态的难题，为交互式世界模型和机器人应用提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Learning physically plausible dynamics from visual observations is essential for interactive world models and embodied agents. However, modeling real-world deformable objects remains challenging because their dynamics often arise from complex, spatially heterogeneous material responses. To address this challenge, we propose PhysReal, a video-driven framework for learning and simulating the underlying physics of real deformable objects. PhysReal integrates a spatially varying hybrid expert-neural constitutive model with a differentiable MPM simulator and 3DGS renderer. Analytical expert models provide interpretable physical priors, while neural constitutive residuals capture material responses beyond predefined formulations. Spatially distributed patches parameterize the constitutive field, enabling a continuous representation of local material variations. To organize the identification of this model from sparse visual observations, we adopt a progressive curriculum that sequentially optimizes global material properties, spatially varying local parameters, and neural constitutive residuals, together with complementary motion and mask supervision. Extensive experiments on diverse deformable-object interactions demonstrate that PhysReal achieves superior performance in dynamic reconstruction and future-state prediction, while showing strong potential for downstream robotic applications.

</details>


### [76] [Zero-Shot Sim-to-Real Contact-Rich Assembly via Proprioception-Anchored Cross-Modal Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07534)
*Yuhan Wang,Yurou Chen,Hongye Jiang,Wenzhao Lian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出PACE（本体感觉锚定的跨模态编码器），利用本体感觉在仿真与硬件间的一致性，通过监督视觉和力/触觉表示预测本体感觉状态转换，来消除跨域差异因素，实现无需真实微调或物体位姿追踪的接触丰富装配策略迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触丰富的装配任务需要亚毫米级空间精度和可靠的力感知，虽然基于仿真的强化学习提供了可扩展的训练范式，但视觉观测、接触动力学和力/触觉测量的域差异常常限制策略迁移。本文观察到本体感觉在跨域间具有较高的一致性，因此希望利用这一特性来解决sim-to-real迁移的核心挑战。

Method: 提出PACE（Proprioception-Anchored Cross-Modal Encoder）框架：通过对时域视觉和力/触觉表示施加监督，使其预测本体感觉状态转换（proprioceptive state transitions）。由于静态域特定因素（如光照、纹理、传感器偏置）对关节运动信息贡献甚少，该目标可促使编码器抑制这些干扰因素，同时保留与任务相关的运动线索。基于冻结PACE特征训练策略，无需真实微调或物体位姿追踪即可部署于硬件。

Result: 在四个接触丰富的装配任务中，PACE在真实世界平均成功率达93.3%，sim-to-real性能落差仅2.7个百分点，且对会大幅降低基于位姿方法和学习融合基线的扰动具有较强鲁棒性。

Conclusion: PACE通过以本体感觉为锚点的跨模态表示学习，有效解决了接触丰富装配中的仿真到真实迁移难题，在保持高性能的同时显著提升了对扰动的鲁棒性，无需真实世界微调即可实现可靠部署。

Abstract: Contact-rich assembly remains challenging because it requires submillimeter spatial accuracy and reliable interpretation of forces during sustained contact. Although simulation-based reinforcement learning offers a scalable training paradigm, discrepancies in visual observations, contact dynamics, and force/torque (F/T) measurements often limit policy transfer. We observe that proprioception is comparatively consistent across domains because calibrated joint positions and consistently computed joint velocities align closely between simulation and hardware. Based on this observation, we present PACE (Proprioception-Anchored Cross-Modal Encoder), which supervises temporal visual and F/T representations by predicting proprioceptive state transitions. Static domain-specific factors, including lighting, texture, and sensor bias, contain little information about joint motion; the proposed objective therefore encourages the encoder to suppress these factors while retaining task-relevant motion cues. Policies trained on frozen PACE features are deployed on hardware without real-world fine-tuning or object-pose tracking. Across four contact-rich assembly tasks, PACE attains an average real-world success rate of 93.3\% and only a 2.7-percentage-point sim-to-real drop, meanwhile remaining robust to perturbations that substantially degrade pose-based and learned-fusion baselines.

</details>


### [77] [Anti-Gravity Walking by a Flying Humanoid Robot via Thrust-Rate Input Whole-Body Model Predictive Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07544)
*Kazuki Sugihara,Kei Okada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于反重力环境下双足行走的实时全身模型预测控制（MPC）框架，通过使用推力变化率作为控制输入来保证接触切换时推力轨迹的连续性，并为足部法向接触力引入下界约束以弥补反重力环境中自然支撑力的缺失，最终在仿真和硬件实验中首次实现了飞行人形机器人的天花板行走。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 飞行人形机器人现有的运动能力主要局限于空中飞行和地面行走，缺乏在复杂三维空间中（如天花板及反重力环境）行走的能力。反重力行走中，接触切换伴随的动态结构突变会引起推力尖峰，导致控制不稳定。

Method: 1) 将推力变化率（thrust-rate）作为控制输入来构建全身MPC，保证接触切换过程中推力轨迹的连续性，同时保持最优控制问题的稀疏结构以实现快速计算；2) 引入足部法向接触力的下界约束，并在双支撑阶段平滑过渡这些约束，以弥补反重力环境中自然支撑力的缺失；3) 通过仿真和真实硬件实验验证所提出框架的性能。

Result: 通过仿真和硬件实验成功演示了飞行人形机器人的反重力（天花板）行走，据作者所知，这是首次实现可变形空中机器人的多接触全身MPC以及飞行人形机器人超越地面的行走。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于推力变化率的全身MPC框架有效解决了反重力行走中接触切换导致的控制不稳定问题，并成功实现了飞行人形机器人超出地面环境的行走能力，拓展了该类机器人的应用范围。

Abstract: Flying humanoids are expected to perform tasks in diverse environments, while their existing locomotion is mainly limited to aerial flight and ground walking. The capability to move in complex three-dimensional space can greatly expand their application range. For such walking motion on ceilings and similar anti-gravity environments, whole-body MPC is effective. However, the discontinuous changes in dynamic structure accompanying contact switching during walking can induce thrust spikes, resulting in control instability. Therefore, in this work, we propose and implement a real-time whole-body MPC framework for anti-gravity bipedal walking. First, we formulate whole-body MPC using the time derivative of thrust, namely thrust-rate, as the control input. This formulation guarantees continuity of the thrust trajectory during contact switching while preserving the sparse structure of the optimal control problem for fast computation. Second, we address the lack of natural support forces in anti-gravity environments. We introduce lower bounds on the foot-normal component of the contact force, and smoothly transfer them during the doublesupport phase. Finally, we implement the proposed framework and demonstrate anti-gravity walking by a flying humanoid through simulation and a hardware experiment. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first demonstration of multi-contact whole-body MPC for a transformable aerial robot and walking by a flying humanoid beyond the ground.

</details>


### [78] [ICI-VLA: In-Context Imitation with Spatiotemporally Aligned Demonstrations for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07581)
*Songhua Yang,Ziyu Liu,Xuetao Li,Ruqi Xiao,Kangxin Zhu,Miao Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出ICI-VLA框架，通过在测试时提供上下文演示（in-context demonstrations）实现少样本自适应，无需对VLA策略进行梯度更新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的VLA策略通常需要额外的梯度更新来适应新操作场景，这在任务数据或计算资源稀缺时限制了快速部署，因此需要一种无需在线更新即可完成测试时自适应的方法。

Method: ICI-VLA在离线训练时更新参数，推理时保持策略固定，通过检索微型演示来调节动作生成。将长轨迹分解为带语义标签的短示例，并训练RD-Encoder（使用DTW挖掘正样本）使检索上下文与当前子任务的阶段和几何对齐。引入目标动作掩码（Target Action Masking）作为上下文损坏目标，减少直接复制动作的倾向，增加对当前观测的依赖。

Result: 在LIBERO上平均成功率97.7%，在RoboTwin 2.0上达到60.4%，比最高基线平均高出19.3个百分点；四个物理任务上达到83.2%。

Conclusion: 固定参数的VLA策略能够通过在测试时条件化于时空对齐的演示中获益，验证了无需梯度更新的少量样本测试时自适应的可行性。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies are commonly adapted to new manipulation settings through additional gradient updates, which limits rapid deployment when task-specific data or compute is scarce. We present ICI-VLA, a training and retrieval framework that equips a text-action VLM with few-shot test-time adaptation through in-context demonstrations. Unlike mainstream VLA designs based on action-specific multimodal fusion, ICI-VLA retains the native text-generation interface. ICI-VLA updates its parameters only during offline training; at inference, the policy remains fixed and conditions action generation on retrieved micro-demonstrations. The framework decomposes long trajectories into short, semantically labeled examples and trains an RD-Encoder with positives mined by Dynamic Time Warping (DTW), aligning the retrieved context with the phase and geometry of the current subtask. We further introduce Target Action Masking, a context-corruption objective designed to reduce direct action copying and increase reliance on the current observation. ICI-VLA reaches average success rates of 97.7% on LIBERO and 60.4% on RoboTwin 2.0, exceeding the highest reported baseline average on RoboTwin 2.0 by 19.3 percentage points. It also achieves 83.2% across four physical tasks. These results indicate that a fixed VLA policy can benefit from conditioning on spatiotemporally aligned demonstrations at test time.

</details>


### [79] [Dex-X: Learning Visual-Tactile Dexterous Manipulation From Human Videos with Simulated Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07747)
*Ruoqu Chen,Feixiang Ruan,Liu Cao,Zihao Wang,Botian Xu,Shiqin Tong,Jiajun Liu,Mingzhi Pei,Chenyu Zhang,Wanli Xing,Kaifeng Zhang,Mengdi Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DEX-X是一个从人类视频演示中学习视觉-触觉灵巧操作策略的框架。其核心洞察在于利用仿真作为"触觉补全引擎"——将单目人类演示在仿真中重建手-物交互，从中获得原始视频中缺失的物理触觉监督信息，训练视觉-触觉策略并蒸馏为可在点云观测和触觉传感上部署的策略。实验展示了零样本仿真到现实迁移的能力，在灵巧手-臂平台上完成了多种抓取和接触丰富的工具操作任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类视频是灵巧操作的丰富来源，但缺乏接触丰富的交互中至关重要的触觉信息。这引发了一个根本性问题：能否仅从人类视频演示中学习可在现实部署的视觉-触觉灵巧操作策略，而无需机器人侧数据采集？

Method: DEX-X框架包含三个关键步骤：(1) 从单目人类视频演示中重建手-物交互场景于仿真环境；(2) 在仿真中利用物理基础的接触动力学生成原始视频中缺失的触觉监督；(3) 利用这些恢复的触觉信息训练视觉-触觉教师策略，并将其蒸馏为可部署策略，该策略基于点云观测和触觉传感运行。

Result: 在灵巧手-臂平台上实现了零样本仿真到现实的迁移，涵盖多种抓取和接触丰富的工具使用任务。教师策略在仿真中六个任务类别平均成功率达65.9%；蒸馏后的视觉-触觉策略在真实世界立方体抓取中成功率达93%，在具有挑战性的桌面清洁任务中达53%。在物体抓取任务中还观察到了对未见物体几何形状的零样本泛化能力。

Conclusion: 结果表明，模拟交互是连接人类视频与可部署灵巧操作策略的关键桥梁，提供了从互联网规模人类视频数据中实现可扩展机器人技能学习所缺失的物理监督。

Abstract: Human videos are an abundant source of dexterous manipulation behaviors, but they lack tactile information that is crucial for contact-rich interaction. This raises a fundamental question: can robots learn deployable visual-tactile dexterous manipulation policies from human video demonstrations without robot-side data collection?
  We present DEX-X, a framework for learning visual-tactile dexterous manipulation from human videos through simulation. Our key insight is that simulation can serve as a tactile completion engine. Given monocular human demonstrations, DEX-X reconstructs hand-object interactions in simulation, where physically grounded contact dynamics provide tactile supervision unavailable in the original videos. Leveraging this recovered tactile information, we train visual-tactile dexterous manipulation policies and distill them into deployable policies operating on point-cloud observations and tactile sensing.
  We demonstrate zero-shot sim-to-real transfer on a dexterous hand-arm platform across diverse grasping and contact-rich tool-use tasks. The teacher policy achieves 65.9% average success across six task categories in simulation, while the distilled visual-tactile policy achieves 93% success on real-world cube picking and 53% on the challenging table-cleaning task. Zero-shot generalization to unseen object geometries is also observed on object-picking tasks.
  Our results suggest that simulated interaction is a key bridge between human videos and deployable dexterous manipulation policies, providing the missing physical supervision needed for scalable robot skill learning from Internet-scale human video data.

</details>


### [80] [ComVLA: Communication-Aware Split Inference for VLA Models in 6G-Connected Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07838)
*Boliang Liu,Wint Yi Poe,Jingyun Di,Riccardo Trivisonno,Giuseppe Caire*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ComVLA是一个面向6G连接机器人的框架，利用语言指令中的语义信息来指导视觉token的筛选，使VLA模型的token预算适应无线信道容量。在LIBERO基准上，用32个token替代512个，推理计算量降低74%，推理延迟降低22%，任务成功率仅损失1.5个百分点（95.4% vs 96.9%），并在Rayleigh和Rician衰落信道下保持在容量预算内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型因体积过大无法在机器人上运行，通常将推理卸载到云端，但无线链路限制了每步控制周期内可传输的传感数据量。现有方案中，语义通信编解码器需要针对信道重新训练，而VLA token剪枝器仅从图像中选择token却忽略了信道状态，两者均未充分利用语言指令中丰富的语义信息。

Method: 提出ComVLA框架，核心洞察是语言指令中蕴含的密集语义信息已经指示了哪些视觉token是重要的。该方法利用语言引导来自适应调整VLA的token预算，使其与信道容量相匹配，实现了VLA推理与无线通信的协同设计。

Result: 在LIBERO基准上，以32个token替代512个token，推理计算量降低74%，推理延迟降低22%（对比OpenVLA-OFT基线），平均任务成功率为95.4% vs 96.9%（损失1.5个百分点），并在Rayleigh和Rician衰落信道下保持在容量预算内。

Conclusion: 结果表明，协同设计VLA推理与无线通信是6G连接机器人领域的一个实用方向，语言引导的token自适应机制能有效兼顾通信约束与任务性能。

Abstract: Connected robotics is an emerging 6G application where mobile robots follow natural-language instructions to manipulate physical objects. The Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models that enable this are too large to run on the robot; a common trend is to offload inference to the cloud. The wireless link, however, limits how much sensing data the edge can transmit per control step. Two recent lines address this constraint: semantic communication codecs compress sensor data but require channel-specific retraining, and VLA token pruners select tokens from image but ignore the channel. Our insight is that the dense semantic information contained in the language already indicates which visual tokens matter. We propose ComVLA, a framework that uses this language guidance to adapt the VLA token budget to the channel capacity. Transmitting 32 tokens instead of 512 on the LIBERO benchmark, ComVLA cuts inference compute by 74% and inference latency by 22% versus the original OpenVLA-OFT baseline, at a cost of 1.5 pp in average task success (95.4% vs. 96.9%), and it stays within the capacity budget under Rayleigh and Rician fading. These results demonstrate that co-designing VLA inference and wireless communication is a practical direction for 6G-connected robotics.

</details>


### [81] [Scene Graph-Driven Haptic Feedback for Safety Enhancement in Robotic Ophthalmic Surgery via Physically Simulated iOCT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07857)
*Danial Arbabi,Korab Hoxha,Angelo Henriques,Mirza Imamovic,M. Ali Nasseri*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于场景图（Scene Graph）的触觉反馈系统，用于机器人辅助视网膜下注射手术。系统通过分析物理模拟的术中OCT影像构建实时手术场景图，并利用确定性规则引擎生成状态依赖的触觉反馈。用户研究（N=16）表明该系统将针头对准误差降低了14%（p=0.044），系统可用性评分提高了8%（p=0.015），同时保持相近的任务完成时间，并促使受试者采用更安全的"先对准后接近"策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人眼科手术虽然精度高，但将外科医生与器械解耦，造成"感觉鸿沟"，即失去了触觉反馈。这增加了手术风险，需要一种能桥接这一感知差距的触觉反馈机制。

Method: 系统首先从物理模拟的术中OCT影像中分析并构建实时的手术场景图作为语义抽象层，随后利用一个确定性、基于规则的引擎，根据手术场景状态在机器人输入设备上生成触觉反馈。

Result: 用户研究（N=16）显示：针头对准误差降低14%（p=0.044），系统可用性评分（SUS）提升8%（p=0.015），任务完成时间无明显差异。轨迹分析揭示了更安全的"先对准后接近"策略的涌现。

Conclusion: 场景图可作为机器人显微手术中实时、增强安全性的情境感知触觉反馈的有效计算基础，有助于缩小机器人手术中的感知鸿沟。

Abstract: Robotic ophthalmic surgery offers high precision but introduces a "sensory gap" by decoupling the surgeon from their instrument, resulting in a loss of tactile feedback. This paper presents a novel haptic feedback system for subretinal injection tasks leveraging Scene Graphs (SG). The system bridges the sensory gap by analyzing a physically simulated intraoperative Optical Coherence Tomography (iOCT) feed to construct a real-time surgical SG. The SG serves as a semantic abstraction layer for the surgical scene, which is then utilized by a deterministic, rule-based engine to generate state-dependent haptic feedback on a robotic input device. The system was evaluated in a user study (N=16) using an anthropomorphic head phantom and a custom-built surgical robot. Results demonstrate that the SG-driven haptic feedback improved surgical precision, reducing needle alignment error by 14% (p = 0.044) and improving System Usability Scale (SUS) scores by 8% (p = 0.015), while maintaining comparable task completion times. A needle trajectory analysis revealed the emergence of a safer "Align-then-Approach" strategy, in which our haptic negative reinforcement prompted users to fine-tune the tool's trajectory before approaching the retinal target. This work suggests that SGs can effectively serve as the direct computational foundation for real-time, safety-enhancing context-aware haptic feedback in robotic microsurgery.

</details>


### [82] [M3-Tele: A Unified Multimodal Teleoperational Framework for Compliant Whole-Body Mobile Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07859)
*Hengxiang Chen,Shenwen Deng,Yujian Ma,Gan Ma,Qiang Li,Nutan Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出M3-Tele框架，一个统一的多模态遥操作系统，用于柔顺的全身移动操作，实现了视觉、触觉、力觉和本体感觉的对齐采集，显著提升了接触丰富的遥操作性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的遥操作和数据采集框架往往忽略了多模态感知与全身协调操作的联合考虑，导致演示采集效率和质量受限，进而影响下游策略学习的效果。

Method: 提出M3-Tele统一多模态遥操作框架，集成了全身协调和物理柔顺控制，能够稳定地进行物理交互，并在任务执行过程中捕获对齐的视觉、触觉、力觉和本体感觉观测数据。

Result: 力跟踪误差从4.132N降至0.346N，接触丢失从每次实验2.46次降至0.02次，触觉变形误差降低65%。四个移动操作任务的用户研究验证了系统的可靠性和可用性。Diffusion Policy实验进一步证明了联合触觉和力传感的价值。

Conclusion: M3-Tele框架通过整合多模态感知与柔顺全身控制，有效提升了接触丰富型遥操作任务的性能与演示数据质量，并证实了多模态传感（尤其是触觉与力觉联合）对下游策略学习的促进作用。

Abstract: Executing contact-rich tasks efficiently requires the seamless integration of whole-body coordination and physical compliance regulation. However, existing teleoperation and data-collection frameworks often overlook the joint consideration of multimodal perception and coordinated whole-body operation. This limitation can reduce the efficiency and quality of demonstration collection, thereby affecting the effectiveness of downstream policy learning. In this work, we present \textbf{M3-Tele}: A Unified \underline{M}ultimodal \underline{Tele}operational Framework for Compliant Whole-Body \underline{M}obile \underline{M}anipulation, enabling stable physical interaction and capturing aligned visual, tactile, force, and proprioceptive observations during task execution. Extensive experiments demonstrate that the proposed framework significantly improves contact-rich teleoperation performance. The proposed controller reduces the force tracking error from 4.132~N to 0.346~N, the contact loss from 2.46 to 0.02 events per trial and the tactile deformation error by 65\%. User studies across four mobile manipulation tasks also verify the reliability and usability of the proposed system. Furthermore, Diffusion Policy experiments highlight the value of joint tactile and force sensing.

</details>


### [83] [Conditional Timed Partial Orders: An Expressive and Interpretable Framework for Robot Task Specification and Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07905)
*Sebastian Escobar,Morteza Lahijanian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出条件时序偏序（cTPO），扩展了传统TPO的表达能力，支持更丰富的相对时序约束和基于环境条件的条件事件激活，同时提出一种分解算法将大型cTPO拆分为子TPO序列以解决计算难题，实验显示最高可达四个数量级的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统TPO仅能表达简单的时序约束和偏序事件，表达能力有限，难以满足复杂机器人任务规范的需求。

Method: 引入cTPO扩展TPO的表达能力，将规划问题归约为MILP；针对大规模MILP计算困难，提出分解算法将cTPO划分为多个子TPO，生成一系列更小的MILP问题。

Result: 证明了分解算法的完备性和最优性保持特性，实验表明相比整体MILP方案可获得最高四个数量级的加速。

Conclusion: cTPO作为机器人任务规范框架有效实用，分解算法显著提升了计算效率，同时增强了复杂任务的可解释性。

Abstract: Timed Partial Orders (TPOs), originally proposed for workflows, provide an interpretable framework for robot task specification with planning algorithms based on mixed-integer linear programming (MILP). However, TPOs are limited in expressivity, capturing only partial-order events with simple timing constraints. In this paper, we introduce Conditional TPOs (cTPOs), which extend TPOs with richer relative-timing constraints and conditional event activations based on environmental conditions. We show that planning for cTPOs also reduces to an MILP problem; however, the added expressivity results in significantly larger MILPs that can become computationally intractable. To address this challenge, we propose a decomposition algorithm that partitions a cTPO into smaller sub-TPOs, yielding a sequence of smaller MILP problems. We prove that this decomposition is complete and preserves plan optimality while improving the interpretability of complex tasks. Experimental results demonstrate the effectiveness of cTPOs as a task specification framework and the efficiency of our decomposition approach, achieving up to four orders of magnitude speedup over the monolithic MILP.

</details>


### [84] [A Multimodal Label Forecasting Method for Aperiodic Visuo-Motor Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07930)
*Borui He,Garrett E Katz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文关注无周期性的时间序列预测（TSF）新任务——基于第一人称视觉和本体感觉预测仿人机器人运动过程中的跌倒。作者贡献了两个新基准数据集（仿真与真实硬件），证明现有深度TSF方法在该任务上表现不佳，并提出一种利用内源与外生变量并严格保证i.i.d采样训练的新架构，在真实数据上提升12.73%以上、仿真数据上提升10.40%以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TSF基准测试大多假设时间序列具有近似周期性，但许多实际场景（如仿人机器人行走的多样性轨迹）并不满足这一假设。作者指出当前Transformer和MLP方法之争主要建立在周期性基准之上，忽视了非周期性场景下深度TSF方法的失效问题，因此需要探索更通用的TSF能力。

Method: (1) 构建两个非周期性跌倒预测基准数据集（仿真+真实硬件）；(2) 提出一种融合内源变量（本体感觉）和外生变量（第一人称视觉）的新型深度学习架构；(3) 设计一种严格强制训练样本i.i.d采样的训练流程，以应对非周期性时序数据带来的分布偏移问题。

Result: 在与先前最优方法的对比中，该方法在多个实验条件下取得统计显著的性能提升：在真实硬件数据上提升12.73%以上，在仿真数据上提升10.40%以上。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了现有深度TSF方法在违反周期性假设的真实任务中的局限性，通过新基准和新型架构证明了外生变量与严格i.i.d训练在非周期性TSF任务中的价值，为该领域提供了新的研究方向和数据资源。

Abstract: Deep learning models have been increasingly applied to Time Series Forecasting (TSF) in recent years. Transformer-based and MLP-based models have both been used effectively on many real-world TSF regression benchmarks, and there is ongoing debate as to which family of methods is best. While these benchmarks have drawn much attention, it is also worth noting that many current datasets and methods assume approximate periodicity in the time series. In this work, we focus on a new TSF task without periodicity: anticipating falls during humanoid locomotion, on the basis of egocentric vision and proprioception. When the locomotion trajectories are sufficiently diverse, periodicity is violated. We contribute two new benchmark datasets (one from simulation, one from real hardware), showing that periodicity is violated and recent deep TSF methods struggle on these benchmarks. We also propose a novel deep learning architecture that exploits both endogenous and exogenous variables and a training process that rigorously enforces i.i.d sampling of training examples. Our results show statistically significant improvement over prior art in multiple experimental conditions, by 12.73% or more on the real data and 10.40% or more on the simulation data. Code and datasets will be available upon acceptance.

</details>


### [85] [SPOT: Spatial Perception-Oriented Long-Horizon Humanoid Teleoperation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07933)
*Lixing Fang,Ziyan Xiong,Sunli Chen,Zhiyang Dou,Chuang Gan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SPOT是一种面向空间感知的VR遥操作系统，通过机器人搭载双目鱼眼相机、宽视场立体显示、视点解耦自由观察和视觉稳定化，为操作者提供宽广、稳定且可主动检查的机器人视角，从而收集长时程人形机器人演示数据，克服传统方法中感知视界受限的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高质量演示数据是训练通用人形机器人的关键瓶颈。现有遥操作系统虽然解决了人体运动到机器人运动的重定向问题，但长时程局部操作任务要求操作者保持任务相关的空间感知（物体位置、环境、机器人姿态），而现有方法因窄视野、行走中相机不稳定以及头视耦合控制干扰机器人运动等问题，显著缩短了操作者的感知视界。

Method: SPOT系统结合四方面技术：机器人双目鱼眼相机提供宽视场感知；宽视场立体显示增强沉浸感；视点解耦自由观察将视觉探索与机器人驱动解耦，自然头部转动只改变观察方向而非控制机器人头部/相机/躯干；视觉稳定化消除行走过程中的图像晃动。系统在操作者周围渲染虚拟半球上的立体观察画面，实现宽、稳、可主动检查的机器人视角。

Result: 在多项感知关键型任务中（掉物恢复、周边取物、大工作空间双臂操作、精细对准、动态交互），SPOT在效率、准确性和恢复速度方面均优于现有方法，验证了其在长时程人形机器人数据采集中的有效性。

Conclusion: SPOT通过提供扩展的感知视界，实现了更友好、可扩展的长时程人形机器人演示数据采集，为克服数据瓶颈提供了有效方案。

Abstract: High-quality demonstration data is becoming a central bottleneck for training general-purpose humanoid robots. While recent humanoid teleoperation systems have made substantial progress in retargeting human motion to robot motion, long-horizon loco-manipulation requires another capability: operators must maintain task-relevant spatial awareness over time, e.g., object locations, surrounding environments, the robot's pose. We call the extent of this awareness the operator's perceptual horizon. However, existing methods often shorten this: narrow views miss peripheral events, robot-mounted cameras become unstable during locomotion, and coupled head-view control makes looking around interfere with robot motion. We present SPOT, a Spatial Perception-Oriented VR Teleoperation system for collecting long-horizon humanoid demonstration data by providing extended perceptual horizon. SPOT combines a robot-mounted binocular fisheye camera, a wide-field stereoscopic display, viewpoint-decoupled free-looking, and visual stabilization to provide a robot-centric view that is wide, stable, and actively inspectable. Unlike conventional egocentric interfaces, SPOT decouples visual exploration from robot actuation: the egocentric stereo observation is rendered on a virtual hemisphere around the operator, so natural head rotations change where the operator looks within the wide-field view rather than commanding the robot head, camera, or torso. We evaluate SPOT on perception-critical humanoid data-collection tasks spanning drop recovery, peripheral retrieval, large-workspace bimanual manipulation, fine alignment, and dynamic interaction. SPOT improves efficiency, accuracy, and recovery speed, demonstrating its effectiveness for user-friendly and scalable long-horizon humanoid data collection.

</details>


### [86] [mjorbit: A Simulation Framework for Space Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08010)
*John Z. Zhang,Joris Verhagen,Fausto Vega,Patrick McKeen,Zachary Manchester*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个用于模拟带接触的多体空间机器人的通用框架 mjorbit，该框架基于 MuJoCo 引擎构建，添加了关键的航天器动力学、执行器和传感器，并提供低延迟的 C++ CPU 后端和高吞吐量的 GPU 后端，通过模型预测控制和强化学习在多个在轨案例中进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间在轨服务、组装和制造（ISAM）应用需要高效、大规模的空间机器人仿真平台，但现有的机器人仿真框架缺乏对轨道动力学和支持接触的多体系统仿真的支持，需要在轨动力学耦合方法和性能之间取得平衡。

Method: 首先对将轨道传播与现有机器人仿真框架耦合的方法进行实证权衡研究；然后基于 MuJoCo 引擎构建 mjorbit 框架，添加航天器动力学、执行器和传感器，提供简单 Python API 背后的低延迟 C++ CPU 后端和高吞吐量 GPU 后端。

Result: 通过模型预测控制和强化学习算法在多个现实的在轨案例研究中验证了 mjorbit 的有效性和性能。

Conclusion: mjorbit 为 ISAM 应用提供了高效、可扩展且通用的空间机器人接触仿真方案，并已开源提供代码和示例。

Abstract: This paper presents a general framework for simulating multi-body space robots with contact. We bring efficient, large-scale robot simulation to in-space servicing, assembly, and manufacturing applications. First, we perform an empirical trade study of methods for coupling orbit propagation with existing robotics simulation frameworks. Next, we present mjorbit, a general, flexible, and performant framework built on the MuJoCo engine widely used in robotics, to which we add key spacecraft dynamics, actuators, and sensors. We provide a low-latency C++ CPU backend and a high-throughput GPU backend behind a simple Python API. We demonstrate mjorbit by solving several realistic on-orbit case studies with both model-predictive control and reinforcement learning. Open-source code and examples are available at: https://johnzhang3.github.io/mjorbit/

</details>


### [87] [DISEIL: Demonstration Distillation for Sample-Efficient Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08123)
*Suyog Khanal,Arun Kumar A,Santu Rana*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出DISEIL框架，用于交互式模仿学习中智能决策"何时纠正失败"以及"如何请求演示"。通过对失败轨迹进行聚类识别失败模式，再利用视觉语言模型和语言模型生成有针对性的专家演示请求。实验显示在10个设置中的7个取得最高平均保留成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的交互式模仿学习方法只决定何时打断学习者，但"纠正哪个失败"和"演示从哪里开始"这两个关键决策却被偶然性事件所决定，缺少有意为之的机制，导致样本效率不高。

Method: DISEIL方法包括：(1)在策略首次变得不可靠的步骤标记每个失败片段；(2)用几何描述符表示该时刻；(3)将失败聚类为反复出现的失败模式；(4)通过视觉语言模型和语言模型阅读选中的失败模式并撰写下一个演示的请求；(5)利用任务约束存储来验证请求可行性后才花费专家时间。整个流程中没有任何模型直接产生机器人动作。

Result: 在5个模拟任务（状态观测和图像观测）下的10个设置中，仅改变专家请求的内容，DISEIL在7个设置中取得最高的平均保留成功率（1个并列），且在最小演示预算下优势最为明显。

Conclusion: DISEIL首次尝试让"纠正哪个失败"和"演示从哪里开始"这两个决策变得有目的性，验证了智能化的演示请求能显著提升样本效率。虽然当前范围有限（单轮练习、模拟环境、多为脚本专家），但长期目标是构建能追踪演示覆盖范围、按请求成本比例向人类教师请求缺失行为的智能学习者。

Abstract: A robot that can be taught a new task from a handful of demonstrations has to work out for itself what it still cannot do, and then ask for exactly that. Interactive imitation learning takes a step in that direction by letting a policy practice on its own and calling an expert when it goes wrong. Existing methods decide when to interrupt the learner. A further 2 decisions are left to whichever episode happened to trigger the interruption: which failure to correct, and where the demonstration should start. This paper is a first attempt at making both of them deliberately. DISEIL (Demonstration dIstillation for Sample-Efficient Imitation Learning) marks each failed episode at the step where the policy first becomes unreliable, represents that moment with a geometric descriptor, and groups the failures into recurring failure modes. A vision-language model and a language model read the selected mode and write a request for the next demonstration, and a store of task constraints checks that the request can be carried out before any expert time is spent. No model produces a robot action. Across 5 simulated tasks under state and image observations, changing only what the expert is asked for gives the highest mean held-out success rate in all 10 settings, with a tie in 1, and the margin is widest at the smallest budget we tested. The scope is narrow: a single round of practice at a time, in simulation, with experts that are mostly scripted. The longer-term aim is a learner that also tracks what its demonstration set already covers, and that asks a human teacher for the missing behavior in proportion to the effort each request costs them.

</details>


### [88] [OmniNav: Robust Long-Horizon Target Navigation in Dynamic Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08159)
*Yujie Tang,Meiling Wang,Jinhao Jiang,Sibo Zuo,Yinan Deng,Xinyu Zhang,Yufeng Yue*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出OmniNav，一种将长时程目标导航形式化为联合场景有效性、目标信念和交互可行性三因子的任务状态后验的连续推断框架，并验证了其在语义目标导航、细粒度实例导航及真实场景抓取任务中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程目标导航需要机器人在不断演化的观测、决策和物理交互中持续执行任务，涉及三个耦合能力：维护有效的场景记忆、在部分可观测条件下修正目标信念、选择交互可行的导航端点。然而每个能力所依赖的状态都是条件性有效的——场景表示会因物体移动或消失而过时、失败搜索会改变目标位置信念、几何上方便的端点可能在操作上不可行。

Method: OmniNav将长时程导航形式化为对因子化任务状态后验的连续推断，涵盖场景有效性、目标信念和交互可行性三方面。具体包括：(1) 增量构建可更新的3D物体场景记忆，防止过时场景证据传播；(2) 引入证据感知的贝叶斯信念修正机制，从语义上下文推导依赖感知的区域先验，将不成功的搜索作为负证据进行更新；(3) 将操作可达性和碰撞约束纳入导航端点选择，并通过层次化闭环恢复传播执行反馈。

Result: 大量实验表明，OmniNav在语义ObjectNav和细粒度实例导航基准上取得了最高成功率，对目标重定位保持鲁棒性，并在真实世界的拾取-放置任务中相较于自适应开环基线将成功率从53.3%提升至71.7%。

Conclusion: OmniNav通过联合建模场景、信念与交互三方面的状态后验，有效解决了长时程导航中场景过时、信念不确定性和端点不可行等核心挑战，比现有方法具有显著性能优势。

Abstract: Long-horizon target navigation requires a robot to sustain task execution across evolving observations, decisions, and physical interactions. This requires three coupled capabilities: maintaining valid scene memory, revising target beliefs under partial observability, and selecting interaction-feasible navigation endpoints. However, the state underlying each capability is only conditionally valid: scene representations become stale when objects move or disappear, unsuccessful searches alter beliefs over target locations, and geometrically convenient endpoints may still be infeasible for manipulation. To address these challenges, we present OmniNav, which formulates long-horizon navigation as continual inference over a factorized task state posterior coupling scene validity, target belief, and interaction feasibility. For representation, OmniNav incrementally constructs an updatable 3D object scene memory, preventing stale scene evidence from propagating to subsequent decisions. For exploration, it introduces an evidence-aware Bayesian belief-revision mechanism that derives dependency-aware region priors from semantic context, incorporates unsuccessful searches as negative evidence, and updates them for posterior-guided frontier selection. For interaction, OmniNav incorporates manipulation reachability and collision constraints into navigation-endpoint selection and propagates execution feedback through hierarchical closed-loop recovery. Extensive experiments demonstrate that OmniNav achieves the highest success rates among the compared methods on semantic ObjectNav and fine-grained instance navigation benchmarks, remains robust to target relocation, and improves real-world pick-and-place success from 53.3% to 71.7% over an adapted open-loop baseline. The project page of OmniNav is available at https://omni-nav.github.io/.

</details>


### [89] [Dual-Layer Semantic-Spatial Belief Mapping for Aerial Object Goal Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08164)
*Jianqiang Xiao,Xiang Deng,Yuexuan Sun,Yanjin Wu,Wenbiao Yan,Liqiang Nie*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出AeroBelief框架，通过双层语义-空间置信映射将VLM的瞬态观测转化为持久空间引导，解决无人机户外目标导航中视觉语言模型输出噪声大、稀疏且空间不稳定等问题，在UAV-ON基准上取得最优的SR、OSR和SPL指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空中目标目标导航（ObjectNav）要求无人机仅凭机载视觉在未知户外环境定位目标。视觉语言模型（VLMs）虽能解释开放式目标描述与视觉观测，但其逐帧输出常含噪声、稀疏且空间上不稳定，难以直接用于可靠导航决策。

Method: 提出AeroBelief框架，包含三个关键组件：(1) 双层语义-空间置信映射：直觉层累积场景级语义线索指导探索，证据层保留目标特定观测用于接近与确认；(2) 证据门控融合将两层结合为空间置信热点；(3) 对象条件视觉推理配合保守证据限定提升观测可靠性，同时通过自我中心区域引导将四叉树覆盖转化为无人机中心的偏航对齐方向提案并通过时间承诺稳定化。区域评分独立于语义置信值，保持探索压力并减少重复低增益搜索。

Result: 在UAV-ON基准上，AeroBelief取得SR 21.61%、OSR 35.57%和SPL 10.62，均为对比方法中的最佳结果。

Conclusion: 实验结果验证了持久语义-空间置信、保守证据限定和时间稳定区域引导在空中ObjectNav任务中的有效性，为解决VLM帧级输出不可靠性提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Aerial Object Goal Navigation (ObjectNav) requires an unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) to locate a described target in an unknown outdoor environment using onboard visual observations. Vision-language models (VLMs) can interpret open-ended target descriptions and visual observations, but their frame-level outputs are often noisy, sparse, and spatially transient. We propose AeroBelief, a dual-layer semantic-spatial belief mapping framework that transforms transient VLM observations into persistent spatial guidance. It separates broad contextual plausibility from target-specific evidence: an intuition layer accumulates scene-level semantic cues for exploration, while an evidence layer preserves qualified target-specific observations for approach and confirmation. Evidence-gated fusion combines the two layers into spatial belief hotspots. We further introduce object-conditioned visual reasoning with conservative evidence qualification to improve observation reliability before spatial accumulation. In parallel, egocentric regional guidance converts quadtree coverage into UAV-centered, yaw-aligned directional proposals and stabilizes them through temporal commitment. Its regional scoring is independent of semantic belief values, maintaining exploration pressure and reducing repeated low-gain search. Experiments on the UAV-ON benchmark show that AeroBelief achieves the best reported overall SR, OSR, and SPL among the compared methods, reaching 21.61%, 35.57%, and 10.62, respectively. These results support the effectiveness of persistent semantic-spatial belief, conservative evidence qualification, and temporally stable regional guidance for aerial ObjectNav.

</details>


### [90] [Monkey See, Can Monkey Do? A Benchmark for Evaluating Robot Skill Learning by Observation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08209)
*Weiwei Gu,Anmol Gupta,Anant Sah,Ryan Varghese,Lalitha Shreya Vanam,Prabhath Adireddi,Peter Karkus,Nakul Gopalan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了RoboReel，一个用于统一评估从人类视频中学习策略（LfO）模型的基准测试平台，包含10个操作任务、真人演示视频、仿真机器人轨迹和评估环境，并设计了四个测试套件以评估模型对视觉干扰的鲁棒性和长时程任务完成能力，覆盖7种以上SOTA算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LfO领域近期虽有进展，但现有方法在假设、硬件选择和环境设置上差异巨大，难以进行有意义的比较和评估领域进展，缺乏统一的基准测试平台。

Method: 构建RoboReel基准：包含捆绑的人类演示视频、仿真机器人轨迹和评估环境的10个操作任务；开发四个测试套件评估鲁棒性和长时程能力；涵盖LfO不同类别模型以及多种表征选择，评测7种以上SOTA算法（包括VLA变体）。

Result: 通过对多类算法在RoboReel基准上的系统评测，分析表明长时程任务和低容错任务对当前模型仍具挑战性；同时研究了多种表征选择对模型性能的影响。

Conclusion: RoboReel提供了公平、可比的LfO评测环境，揭示了当前模型在长时程和精细操作任务上的局限性，为领域后续进展提供了标准化评估框架。

Abstract: Learning from Observation (LfO) is a fundamental robotic capability that replicates how humans and animals socially learn from each other. Beyond its biological parallels, this modality provides a practical solution for data scaling in sample-inefficient and data-starved domains like robotics. Recent work has demonstrated promising results in learning manipulation skills from human videos, yet progress in this area remains difficult to assess. Existing methods vary widely in assumptions, hardware choices, and environment setups making it difficult to draw meaningful comparisons and identify advances in the field. To address these challenges, we introduce RoboReel: a unified benchmark for evaluating models that learn policies from human videos. RoboReel consists of bundled real-world human demonstration videos, simulated robot trajectories, and evaluation environments on ten manipulation tasks. We develop four test suites to evaluate the models' performance on multiple axes, including the robustness to visual distractors and the ability to complete long-horizon tasks. Our benchmark covers learning-from-observation models from different categories, and studies the effectiveness of multiple representation choices in our benchmark evaluation that covers over seven state-of-the-art algorithms (including our VLA based variants) in the field of LfO. Finally, we present an analysis of the different types of algorithms showing that long-horizon tasks and tasks with low tolerances are still challenging for current models. Webpage: https://roboreel.github.io

</details>


### [91] [TacClip: a clip-on sensor measures dynamic contact forces without covering the fingerpads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08214)
*Yuqian Ye,Hao Li,Jingxi Xu,Haojun Feng,Seongheon Hong,Mark R. Cutkosky*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TacClip是一种轻量化可穿戴设备，通过光纤布拉格光栅(FBG)记录指尖接触力与振动引发的形变，可与视觉或手套式手部追踪系统结合，同时保留指尖裸露以维持自然触觉灵敏度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有手部追踪系统多关注手部姿态与运动，对动态接触交互（如力与振动）的捕捉不足，且传统传感方案可能遮蔽指尖、影响用户的自然触觉感知。

Method: 将FBG嵌入置于指甲上的小型塑料夹中，利用其感知指尖形变；可选地在聚酰亚胺条上附加FBG以配合视觉追踪的姿态估计。设备无需电子部件，可水下使用。

Result: 在手指按压实验中，TacClip在0-8N范围内的典型力估计误差低于0.5N；在布料处理和胶带边缘寻找测试中，能够捕捉探索性滑动产生的振动与动态事件。

Conclusion: TacClip提供了一种不遮蔽指尖的可穿戴力触觉传感方案，保留了自然触觉灵敏度，并具备水下适用性，适合与视觉/手套式手部追踪系统协同使用。

Abstract: TacClip is a minimally encumbering wearable device for recording fingertip deformation caused by contact forces and vibrations. It can be combined with vision- or glove-based hand tracking systems that leave the fingertips uncovered and provides a measure of dynamic contact interactions, while leaving the finger pads exposed so that the user retains natural sensitivity to texture, friction, temperature, and fine surface features. The signal is produced by a Fiber Bragg Grating (FBG) embedded on a small plastic clip mounted over the fingernail. Optionally, for use with vision-based tracking, additional FBGs on polyimide strips can complement camera-based pose estimation. In finger pressing tests, TacClip estimates the force magnitude with typical errors below $0.5~\mathrm{N}$ over a $0$--$8~\mathrm{N}$ range. In tests of cloth handling and tape edge finding, we show that it captures the vibrations and dynamic events generated during exploratory sliding. With no electronics, TacClip can also be used submerged in water, while preserving bare finger contact.

</details>


### [92] [Bridging Language and Physics: Automated Design of Continuum Robots with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08220)
*Jingyi Chen,Mohan Zhang,Laura Yao,Yingtai Ni,Jianmin Ji,Jie Peng,Song Wang,Tianlong Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出AID-SR多层级框架，通过将模拟器观测的物理状态转化为结构化反馈与LLM设计者形成闭环，结合语义批判、人工反馈和迭代优化，显著提升连续体机器人设计的物理可行性与功能性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在从高级描述自动化设计机器人方面展现潜力，但面对复杂物理交互时表现不佳，主要原因在于语言推理与实体物理后果之间存在鸿沟，导致生成的设计物理有效性低。

Method: 提出AID-SR多层级框架，构建闭环机制将模拟器观测到的物理状态转化为结构化反馈提供给LLM设计者，并整合语义批判、人工反馈与迭代细化过程，引导生成物理可行且功能有意义的机器人设计。

Result: 在14个跨及抓取、运动、操纵等任务的肌腱驱动连续体机器人基准上，AID-SR达到96.2%的仿真可行性检查通过率；经通用强化学习训练后，26.7%的机器人能成功完成任务；制造三台已设计机器人并在真实世界成功完成任务。

Conclusion: 广泛仿真与实物实验证明了AID-SR能够突破LLM在自动化设计连续体机器人方面的壁垒。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have recently emerged as a promising tool for automating robot design from high-level specifications, yet they remain ineffective for robots operating under complex physical interactions. This limitation stems from the gap between language-based reasoning and the physical consequences of embodiment, often resulting in designs with low physical validity. In this work, we propose a multi-layered framework, AID-SR, that establishes a closed loop by translating simulator-observed physical states into structured feedback for the LLM designer. Combined with semantic critique, human feedback, and iterative refinement, the framework promotes the generation of physically feasible and functionally meaningful robot designs. We evaluate our approach on tendon-driven continuum robots across a benchmark of 14 tasks spanning reaching, grasping, locomotion, and manipulation. The proposed framework achieves 96.2% rate for passing the simulation feasibility check and by applying a common reinforcement learning training, 26.7% robots can successfully fulfill the corresponding task. We then fabricate three designed robots of AID-SR that successfully complete the task in real-world. These extensive experiments across simulation and real-world environments demonstrate and break the wall of utilizing the LLMs for automated design of continuum robots. The source code and experimental resources are publicly available at https://github.com/UNITES-Lab/AID-SR.

</details>


### [93] [3DWay: Generalizing Robot Manipulation via 3D Consistent Waypoints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08224)
*Ziqin Huang,Yingyue Li,Chenyangguang Zhang,Ruida Zhang,Yuxin Chen,Gu Wang,Xingyu Liu,Masayoshi Tomizuka,Xiangyang Ji*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出3DWay框架，通过将3D路点预测重构为多视角一致的2D路点预测加几何三角化，实现显式的3D运动规范，同时保留预训练VLM的强先验，从而提升机器人在3D空间中的操作泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于轨迹的中间表示大多在2D图像空间中预测轨迹，存在固有的3D模糊性问题；即使结合深度信息，自由空间路点仍然存在歧义，限制了可靠的3D推理能力。

Method: 从多视角图像中预测3D一致的路点（3DWay），具体做法是将3D路点预测重新表述为多视角一致的2D路点预测，随后通过几何三角化恢复3D信息，从而兼顾VLM的强先验与显式的3D运动规范。

Result: 大量实验表明3DWay在3D空间定位和视觉语言推理方面有显著提升，展示了在可泛化机器人操作中的强大潜力，并能引导现有VLA模型获得更好的泛化性能。

Conclusion: 3DWay通过多视角一致2D路点加几何三角化的方案，有效弥合了模态差距，为机器人操作与预训练VLM之间的3D推理提供了可靠且可泛化的中间表示。

Abstract: Intermediate representations are key to bridging the modality gap between generalizable manipulation policies and large-scale pretrained vision-language models (VLMs). Among these, trajectory-based representations compactly represent motion-relevant cues, yet most existing approaches predict trajectories in 2D image space, resulting in intrinsic 3D ambiguity. Moreover, using 2D trajectories with depth still leaves the free-space waypoints ambiguous, limiting reliable 3D reasoning. To address this, we propose predicting 3D consistent waypoints (3DWay) from multi-view images. By reformulating 3D waypoints prediction as generating multi-view consistent 2D waypoints followed by geometric triangulation, we enable explicit 3D motion specification while preserving the strong priors of pretrained VLMs. The predicted waypoints can guide existing VLA models for better generalization or be directly executed on simple tasks. Extensive experiments show that 3DWay substantially improves 3D spatial grounding and vision-language reasoning, demonstrating strong potential for generalizable robot manipulation. Codes will be released at https://github.com/ziqin-h/3DWay.

</details>


### [94] [CALIPER: Clean Scenes Cannot Rank Physical Inference in Pretrained Visual Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08250)
*Aman Mehta,Riya Baviskar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出CALIPER测试方法，用于直接评估视觉编码器在物理推理任务中的能力。研究发现传统线性探针和扰动基准无法区分真正推断物理规律的编码器与不推断的编码器，而CALIPER通过在变化场景下校准-预测范式的测试，能有效拉开不同表征之间的性能差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练视觉编码器日益被用作操控世界模型的感知前端，但现有评估方式（扰动基准、线性探针）几乎总在干净固定相机场景下进行，无法区分编码器是真正推断物理还是仅利用视觉表面线索。需要一种能真正检验物理推理能力的直接测试方法。

Method: CALIPER方法：对未知质量和摩擦力的物体进行两次已知速度的撞击，第三次撞击仅显示到接触瞬间，用冻结特征的线性读出器预测物体滑动距离；通过交换另一物体的校准片段来检验证据是否真正被使用。在2,000个模拟场景和八种表征（V-JEPA 2到随机初始化的ViT及原始像素）上进行评估，并重新采样相机、光照和杂乱场景。

Result: 在变化场景下，校准增加+0.50 R²，交换则消除该增益；各表征性能从固定场景下差距小于0.02 R²扩展到0.50 R²的范围。V-JEPA 2在目标速度选择中误差4mm，随机ViT误差20mm（与忽略物体无异）。线性探针无法追踪这些差异：帧聚合方式变化比预训练影响更大。

Conclusion: 基准能否对模型进行排名是经验性问题，需要特定检验条件。论文提供三项检验来建立有效的物理推理基准：直接物理预测任务、证据使用检验（校准与交换），以及场景多样性压力测试。

Abstract: How far a pushed object slides depends on its mass and friction, which no single image reveals. Pretrained visual encoders are increasingly used as the perception front end of world models for manipulation, and their physical competence is assessed with perturbation benchmarks and linear probes, almost always in a clean, fixed-camera scene. We show that these assessments cannot distinguish an encoder that infers physics from one that does not. CALIPER (calibrate, then predict) is a direct test: an object of unknown mass and friction is struck twice at known speeds, a third strike is shown only up to the moment of contact, and a linear readout on frozen features must predict how far the object slides. Swapping in another object's calibration clips checks that the evidence is actually used. Across 2,000 simulated episodes and eight representations, from V-JEPA 2 to a randomly initialised ViT and raw pixels, calibration adds +0.50 R^2 and the swap removes it. Yet in the clean scene every representation lands within 0.02 R^2 of the ceiling set by true simulator state, because a fixed camera exposes the object's displacement directly in pixel coordinates. Resampling camera, lighting, and clutter for every clip spreads the same representations across 0.50 R^2; when the readout chooses a push speed for a goal distance, V-JEPA 2 misses by 4 mm and the random ViT by 20 mm, no better than ignoring the object. Linear probes track none of this: a change in frame aggregation moves a probe more than pretraining does, and erasing the probed mass direction from the same representation costs nothing in one scene and 0.35 R^2 in the other. Whether a benchmark can rank models is an empirical property, and we give three checks that establish it.

</details>


### [95] [Seeing is Not Believing: Breaking the Physical-to-Digital Trust Boundary in Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08280)
*Leming Shen,Shikai Geng,Yuanqing Zheng,Chris Xiaoxuan Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文揭示了ROS 2中一个严重漏洞：攻击者只需修改单个环境变量，即可通过预构建的钩子渗透并拦截发布前的遥测和控制信号，从而劫持机器人执行危险任务，同时用合成的虚假遥测欺骗下游验证器。在物理Franka Emika机械臂上的实验表明，该攻击可实现实时注入（抖动约3ms），对AI检测器的成功率高达87%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人协作中的任务交接依赖下游验证器执行的远程证明，该机制通过检查传感器遥测来确保机器人的物理行为与分配任务严格匹配。但作者质疑这些遥测数据本身是否可信，并发现其往往不可信，由此引出对ROS 2系统中信任链底层漏洞的研究动机。

Method: 作者通过修改单个环境变量触发预构建的恶意钩子，在ROS 2框架中实现遥测与控制信号的隐蔽拦截和注入；同时利用第三方Docker容器和辅助工具的广泛依赖，将包含恶意钩子的受损软件包进行分发，从而以低成本方式发起攻击。

Result: 在运行Secure ROS 2的物理Franka Emika机械臂上，该攻击能以约3ms抖动实时注入伪造遥测，保持时间同步和硬件完整性，且对基于AI的检测器实现了87%的攻击成功率。

Conclusion: ROS 2的安全机制存在根本性信任缺陷，遥测数据可能被轻易伪造，使得远程证明的可靠性受到严重威胁。作者已向ROS 2开发团队负责任地披露了这一发现，并提供了演示视频。

Abstract: In multi-robot collaboration, task handovers rely on downstream verifiers performing remote attestation, which inspects sensor telemetry to ensure a robot's physical behavior strictly matches its assigned task. But can this telemetry be trusted? We show that it often cannot. In this paper, we uncover a severe vulnerability in Robot Operating System (ROS) 2: by modifying a single environment variable, an adversary can execute a pre-built hook to covertly intercept and inject both telemetry and control signals before they are published. Consequently, adversaries can hijack a robot to perform dangerous tasks while spoofing downstream verifiers with synthesized fake telemetry. Worse still, by exploiting the widespread reliance on third-party Docker containers and auxiliary tools, attackers can distribute compromised packages embedded with these malicious hooks to launch such attacks easily. On a physical Franka Emika robotic arm running Secure ROS 2, our attack injects fabricated telemetry in real time with only around 3 ms of jitter, preserving temporal synchronization and hardware integrity while achieving an 87% success rate even against an AI-based detector. We have responsibly disclosed these findings to the ROS 2 development team. We prepared a demo video available at https://youtu.be/ExeiGqUrnhQ.

</details>


### [96] [EvoNav-Bench: Benchmarking Lifelong Navigation in Evolving Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08292)
*Xilin Wang,Guoxi Zhang,Hongming Xu,Zhuofan Zhang,Tianxu Wang,Lifeng Fan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了EvoNav-Bench基准，用于评估终身导航智能体在环境动态演化场景下的表现。研究发现在环境变化时，现有的基于场景表示重用的导航方法表现脆弱，而三种启发式适应策略（Frontier-Update、Fail-then-Update和Stage-Reset）可以有效缓解环境演化带来的负面影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有终身导航研究通常假设环境是静止不变的，但在真实世界中人类活动会导致环境持续演化。静止假设被违反时，现有方法可能融合过时的先验观测与新的观测，导致导航性能下降。然而当前的基准无法揭示这一失败模式，因此需要一个新的基准来评估环境演化对终身导航智能体的影响。

Method: 基于ProcTHOR框架构建EvoNav-Bench，扩展了GOAT-Bench风格的终身导航任务设定，在导航子任务之间引入环境修改操作，使先验经验仍然有用但不再完全可靠。通过三种启发式策略（Frontier-Update、Fail-then-Update、Stage-Reset）与三种现有场景表示重用方法进行对比实验。

Result: 基准测试结果表明，现有方法在环境演化条件下表现脆弱，无法有效处理环境变化带来的信息过时问题。相比之下，三种简单启发式策略能够对智能体如何适应场景变化提供可控分析，并有效缓解环境演化对导航性能的负面影响。

Conclusion: 环境的动态演化是终身导航任务中不可忽视的重要因素。EvoNav-Bench为社区提供了首个在演化环境下评估终身导航智能体的基准，并揭示了现有方法在此类场景下需要更强的场景自适应和更新机制来维持长期的导航性能。

Abstract: Lifelong navigation (LN) requires an embodied agent to solve a sequence of navigation subtasks in the same environment. Since solving each subtask from scratch incurs redundant exploration, an LN agent must consolidate experience from earlier stages and reuse it in later stages, often through persistent scene representations such as scene graphs or visual snapshots. However, existing approaches typically assume a stationary environment, whereas in real-world LN settings, human activities can cause the environment to evolve. With the stationary assumption violated, existing methods may fuse outdated prior observations with new observations, yet current benchmarks cannot reveal this failure mode. In this paper, we present EvoNav-Bench, which extends the GOAT-Bench style LN formulation in the context of evolving environments. Built on the ProcTHOR framework, EvoNav-Bench introduces environment modifications between navigation tasks, making prior experience useful but not fully reliable. This design enables controlled evaluation of how environment evolution affects LN agents that reuse prior scene observations. Using EvoNav-Bench, we benchmark three recent methods that build and reuse scene representations for navigation. We also compare three simple heuristic strategies for handling environment evolution: Frontier-Update, Fail-then-Update, and Stage-Reset. Our results show that existing methods are brittle under environment evolution, while the heuristic strategies enable a controlled analysis of how agents can adapt to scene changes and mitigate their impact.

</details>


### [97] [A Multi-Modal Perception Pipeline for Object Detection and Tracking in Autonomous Racing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08338)
*Davide Malvezzi,Michele Pestarino,Vittoria Cavicchioli,Valentina La Gamba,Silvia Severi,Fabio Bagni,Luca Bartoli,Massimiliano Bosi,Francesco Gatti,Micaela Verucchi,Ayoub Raji,Marko Bertogna*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向自动驾驶赛车领域的多模态后期融合感知流水线，通过融合摄像头、LiDAR和RADAR的独立检测结果，结合补偿检测延迟并嵌入车辆动力学与赛道布局先验知识的多目标跟踪框架，在真实世界多种关键场景（包括城市驾驶中的边缘案例）中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶赛车场景中车辆高速运行、振动剧烈、安全裕度小，且面临能见度受限、传感器噪声和故障等不利条件。现有感知方法在如此极端且动态的环境中难以保持鲁棒性，需要一种能够充分利用所有车载传感器并可靠追踪周围车辆的感知方案。

Method: 采用多模态后期融合方法构建感知流水线：首先分别从摄像头、LiDAR和RADAR生成独立的检测结果，然后通过后期融合策略将各模态信息组合，以获得及时且鲁棒的周围车辆状态估计。跟踪部分采用专门的多目标跟踪框架，显式补偿检测延迟，并在模型中嵌入车辆动力学和赛道布局的先验知识。

Result: 在真实世界数据上的实验评估覆盖多种关键场景（包括具有挑战性的城市驾驶边缘案例），实验结果表明所提出的感知流水线有效，能够支持安全且自适应的规划决策。

Conclusion: 通过拓展先前工作并融合全部车载传感器，结合考虑车辆动力学和赛道布局的多目标跟踪框架，提出感知系统在自动驾驶赛车等极限工况下表现出了良好的鲁棒性和实用性，其有效性亦延伸至城市驾驶场景。

Abstract: Object detection and tracking are fundamental components of perception systems for autonomous driving. Achieving robust performance under adverse conditions such as limited visibility, sensor noise, and failures remains an open challenge, particularly in autonomous racing, where vehicles operate at very high speeds, experience strong vibrations, and interact under small safety margins. This paper presents a multi-modal late-fusion perception pipeline for object detection and tracking in the autonomous racing domain. The proposed system extends previous work by exploiting all onboard sensors through a late-fusion approach and a dedicated multi-object tracking framework. Independent detections from cameras, LiDARs, and RADARs are combined to provide timely and robust state estimates of surrounding vehicles. The tracking method explicitly compensates for detection delays and embeds in its model prior knowledge of vehicle dynamics and track layout. Experimental evaluation on real-world data across diverse critical scenarios, representative of challenging edge cases also in urban driving, confirms the effectiveness of the proposed pipeline and its suitability to support safe and adaptive planning decisions.

</details>


### [98] [RoboCousin: Build Your Own Simulation Playground for Robust Bimanual Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08339)
*Jingxuan Zhu,Jingyi Li,LiangLiang Chen,Zhiyuan Jing,Jidong Zhang,Hongming Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboCousin是一个基于仿真的双臂操作数据生成平台，可将用户提供的观测图像转化为可复用的资产、场景和专家轨迹，并生成超过一百万条专家轨迹及包含3,000多个标注物体的数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双臂操作策略需要大规模且多样化的训练数据，但物理机器人上的演示收集昂贵且难以扩展。现有仿真管线局限于封闭资产库和预设场景，添加新物体或环境需要大量人工工作来重建几何、标注物理和语义属性。

Method: 基于RoboTwin 2.0构建，将物体图像转换为具备视觉与碰撞几何、语义物理元数据及自动生成抓取接触候选的可仿真资产；构建数字孪生变体来变化兼容物体、背景、布局和语言指令；支持桌面级和房间级场景构建，并具有碰撞感知的基座控制。

Result: 发布RoboCousin-OBD数据集（3,000多个标注物体实例、50个背景环境），生成超过100万条涵盖50个任务的专家轨迹。仿真和真实机器人实验表明自动生成的交互标注与人工标注相当，生成的资产提供有效的sim-to-real监督，能提升迁移性能。

Conclusion: RoboCousin为扩展合成双臂操作数据的规模和覆盖范围提供了一条实用路径，降低了对人工资产重建和场景标注的依赖。

Abstract: Bimanual manipulation policies require large and diverse training datasets, yet collecting demonstrations on physical robots is expensive and difficult to scale. Simulation can generate data efficiently, but existing pipelines typically operate within closed asset libraries and predefined scenes: adding a newly observed object or environment still requires substantial effort to reconstruct geometry, specify physical and semantic properties, annotate interactions, and integrate the result into executable tasks. We present RoboCousin, an extensible simulation-based data-generation platform that turns user-provided observations into reusable assets, scenes, and expert trajectories for bimanual manipulation. Built on RoboTwin~2.0, RoboCousin converts object images into simulation-ready assets with visual and collision geometry, semantic and physical metadata, and automatically generated grasp-contact candidates. It further constructs digital cousins that vary compatible objects, backgrounds, layouts, and language instructions while preserving task-relevant affordances and spatial relations. The same asset system supports tabletop and room-level scene construction, with collision-aware base control for interaction beyond a fixed workspace. We release RoboCousin-OBD, containing more than 3,000 annotated object instances and 50 background environments, and use RoboCousin to generate over one million expert trajectories across 50 tasks. Simulation and real-robot experiments show that the automatically generated interaction annotations are comparable to curated annotations, generated assets provide effective sim-to-real supervision, and tabletop cousins can improve transfer beyond training on a single reconstructed scene. RoboCousin therefore provides a practical path for expanding both the scale and coverage of synthetic bimanual manipulation data.

</details>


### [99] [Localized Visual Feature Aggregation via Focus Pooling for Visuomotor Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08408)
*Ruiyu Wang,Zheyu Zhuang,Danica Kragic,Florian T. Pokorny*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出FocusPool，一种新的注意力池化模块，能直接从CNN中间特征中聚合与机器人当前本体感受上下文相关的局部视觉特征，避免了对输入进行裁剪等预处理。实验表明，FocusPool在成功率和数据效率上优于现有池化方法和显式局部聚焦方法，且仅需训练5.8%的编码器参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉运动策略中，聚焦空间局部化的控制相关视觉线索能提高数据效率，但现有方法多依赖输入预处理（如裁剪），尚未充分探索是否可直接从CNN编码特征中暴露这些局部特征，因为现有池化方法无法有效聚合中间层的局部视觉上下文。

Method: 提出FocusPool注意力池化模块，根据特征与机器人当前本体感受上下文的相关性，选择性地聚合CNN的中间视觉特征，生成能捕捉任务进展、控制相关的局部信息表征，直接用于策略学习。

Result: 在仿真和真实世界实验中，与池化方法和显式局部聚焦方法相比，FocusPool将策略成功率分别提高了36.2%和41.2%，且仅需训练5.8%的编码器参数。

Conclusion: 中间CNN特征确实保留了控制相关的局部视觉上下文，FocusPool能够有效聚合这些特征，在不依赖输入预处理的条件下显著提升策略性能和数据效率。

Abstract: Focusing on spatially localized, control-relevant visual cues has been shown to improve data efficiency in visuomotor policies by reducing the need to model task-irrelevant visual variation. Existing methods often impose this focus through input preprocessing, such as cropping control- or object-centric regions in RGB images or point-clouds. However, it remains underexplored whether such localized features can be exposed directly from commonly used convolutional neural network (CNN) encoded features. In this paper, we show that intermediate CNN features preserve localized visual context for control, but existing pooling methods fail to aggregate it effectively. We introduce FocusPool, an attention pooling module that selectively aggregates intermediate visual features according to their relevance to the robot's current proprioceptive context. The resulting pooled representation captures task-progressive, control-relevant local information and is used directly for policy learning. Across simulation and real-world experiments, FocusPool improves policy success rates over pooling and explicit local focus methods by 36.2% and 41.2%, with training only 5.8% of encoder parameters.

</details>


### [100] [Coverage Path Planning for Redundant Manipulators using Generalized Spanning Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08409)
*Raksi Kopo,Kostas J. Kyriakopoulos*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对冗余机械臂的表面覆盖路径规划问题，提出了离线与在线两种联合生成树覆盖（JSTC）算法，分别通过GMST优化选择和增量式扩展回溯策略，实现非重复覆盖路径的高效生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冗余机械臂表面覆盖任务中，每个表面点可能对应多个逆运动学（IK）解，构型选择会显著影响运动质量，这使得传统覆盖方法面临挑战。

Method: 离线JSTC：对每个网格单元采样多个IK解，将问题建模为广义最小生成树（GMST），选择每个单元的最优构型并追踪生成树获得非重复覆盖路径。在线JSTC：增量式扩展并回溯生成树，结合可行性与代价评估，同时处理动态网格更新。

Result: 仿真结果表明，离线JSTC相比其他方法在计算时间、重配置次数和关节运动量方面均有降低；在线JSTC在动态场景中能够实现快速的逐步行规划。

Conclusion: JSTC算法有效解决了冗余机械臂的表面覆盖问题，在静态和动态场景下分别展现了较高的计算效率和运动质量优势。

Abstract: Surface coverage with task-redundant manipulators is challenging because each surface point may admit multiple inverse kinematics (IK) solutions, and configuration choices strongly affect motion quality. This paper extends the classical Spanning Tree Coverage (STC) method to redundant manipulators through offline and online Joint Spanning Tree Coverage (JSTC) algorithms. Offline JSTC samples multiple Inverse Kinematics (IK) solutions per grid cell and formulates the problem as a Generalized Minimum Spanning Tree (GMST), selecting one configuration per cell and tracing the resulting tree to obtain a non-revisiting coverage path. Online JSTC incrementally expands and backtracks a spanning tree with feasibility and cost evaluation while handling dynamic grid updates. Simulation results show that offline JSTC reduces computation time, reconfigurations, and joint motion compared to other methods, while online JSTC achieves fast per-step planning in dynamic scenarios.

</details>


### [101] [Safe Task Planning with Long-Term Graph Memory for Embodied Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08444)
*Siyuan Li,Taiyan Lang,Aoqi Yan,Jia Yu,Feifan Liu,Yihan Du,Yu Zheng,Xun Wang,Peng Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出SafeMem框架，通过构建和更新环境的长期语义图记忆，为具身智能体的零样本任务规划提供物理风险感知能力，在部分可观测环境下显著提升了安全成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM/VLM驱动的任务规划方法在生成高层安全动作方面存在不足，因为它们缺乏物理风险意识，尤其在部分可观测环境中，潜在危险可能位于当前视野之外。

Method: SafeMem框架：(1) 基于自我中心的观测增量式构建环境物体的长期语义图记忆；(2) 利用LLM作为风险预测器，基于图记忆评估候选动作的安全性；(3) 设计一个由保守度调制的重规划循环，当检测到危险时提供解释并触发安全重规划。

Result: 在IS-Bench基准测试和真实机器人平台上，SafeMem相比最先进的VLM驱动任务规划器显著提高了安全成功率。

Conclusion: 通过长期语义图记忆与LLM驱动的风险预测相结合，SafeMem能够有效增强具身智能体在部分可观测开放动态环境中的安全任务规划能力。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) and vision-language models (VLMs) have significantly advanced zero-shot task planning for embodied agents. However, most LLM- and VLM-driven methods struggle to generate safe high-level actions due to a lack of physical risk awareness, particularly under partial observability, where hazards lie outside the immediate field of view. To address this challenge, we propose a novel safe task-planning framework, SafeMem, which constructs and maintains a long-term semantic graph memory of the open and dynamic environment. Based on egocentric observations, the proposed framework incrementally accumulates knowledge about surrounding objects and their relationships with a graph. Then, an LLM-based risk predictor evaluates candidate actions using the graph memory, triggering a conservatism-modulated replanning loop with explanations for detected hazards. Extensive experiments on the IS-Bench benchmark and a real-world robot platform demonstrate that the SafeMem framework substantially improves safe success rates compared to state-of-the-art VLM-driven task planners. Video results are available on our webpage: https://sites.google.com/view/safemem.

</details>


### [102] [Multi-bounce Drum Roll with Optimized Active Tricks to Leverage Soft Embodiment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08490)
*Naoto Yamanaka,Takanori Jin,Taisuke Kobayashi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种软体机器人鼓手，通过Tap-Pull和Micro-Pulse两种技巧结合贝叶斯优化，实现了高精度、高效率的鼓滚奏（drum rolls），软体端执行器在反弹次数和声学效率上均显著优于刚性端执行器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高速鼓滚奏需要"多次反弹技术"，即在单次挥棒中让鼓棒多次弹跳。机器人在复现该技术时，需要利用身体弹性，同时在击打过程中加入精细动作以最大化弹性潜力。

Method: 设计两种技巧：1) Tap-Pull(TP)技巧——在击打后增加回拉动作以增加反弹次数；2) Micro-Pulse(MP)技巧——在挥棒过程中注入微小振荡以维持击鼓音量。由于软体本身的非线性复杂性，两种技巧均通过贝叶斯优化以数据驱动方式高效调参。

Result: 软体TP实现了最高的反弹次数（每次挥棒12.25次）且时间间隔均匀；软体MP有效抑制了音量衰减，声学效率是刚性MP的6.8倍。

Conclusion: 所提出的技巧结合弹性与优化方法，能够使机器人演奏出色的鼓滚奏，验证了软体材料与数据驱动优化在机器人精细运动控制中的潜力。

Abstract: This paper presents a soft robotic drummer for accurate and efficient drum rolls. High-frequency drum rolls require the "multi-bounce technique," where a drumstick bounces multiple times with a single stroke. In robotic reproduction of this technique, the body's elasticity is key, while the fine motion during the stroke is also crucial for maximizing the potential of that elasticity. Therefore, we design two tricks: i) Tap-Pull (TP) trick to increase the number of rebounds by adding a pulling motion after impact; and ii) Micro-Pulse (MP) trick to keep the drumming volume by injecting small oscillations during the stroke. Due to the nonlinear complexity of soft embodiment, both tricks are efficiently tuned using Bayesian optimization in a data-driven manner for accomplishing the respective objectives quantified. We evaluated the optimized behaviors with soft and rigid end-effectors. As a result, the soft TP achieved the highest bounce count (12.25 per stroke) with uniform intervals. The soft MP suppressed the volume decay, yielding 6.8-times higher acoustic efficiency compared to the rigid MP. These results indicate that the proposed tricks with the combination of elasticity and optimization can make robots play excellent drum rolls.

</details>


### [103] [AURORA: Active Uncertainty-Driven Re-Orientation for In-Hand Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08493)
*Feiyu Zhao,Yuetong Li,Chenxi Xiao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出AURORA框架，通过Ray-GPIS在候选观察光线上估计重建不确定性，并以不确定性-新颖性目标选择下一最佳视角，由轴向条件的手内旋转策略实现，主动闭环驱动手内重定向以完成物体密集3D重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机械手抓取物体时存在严重视觉遮挡，现有手内操作常依赖预定义或开环重定向策略，未显式针对欠观测区域优化，导致重建质量受限。因此需要一种主动选择视角的方法来闭环指导旋转。

Method: AURORA由两个核心组件构成：(1) Ray-GPIS，沿候选观察光线估计方向性重建不确定性，并结合不确定性-新颖性目标选出最优下一视角；(2) 轴向条件的手内旋转策略，将选择的目标视角转化为具体的旋转动作。RGB-D观测通过无CAD的6D位姿跟踪和轻量级几何重建增量融合。

Result: 实验表明，相比非主动旋转策略，AURORA在重建质量和信息获取效率上均有提升；Ray-GPIS在重建性能、动作排序质量和规划效率上优于主动视角规划基线；针对性消融实验验证了其对机械手遮挡和位姿误差的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 主动视角选择与手内旋转策略的闭环结合能有效克服物体抓取过程中的视觉遮挡问题，AURORA为机器人手内主动重建提供了一条可行且高效的技术路线。

Abstract: Observing objects grasped by a robot hand is challenging due to severe visual occlusions. Although in-hand manipulation can expose hidden surfaces, existing approaches often rely on predefined or open-loop reorientation strategies that do not explicitly target under-observed regions. We propose AURORA, an active 3D reconstruction framework that closes the loop between online object-centric reconstruction and in-hand reorientation. At its core, Ray-GPIS estimates direction-wise reconstruction uncertainty along candidate viewing rays and selects next-best-view targets using an uncertainty--novelty objective, which are realized through an axis-conditioned in-hand rotation policy. The resulting RGB-D observations are fused incrementally using CAD-free 6D pose tracking and lightweight geometric reconstruction. Experiments demonstrate that AURORA improves reconstruction quality and information-acquisition efficiency over non-active rotation strategies, while Ray-GPIS also outperforms active view-planning baselines in reconstruction performance, action-ranking quality, and planning efficiency. Targeted ablations further validate its robustness to hand occlusion and pose errors. The project webpage is available at https://aurorahand.github.io/

</details>


### [104] [PGMT: Perceptive General Motion Tracking for Humanoid Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08511)
*Hongyi Li,Li Peizhuo,Yucheng Tao,Ze Wang,Fangzhou Xu,Jinyi Chen,Yanyan Yuan,Dapeng Jia,Yongbin Jin,Mingfeng Fan,Guillaume Sartoretti,Hongtao Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PGMT是一种面向人形机器人的感知式通用运动跟踪管线，通过独立选择的运动参考和地形学习地形自适应能力，并成功在Unitree G1上实现零样本部署，可穿越最高37cm障碍的真实地形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有人形运动跟踪器在地形无关参考变得物理不可行的复杂地形上性能会退化，需要让运动跟踪具备地形感知与适应能力。

Method: PGMT首先学习通用的运动跟踪与恢复先验，然后通过运动条件化的地形片段感知来选择性编码与当前运动相关的区域，并采用地形感知的运动跟踪松弛机制，允许偏离参考的同时保留运动意图。

Result: 在Unitree G1上零样本部署展示了鲁棒的地形自适应运动与全身动作执行能力，可穿越最高37cm障碍的真实地形，并支持遥操作、动态运动跟踪和摔倒恢复。

Conclusion: PGMT将通用人形运动跟踪从平地扩展到复杂地形，提供了一个统一的策略，用于地形自适应运动、多样化全身行为以及复杂环境中的遥操作。

Abstract: Humanoid motion trackers can reproduce diverse whole-body motions, but their performance degrades on complex terrain where terrain-agnostic references become physically infeasible. We present PGMT, a Perceptive General Motion Tracking pipeline for humanoid robots that learns terrain adaptation from independently selected motion references and terrains. PGMT first learns a general tracking and recovery prior, then incorporates terrain perception through motion-conditioned terrain glimpses that selectively encode regions relevant to the current motion. Terrain-aware tracking relaxation allows necessary deviations from the reference while preserving its motion intent. Zero-shot deployment on a Unitree G1 demonstrates robust terrain-adaptive locomotion and whole-body motion execution over real-world terrain with obstacles up to 37 cm high, while supporting teleoperation, dynamic motion tracking, and fall recovery. PGMT extends general humanoid motion tracking beyond flat ground, providing a unified policy for terrain-adaptive locomotion, diverse whole-body behaviors, and teleoperation in complex environments.
