</details>


### [105] [TASG-Explore: Traversability-Aware Sector-Guided Exploration for Ground Robot on Uneven Terrain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08512)
*Shaocong Wang,Shiliang Shao,Ting Wang,Guangjie Han,Lianqing Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出TASG-Explore，一种面向地面机器人的地形感知扇形引导自主探索框架，通过分层地形可通行性分析（可变体素地面拟合与自适应8位障碍编码）、扇形分割与动态拓扑路标图构建，以及扇形引导规划器，在保持高精度地形分析的同时大幅提升探索效率与覆盖率，在洞穴、森林和崎岖丘陵等复杂环境中超越六种主流规划器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地面机器人在不平坦地形上进行自主探索时面临探索效率、覆盖完整性和地形安全性之间的权衡：精细地形推理有助于局部可靠性但拖慢大规模探索速度，而粗粒度区域引导能快速覆盖开阔区域却可能遗漏狭窄通道和不规则可通行边界。

Method: TASG-Explore框架包含三个核心模块：（1）分层地形可通行性分析，采用可变体素地面拟合和自适应8位障碍编码；（2）将代价地图划分为扇形，增量更新扇形簇，提取地形耦合的前沿视点，并维护带有未知拓扑假设的动态拓扑路标图；（3）扇形引导规划器，选择区域目标并插入局部视点以生成高效探索路径。

Result: 在洞穴、森林和崎岖丘陵等多样化挑战性环境的基准实验中，TASG-Explore在六个代表性SOTA规划器中取得最佳综合性能。地形分析处理效率提升6.3倍且保持高精度，探索规划器在崎岖丘陵场景中探索效率提升51%、覆盖率最多提升2.95倍。大规模真实世界实验进一步验证了方法的实用价值。

Conclusion: TASG-Explore通过将精细地形推理与粗粒度引导策略有效融合，实现了探索效率与覆盖完整性的良好平衡，为地面机器人在复杂不均匀地形上的自主探索提供了一种高效且实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Autonomous exploration on uneven terrain requires ground robots to balance exploration efficiency, coverage completeness, and terrain safety. Detailed tsrrain reasoning improves local reliability but can slow large-scale exploration, whereas coarse region guidance expands quickly in open areas but can miss narrow passages and irregular traversable boundaries. To address this challenge, this paper presents TASG-Explore, a traversability-aware sector-guided exploration framework for ground robots. The framework first performs hierarchical traversability analysis using variable-voxel ground fitting and adaptive 8-bit obstacle encoding. It then splitting cost map into sectors, incrementally updates sector clusters, extracts terrain-coupled frontier viewpoints, and maintains a dynamic topological roadmap with unknown topological hypotheses. Finally, a sector-guided planner selects region targets and inserts local viewpoints to generate efficient exploration routes. Benchmark experiments in diverse challenging environments, including caves, forests, and rugged hills, show that TASG-Explore achieves the best overall performance among six representative state-of-the-art planners. The proposed traversability analysis improves processing efficiency by 6.3 times while maintaining high accuracy, and the exploration planner improves exploration efficiency by 51% and increases coverage by up to 2.95 times in rugged hill scene. Large-scale real-world experiments further demonstrate the practical value of the proposed method.

</details>


### [106] [Estimating Semantic Ambiguity via Gaussian Context Distributions for VLM-Driven Traversability Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08583)
*Ramona Häuselmann,Mario A. V. Saucedo,Christoforos Kanellakis,George Nikolakopoulos*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于视觉语言模型的穿越性估计方法，通过概念锚定将开放词汇的VLM预测映射到连续物理穿越性尺度上，并用高斯上下文分布显式建模语义不确定性，在GOOSE数据集上验证了该方法能有效关联视觉伪影和混合地形等不确定性来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非结构化环境中的自主导航需要强大的场景理解能力，但视觉语言模型（VLMs）常受语义歧义困扰，冲突预测可能导致危险的导航失败，因此需要显式建模上下文不确定性来提升安全性。

Method: 提出一个新颖的穿越性估计pipeline：采用概念锚定（Conceptual Anchoring）将开放词汇的VLM预测映射到连续物理穿越性尺度；将模型响应建模为高斯上下文分布（GCD），并基于该分布的统计特性推导出稠密穿越性地图和稠密不确定性地图。

Result: 在真实世界的GOOSE数据集上进行实验验证，所提出的不确定性度量与视觉伪影和混合地形重叠等歧义来源有效相关，方法性能具有竞争力，并提供统计不确定性估计的优势。

Conclusion: 该方法通过显式建模语义不确定性，为解决复杂户外环境中的语义歧义提供了统计层面的不确定性估计，从而实现更安全、更可靠的自主导航行为。

Abstract: Autonomous navigation in unstructured environments requires robust scene understanding, yet Vision-Language Models (VLMs) often suffer from semantic ambiguity, where conflicting predictions can lead to dangerous failures. To address this, we present a novel pipeline for vision-based traversability estimation that explicitly models contextual uncertainty. Our approach utilizes Conceptual Anchoring to ground open-vocabulary VLM predictions onto a continuous physical traversability scale. By formulating the model's responses as a Gaussian Context Distribution (GCD), we derive both a dense traversability map and a dense uncertainty map based on the statistical properties of the distribution. Experimental validation on the real-world GOOSE dataset demonstrates that our proposed uncertainty metric effectively correlates with sources of ambiguity, such as visual artifacts and mixed terrain overlap. The method exhibits competitive performance while offering the distinct advantage of providing statistical uncertainty estimates to address semantic ambiguity, enabling safer and more reliable autonomous behavior in complex outdoor settings.

</details>


### [107] [MFVINS: Multiple Fisheye Camera-Based Visual Inertial System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08626)
*Eunseong Jang,YuJin Chung,Sang Jun Lee,Jihyun Yoon,HyungGi Jo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于多鱼眼相机与低成本IMU融合的视觉惯性系统MFVINS，通过IMU辅助的FAST特征跟踪器、归一化图像平面上的畸变离群点过滤以及基于学习深度估计的物理有效性约束重投影误差，提高了系统的鲁棒性，并能实时运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的视觉惯性系统（VINS）通常仅使用单个相机作为视觉输入，在遮挡、光照变化和无纹理环境下容易出现误差累积问题，影响定位与建图精度。

Method: 提出MFVINS，包含三个关键组件：(1)面向多相机的IMU辅助FAST特征跟踪器，实现高效局部特征提取与鲁棒匹配；(2)在归一化图像平面上过滤由鱼眼畸变引起的离群点；(3)结合学习式深度估计，提出带物理有效性约束的重投影误差用于束调整（bundle adjustment）。

Result: 将MFVINS应用于多种场景，并与先前VINS方法进行对比实验，验证了其有效性。MFVINS以实时方式运行，在保持多相机优势（对遮挡和无纹理区域的鲁棒性）的同时降低了计算负担。

Conclusion: MFVINS通过多鱼眼相机配置有效解决了单目VINS在遮挡、光照变化和无纹理环境下的误差累积问题，在提升鲁棒性的同时实现了实时处理能力，证明了多相机融合方法相较传统单目VINS的性能优势。

Abstract: A simultaneous localization and mapping (SLAM) method using a monocular camera and a low-cost inertial measurement unit (IMU) sensor is an effective way to fulfill a low-cost sensor configuration. Using this sensor configuration, visual-inertial system (VINS) focuses on fusing data from a camera and an IMU sensor to estimate the six degrees-of-freedom (DOF) of the sensor pose. Typically, VINS uses only a single camera as visual input, which lead to problems such as error accumulation due to occlusion, various illumination, and textureless environments. In this paper, we propose a new multiple fisheye camera-based visual-inertial system called MFVINS. We present an IMU-aided FAST feature tracker for multiple cameras that enables efficient extraction and robust matching of local features. Then, the proposed method filters out outliers caused by fisheye distortion on the normalized image plane. Subsequently, a new reprojection error with physical validity constraints is proposed for bundle adjustment using learning-based depth estimation. The proposed method is applied to various scenarios, and its effectiveness is demonstrated by comparing previous VINS methods. In particular, MFVINS is implemented in real-time process to leverage the advantages of using multiple cameras -- robustness against occlusion and textureless regions -- while reducing the computational burden.

</details>


### [108] [CASD: Chunk-Aligned Semantic Distillation for Multi-StageRobot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08638)
*Tinghe Ding,Jiahao Li,He Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Chunk-Aligned Semantic Distillation (CASD)，利用离线视觉语言模型将演示分解为阶段描述，为整个动作片段生成加权的语义目标（包括阶段间的转换）。生成器被冻结后用于条件化策略训练。在多个基准（LIBERO、RoboTwin 2.0、MolmoSpaces）上，集成CASD的多种方法（IDM、Joint、DreamZero）相比已发表基线均有提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动作片段可能跨越操作任务的多个阶段，但现有标签仅描述当前阶段的第一步，缺乏对整个动作片段的语义覆盖，限制了策略学习的语义指导。

Method: CASD方法分为三步：1) 离线使用视觉语言模型将示范视频分割为带描述阶段，计算每个阶段在动作片段内的占用比例，生成加权语义目标（包含阶段切换）；2) 训练CASD生成器，从当前观测、机器人状态和任务指令预测语义目标；3) 冻结生成器，训练以生成器预测为条件的策略。语义分支每次策略查询只运行一次，无需在线VLM调用或推理跟踪解码。

Result: 在LIBERO上IDM+CASD达到98.9%平均成功率（对比基线98.0%）；Joint+CASD在RoboTwin 2.0达93.0%（对比90.6%）；DreamZero+CASD在MolmoSpaces四类操作平均达47.9%（对比40.7%）；同时分析了不同主干集成的性能差异。

Conclusion: CASD通过为整个动作片段提供对齐语义目标，有效提升了操纵任务的成功率，且在不同策略骨架（IDM、Joint、DreamZero）上均展现出正向增益，但性能受主干集成方式影响。

Abstract: An action chunk can span several stages of a manipulation task, yet a label for its first step describes only the current stage. We introduce Chunk-Aligned Semantic Distillation (CASD), which derives semantic targets for entire action chunks. An offline vision--language model segments demonstrations into described stages. Their occupancy within each action chunk determines a weighted semantic target, including transitions between stages. A CASD generator learns to predict this target from the current observation, robot state, and task instruction. We then freeze the generator and train a policy conditioned on its predictions. The semantic branch runs once per policy query, without online VLM calls or reasoning-trace decoding. Teacher matching on annotated LIBERO training episodes is above chance for both single-stage and boundary-crossing chunks. We evaluate three Fast-WAM variants and a DreamZero integration across four benchmarks, including distribution shifts on LIBERO-Plus. Compared with published references, IDM+CASD reaches 98.9\% versus 98.0\% average success on LIBERO, while Uncond falls below its reference. Joint+CASD reaches 93.0\% versus 90.6\% on RoboTwin 2.0, and DreamZero+CASD reaches a 47.9\% four-category MolmoSpaces manipulation average versus 40.7\%. Performance varies across backbone integrations.

</details>


### [109] [Learning to build covering structures with continuous adjustments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08669)
*Gabriel Vallat,Maryam Kamgarpour,Stefana Parascho*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于强化学习的机器人建造方法（HSAC），完全摒弃预定义建造计划，通过图结构状态表示和混合动作空间，在物理建造过程中自适应地生成建造序列。该方法在模拟和真实双机器人系统中均成功验证了跨越拱门的搭建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器人建造依赖刚性高精度的预定义计划，无法适应物理制造中固有的公差、误差和意外变化，需要更灵活的自适应建造方法。

Method: 采用强化学习方法，使用图结构状态表示和混合（参数化）动作空间（离散块选择+连续放置参数）；将单边约束边引入图神经网络，并将soft actor-critic（SAC）扩展到混合动作空间以提升探索效率。

Result: 与先前方法混合PPO（HPPO）相比，HSAC在渐近性能和样本效率上显著更优；对超参数选择具有鲁棒性，可处理多达10个离散动作而不退化；在真实双机器人平台上成功闭环搭建了3D打印块的跨越拱门，验证了模拟到真实的迁移能力。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，完全摒弃预定义计划的强化学习方法可以在机器人建造中实现高适应性和可迁移性，为将来更复杂、更灵活的材料高效建造提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Robotic construction offers the potential to use materials more efficiently and create complex geometries, but current methods rely on rigid, high-precision plans that cannot accommodate the tolerances, inaccuracies, and unexpected changes inherent in physical fabrication. In this work, we introduce a reinforcement learning approach that forgoes predefined plans entirely, instead generating construction sequences adaptively as the structure is built. Our method operates on graph-structured state representations and a mixed (parameterized) action space, requiring both discrete block selection and continuous placement parameters. Because the stability simulation of a structure is computationally heavy, we develop an efficient exploration strategy by incorporating unilateral edges into graph neural networks, extending soft actor-critic (SAC) to this hybrid setting. We evaluate our algorithm, HSAC, against the prior method hybrid-PPO (HPPO), demonstrating significantly higher asymptotic performance and good sample efficiency. We also demonstrate HSAC's robustness to hyperparameter choices and its exploration capability, handling up to 10 discrete actions without performance degradation. Finally, we validate our approach on a physical two-robot setup, successfully building a spanning arch with 3D-printed blocks in closed-loop execution, confirming that policies trained in simulation transfer to real hardware.

</details>


### [110] [BIFTA: Brain-Inspired Few-Shot Tactile Adaptation for Unknown Sensors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08673)
*Boheng Liu,Ziyu Li,Xia Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出BIFTA框架，受大脑快速感觉适应机制启发，通过少量带标签数据将冻结编码器适配到未知触觉传感器，在三个触觉数据集上大幅超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不同触觉传感器的光学设计、弹性体力学和成像几何差异大，导致已训练模型在未知传感器上性能骤降（性能崩溃）。需要一种数据高效的适配方法，使触觉模型能够跨异构传感器硬件迁移。

Method: BIFTA框架包含三个关键组件：1) 双视图统计记忆——保留预训练表征；2) 支持条件谱图——修复传感器相关的特征邻域；3) 不确定性门控循环传播——增强可靠的跨查询证据传播。通过少量带标签支持集将冻结编码器适配至未知触觉传感器。

Result: 在SITR数据集上仅用10%带标签目标数据，BIFTA将平均Sparsh准确率从冻结源分类器的6.86%提升至87.09%，超过最强基线47.22个百分点；增益跨数据集、预训练骨干和触觉任务普遍存在。

Conclusion: BIFTA验证了对未知触觉传感器进行数据高效适配的有效性，为跨异构硬件的触觉模型迁移提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Advances in tactile sensing have made contact-rich perception possible, accelerating progress in robotic manipulation, material understanding, and embodied interaction. However, because optical design, elastomer mechanics, and imaging geometry differ substantially across tactile sensors, models trained on known sensor types can suffer an abrupt performance collapse on unknown sensors. To address this problem, we propose the Brain-Inspired Few-Shot Tactile Adaptation (BIFTA) framework; it draws on the brain's rapid sensory adaptation mechanism to adapt a frozen encoder to an unknown tactile sensor from a small labeled support set. BIFTA preserves pretrained representations through dual-view statistical memory, constructs support-conditioned spectral graphs to repair sensor-dependent feature neighborhoods, and applies uncertainty-gated recurrent propagation to strengthen reliable cross-query evidence. Extensive benchmarks across three tactile datasets show that BIFTA substantially improves adaptation to unknown sensors: with only 10\% labeled target data on SITR, it raises mean Sparsh accuracy from 6.86\% for the frozen source classifier to 87.09\%, exceeding the strongest implemented prior comparison by 47.22 percentage points, and these gains generalize across datasets, pretrained backbones, and tactile tasks. These results validate BIFTA for data-efficient adaptation to unknown tactile sensors and offer a promising route toward tactile models that transfer across heterogeneous hardware.

</details>


### [111] [HiBRIDGE: A Hierarchical Bayesian Neural Network Framework for Interpretable Dialogue Management in Group-Robot Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08678)
*Massimiliano Nigro,Hatice Gunes,Micol Spitale,Fethiye Irmak Dogan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出HiBRIDGE，一个用于群体-机器人对话管理的分层贝叶斯神经网络框架。其贝叶斯公式支持不确定性感知预测，分层结构将行为选择建模为多阶段决策过程。实验表明贝叶斯方法优于确定性方法，且分层模型生成的解释更受用户青睐，同时通过真人实验验证了实时自主群体交互的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多主体人机交互中，机器人需要持续决定对谁说话和说什么，但现实中多个行为可能同时合理，现有方法在表达不确定性方面有限，也缺乏将决策结构化为语义上有意义的中间步骤的能力，使机器人决策难以解释。

Method: 提出HiBRIDGE分层贝叶斯神经网络框架，采用贝叶斯公式实现不确定性感知预测并从有限交互数据中稳健学习，通过分层结构将行为选择建模为结构化多阶段决策过程，并利用决策树代理模型探究可解释性。

Result: 在三个离线群体人机交互数据集上，贝叶斯方法优于确定性方法和多个最先进基线；在线研究（N=20）中分层模型的解释更受用户好评；真人研究（N=12）中验证了实时自主群体交互的可行性，分层和平面贝叶斯变体均获正面评价。

Conclusion: HiBRIDGE结合了强大的预测性能与结构化的决策过程，能够支持对机器人行为更具可解释性的说明，为群体机器人对话管理提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: In multi-party human-robot interaction, a robot must continuously decide whom to address and what to say to participate effectively in the conversation. In real-world interactions, this is challenging because several behaviours may be plausible at the same time: a robot might continue a topic with one participant, involve another through a question, or address the whole group, with the appropriate choice depending on both whom it addresses and the interaction context. Current approaches remain limited in representing uncertainty when several behaviours are plausible and in structuring decisions into semantically meaningful intermediate steps that make robot decisions easier to interpret. Addressing these, we present HiBRIDGE, a hierarchical Bayesian neural network framework for group-robot dialogue management. Its Bayesian formulation enables uncertainty-aware prediction and robust learning from limited interaction data, while the hierarchical approach formulates behaviour selection as a structured, multi-stage decision process. We further use decision-tree surrogates to investigate whether this structure can support more interpretable explanations. Across three offline group-HRI datasets, our findings show that Bayesian formulations outperform their deterministic counterparts and several state-of-the-art baselines. Next, through an online study (N=20), we show that explanations derived from the hierarchical model are rated as more helpful for understanding robot behaviour and are preferred over those derived from the flat model. Finally, through our in-person study (N=12), we demonstrate the feasibility of HiBRIDGE for autonomous real-time group interaction, with both hierarchical and flat Bayesian variants positively perceived. Overall, HiBRIDGE combines strong predictive performance with a structured decision process that supports more interpretable explanations of robot behaviour.

</details>


### [112] [DCLP++: Learning to Navigate with Footprint Clearance and Relative Motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08711)
*Shanze Wang,Wei Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出DCLP++，一种基于足迹间隙的局部导航框架，用地面上机器人脚印的最短欧氏距离替代传感距离作为几何特征，用于动态环境中的导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统局部导航将观测建立在传感器距离上，未能充分考虑机器人占用体积（footprint）对相对运动特征的影响，导致在动态障碍物环境中性能受限。

Method: 将每次有效LiDAR返回映射到填充后的机器人足迹的最短欧氏距离，再进行互易编码；通过径向测量或模拟的平面相对速度提供短时域特征，且无需在策略输入中加入静态/动态标签。

Result: 在20个移动障碍物、限速1 m/s的矩形机器人环境下，100个固定验证任务中，两个种子在20万环境步后采用传感距离的平均成功率为42%，采用足迹间隙的平均成功率为70%。运动变体带来混合的额外增益。

Conclusion: 实验结果支持在评测场景中基于足迹间隙的观测方案的优势；但可靠的运动特征收益以及跨机器人迁移仍需进一步验证。

Abstract: We present DCLP++, a local navigation frameworkthat uses footprint clearance as the geometric basis for studying relative motion features in dynamic environments. Each valid LiDAR return is mapped to its shortest Euclidean distance from the filled robot footprint before reciprocal encoding, replacing distance from the sensor with distance to the occupied body. Radial measurementsor simulated planar relative velocities provide short-horizon features without static-dynamic labels in the policy input. A preliminary study uses a rectangular robot with a speed limit of 1 m/s among 20 moving obstacles. On 100 fixed validation tasks, two selected training seeds yield mean success rates of 42% with sensor rangeand 70% with footprint clearance after 200,000 environment steps.Motion variants show mixed additional gains. These results supportthe clearance-based observation in the evaluated setting; reliable motion benefits and transfer across robots require further evaluation.

</details>


### [113] [FOCI Policy: Focus on Object-Centric Interactions for Relational Manipulation Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08743)
*Ze Fu,Pinhao Song,Yutong Hu,Renaud Detry*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Foci Policy，一种以交互为中心的操纵策略框架，通过(1)时间维度上自动提取紧凑交互片段，(2)空间维度上用任务相关物体间的相对SE(3)运动表示技能，实现双重抽象。在RLBench、COLOSSEUM和真实世界任务上，该策略以显著更少的训练数据达到强性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以对象为中心的操纵策略通过建模对象运动而非直接预测机器人动作来提升泛化能力，但现有方法受限于表征——要么过于简单无法捕捉交互动力学，要么过于密集导致学习效率低下。

Method: 提出交互中心框架Foci Policy，实现双重抽象：(1)时间维度——从演示中自动提取紧凑交互片段；(2)空间维度——将技能表示为任务相关对象间的相对SE(3)运动，从而对场景配置和机器人本体具有不变性。

Result: 在RLBench、COLOSSEUM和真实世界任务上的实验表明，Foci Policy以远少于先前对象中心和行为中心策略的训练数据实现了强性能。

Conclusion: 建模对象-对象交互为刚性关系操纵提供了一种简单且高效的归纳偏置，验证了许多刚性关系操纵任务由约束紧密的短交互阶段支配这一观察。

Abstract: Object-centric manipulation policies improve generalization by modeling object motion instead of directly predicting robot actions. However, existing methods are often limited by representations which are either too simplistic to capture interaction dynamics or too dense to learn efficiently. We observe that many rigid relational manipulation tasks are governed by short interaction phases where the relative motion between task-relevant objects is tightly constrained. Based on this observation, we propose \textsc{Foci Policy}, an interaction-centric framework that achieves a two-fold abstraction: (1) temporally, by automatically extracting compact interaction segments from demonstrations;(2) spatially, by representing skills as relative $SE(3)$ motion between task-relevant objects, yielding invariance to scene configurations and robot embodiment. Experiments on RLBench, COLOSSEUM, and real-world tasks show that \textsc{Foci Policy} achieves strong performance with substantially less training data than prior object-centric and action-centric policies. These results suggest that modeling object-object interactions provides a simple and efficient inductive bias for rigid relational manipulation. Project page: \href{https://fitz0401.github.io/foci-page/}{fitz0401.github.io/foci-page/}.

</details>


### [114] [A Controlled Comparison of Manual and Teleoperated Intraocular Instrument Motion for an Input Device](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08770)
*Korab Hoxha,Mirza Imamovic,Angelo Henriques,M. Ali Nasseri*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究比较了手动与遥操作两种方式在机器人显微手术中的器械运动差异，发现任务结果相同但执行过程在所有指标上均有显著区别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 显微手术输入设备常被宣称能保留外科医生的训练技术，但这种说法很少被量化验证。现有比较无法固定器械变量，因为机器人器械与手动器械本质上不同。

Method: 16名参与者在商用眼科模拟器上分别通过手动方式和三自由度输入设备操作五关节机器人完成导航任务，保持套管针约束、器械、眼球模型和追踪源一致，仅改变控制界面。

Result: 两种方式下任务结果均达到天花板水平（全部获得五个目标且无损伤）。但执行指标全面不同：遥操作耗时3倍、中位速度仅为四分之一、角工作范围不足一半、分离动作次数为3.5倍。手指活动量翻倍、捏合力变异度增至3倍。练习四次后完成时间和动作碎片化显著改善且未达平台期。

Conclusion: 遥操作界面保留了任务结果但重塑了执行方式，减少器械自由度并未减少手动努力，而是将其重新分配。速度上限和关节限制等配置参数对行为变化影响最小。

Abstract: Input devices for robotic microsurgery are frequently described as preserving the surgeon's trained technique, but the claim is rarely measured. We compared manual and teleoperated intraocular instrument motion with the trocar constraint, the instrument, the eye model and the tracking source common to both conditions, so that the control interface was the only factor varied. Prior comparisons cannot hold the instrument fixed, because a robotic instrument is not the tool used manually. Sixteen participants performed a navigation task on a commercial ophthalmic simulator by hand and through a three-degree-of-freedom input device commanding a five-joint robot. Task outcome was equal but at ceiling: every participant acquired all five targets under both interfaces with no retinal or lens injury. Execution differed on every measure. Teleoperated trials took three times as long at a quarter of the median speed, covered less than half the angular working range, and were broken into 3.5 times as many separate movements. Completion time and movement fragmentation improved substantially across four trials of practice and had not plateaued; the measures set by the configured rate ceiling and joint limit changed the least. Finger activity doubled and pinch variability tripled, so reducing instrument degrees of freedom redistributed manual effort rather than reducing it. The interface preserves the outcome and reshapes the execution.

</details>


### [115] [Ostrich: Taking Large Strides Through Stiff Contact in Differentiable Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08800)
*Aleš Kučera,Karel Zimmermann*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Ostrich，一种GPU加速的刚体模拟器，通过非光滑牛顿迭代在大时间步长（约0.1秒）下求解硬接触和摩擦问题，并利用隐函数定理以O(1)内存对收敛残差求微分，在保持MuJoCo精度水平的同时实现了比MJX快211倍、比Newton Semi-Implicit快4.7倍的仿真速度，并能在单张24GB GPU上同时微分8192个并行世界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可微模拟器在通过接触驱动基于梯度的优化时面临三角矛盾：基于tape的引擎（如MJX和Newton Semi-Implicit）需要极小时间步以保证接触数值可解性，且反向传播内存随时间步数T线性增长；替代代理模型虽限制了内存但通过近似接触，导致梯度丢失了优化所依赖的几何信息。亟需一种兼顾仿真精度、梯度可靠性和单步成本的方案。

Method: Ostrich采用非光滑牛顿迭代在高达h~0.1秒的大时间步长下解析硬接触与摩擦力，并通过隐函数定理对收敛残差进行微分，复用前向Schur补来计算伴随量，实现每个时间步O(1)的内存开销。

Result: 在真实机器人穿越托盘障碍物的轨迹上，Ostrich在高达50倍更大的时间步长下保持了MuJoCo的仿真到现实精度；其梯度从随机初始化即可收敛（而MJX下降缓慢、Newton Semi-Implicit停滞）。单次热启动运行比MJX快211倍、比Semi-Implicit快4.7倍；在同一场景下可在单张24GB GPU上微分8192个并行世界，相比checkpointed MJX实现29倍的优化吞吐量提升。

Conclusion: Ostrich通过在大时间步长下保持接触精度并以O(1)内存计算精确梯度，突破了可微刚体模拟在精度、梯度可靠性与成本之间的三难困境，使基于梯度的轨迹优化能够首次应用于跨10秒时域、三角网格地形的复杂场景，这是此前方法无法实现的。

Abstract: Three properties determine whether a differentiable simulator can drive gradient-based optimization through contact: simulation accuracy, gradient reliability, and per-iteration cost. Tape-based engines such as MJX and Newton Semi-Implicit require timesteps small enough to keep contacts numerically tractable, and their backpropagation memory grows linearly with the number of timesteps T. Surrogate models bound memory by approximating contact away, but the resulting gradients lose the geometry the optimization depends on. We present Ostrich, a GPU-accelerated rigid-body simulator that resolves hard contacts and friction with non-smooth Newton iteration at large timesteps (h ~ 0.1 s), and differentiates the converged residual via the implicit function theorem, reusing the forward Schur complement to compute the adjoint at O(1) memory per timestep. On real-robot trajectories over a pallet obstacle, Ostrich holds MuJoCo's sim-to-real accuracy up to a 50x larger timestep. Its gradients converge from random initializations where MJX descends slowly and Newton Semi-Implicit stalls; a warm iteration runs 211x faster than MJX's and 4.7x faster than Semi-Implicit's. On the same scene Ostrich differentiates 8,192 parallel worlds on a single 24 GB GPU, sustaining 29x checkpointed MJX's optimization throughput; without checkpointing both baselines exhaust memory at far fewer worlds. We close with a gradient-based trajectory optimization demonstration over triangle-mesh terrain across a 10 s horizon, a setting where prior engines either restrict to primitive geometry or face the convergence and memory limits shown above.

</details>


### [116] [Graph-Based Safe Reinforcement Learning for Multi-Agent Systems with Time-Varying Topology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08802)
*Xiao Sizhe,Dong Lijing,Bai Rui,Tan Xin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于图的安全多智能体强化学习（MARL）框架，用于时变拓扑下的协同导航。通过引入一类控制障碍函数（CBLF）动作筛选层实现安全解耦机制，保证物理安全约束的严格满足；并采用基于注意力机制的Actor和图注意力网络（GAT）集中式Critic的统一架构，在真实差速驱动机器人平台上验证了其优越的稳定性和安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在具有感知约束的环境中（如视野受限），确保多智能体协同导航的安全性是一个关键挑战。现有方法难以弥合离散LiDAR感知与连续安全约束之间的鸿沟，且在学习过程中安全性无法得到严格保障，亟需一种能在时变通信拓扑下兼顾安全与稳定性的框架。

Method: 提出图基安全MARL框架，包含两部分：（1）安全解耦机制——通过CBLF动作筛选层桥接离散LiDAR感知与连续安全约束，使物理安全约束独立于学习进度而严格成立；（2）统一结构架构——采用基于注意力的Actor和GAT集中式Critic。Actor利用值向量重构机制并结合协同跟踪误差矩阵编码相对几何关系，实现尺度不敏感策略学习；Critic建模时变交互结构以实现准确的全局值估计。

Result: 在真实差速驱动机器人平台上对框架进行了验证。实验结果表明，在视野受限的动态场景中，该方法表现出优越的稳定性和安全性。

Conclusion: 该框架成功解决了时变拓扑下多智能体协同导航的安全性问题，通过安全解耦与图注意力架构的结合，在真实机器人平台上验证了其在受限感知环境中的有效性与实用性。

Abstract: This paper presents a graph-based safe multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) framework for cooperative navigation with time-varying topology. To address the critical challenge of ensuring safety in environments with sensing constraints, a safety-decoupled mechanism is introduced through a Control Barrier-Like Function (CBLF) action screening layer. This mechanism bridges the gap between discrete LiDAR perception and continuous safety constraints, ensuring that physical safety constraints are strictly satisfied regardless of the learning progress. Building upon this safety foundation, a unified structural architecture is proposed, integrating a attention-based actor and a Graph Attention Network (GAT) centralized critic. The actor utilizes a value vector reconstruction mechanism that explicitly encodes relative geometric relations through a collaborative tracking error matrix, enabling scale-insensitive policy learning under time-varying communication topologies. Meanwhile, the GAT-based critic models evolving interaction structures for accurate global value estimation. The proposed framework is validated on real differential-drive robot platforms, and experimental results demonstrate superior stability and safety in dynamic scenarios with limited fields-of-view.

</details>


### [117] [Real-time Puncture Detection and Recovery for Pneumatic Soft Actuators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08804)
*Tejonidhi R. Deshpande,Tingyu Cheng,Josiah Hester*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于单个惯性测量单元（IMU）运动数据的软体气动执行器穿刺检测系统，能够识别被穿刺的气室并估计损伤严重程度，同时提出了一种故障恢复策略以维持失效后的驱动力，从而提升软体机器人的可靠性和安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软体机器人因其柔顺性适合与人及非结构化环境安全交互，但气动执行器常由软硅胶制成，易发生穿刺和撕裂等机械故障，严重限制了实际部署应用。现有研究缺乏对软体气动执行器损伤的实时检测与评估手段，亟需一种数据驱动的故障检测方法。

Method: 该方法利用单个IMU采集软体执行器的运动数据，提取特征训练异常检测器用于穿刺检测，并使用非线性模型估计损伤严重程度。系统配备一种多腔室气动软体弯曲执行器，通过选择性腔室充气实现多样化构型；利用腔室扰动方案识别被穿刺的具体腔室，异常检测器基于正常运行数据训练，通过重建误差检测损伤，严重性则通过单独模型在轻微修改条件下训练估估计。

Result: 系统能够有效识别被穿刺的腔室并提供严重程度评分。此外，展示了故障恢复策略，在执行器失效后仍能维持驱动力。整体提升了软体机器人系统的可靠性和安全性。

Conclusion: 通过实时、数据驱动的损伤检测方法，本文有效解决了软体气动执行器的脆弱性问题，为软体机器人在实际应用中的部署提供了技术保障，兼顾了实时监测与故障后恢复的能力。

Abstract: Soft robots offer safe and adaptive interaction with humans and unstructured environments through their inherent ability to deform and comply. Pneumatic actuators are one way to build soft robots. They are typically made from soft silicone materials and are especially effective for driving such systems, enabling smooth and adaptable motion. However, their compliant nature also makes them vulnerable to mechanical failures like punctures and tears, limiting practical deployment. To address this, we propose a puncture detection system for soft actuators using motion data from a single inertial measurement unit. Extracted features are used to train anomaly detectors for puncture detection and non-linear models to estimate severity. We also introduce a multi-chamber pneumatic soft bending actuator capable of diverse configurations via selective chamber inflation. Our algorithm identifies the punctured chamber and provides a severity score using a chamber perturbation scheme. Anomaly detectors are trained on normal operation data and detect damage through reconstruction errors, while severity is estimated by a separate model trained under slightly modified conditions. Finally, we demonstrate a failure recovery strategy to maintain actuation force post-failure. This approach enhances the reliability and safety of soft robotic systems through real-time, data-driven damage detection.

</details>


### [118] [CAST: Alternating State-Value Targets and Expanded Policy Gradients for Model-Based Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08853)
*Pietro Noah Crestaz,Mohamed Yassine Kabouri,Nicolas Mansard,Andrea Del Prete*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CAST是一种基于模型的强化学习方法，通过状态值批评器结合规划器引导行为与当前策略的交替过程来提升价值学习，在DeepMind Control、HumanoidBench以及真实Unitree Go2四足机器人上均验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的将学习策略与在线规划结合的MBRL方法通常学习策略本身的价值，而非更强的规划器引导行为对应的价值，导致价值函数无法充分利用规划器带来的性能提升。

Method: 提出CAST方法，用状态值批评器替代动作值批评器，训练目标结合真实规划器引导的转移与当前策略下的想象转移，使价值函数对应规划器引导行为与当前策略之间的交替过程，从而兼顾规划器强行为与策略正则化。

Result: 在DeepMind Control和HumanoidBench基准上与多种先进方法对比表现优异，并成功迁移到真实Unitree Go2四足机器人执行动态倒立任务。

Conclusion: CAST证明了将规划器引导行为纳入价值学习的有效性，通过交替过程设计实现了从更强的规划器行为中获益的同时保持策略学习的稳定性，并验证了从仿真到真实机器人的迁移能力。

Abstract: Model-based reinforcement learning (MBRL) is a family of RL methods that learn a model of the environment and use it for action selection, making it well suited to robotics due to its sample efficiency. Combining learned models with online planning can further improve action selection, as the planner can exploit the model to find better actions than the learned policy alone. Recent methods combining learned policies with online planning typically learn the value of the policy rather than the stronger planner-guided behavior. We present CAST (Critic with Alternating State-value Target), which uses planner-guided behavior to improve value learning while regularizing the value estimate with the current policy. CAST replaces the action-value critic with a state-value critic, trained using a target that combines a real planner-guided transition and an imagined transition under the current policy. The resulting value function corresponds to an alternating process between planner-guided behavior and the current policy, allowing it to benefit from the stronger planner behavior while being regularised by the policy being learned. We evaluate CAST on the DeepMind Control and HumanoidBench Suites against several state-of-the-art methods, and demonstrate successful transfer to a physical Unitree Go2 quadruped performing a dynamic handstand.

</details>


### [119] [Visible-Reachable Workspace for Perception-Aware Humanoid Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08905)
*Boxi Xia,Zijiang Yang,Ryan Shin,Bokuan Li,Eric Wun-Hao Lu,Jacob Lee,Jiaxun Liu,Boyuan Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出可见可达工作空间(VRW)这一设计阶段评估指标，将可达性与感知可见性联合建模，并推广到多工作区域的同步可见性。通过在31自由度人形机器人上配备独立驱动的RGB-D相机，有源相机设计将可见可达覆盖率从38%提升到97%，双相机使成对覆盖率从0.45提升到0.95。硬件实验验证了无需躯干重新定向即可同时观测和操作前/后、左/右目标对，平均完成任务时间降低17%，机械能耗降低19%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统工作空间分析只衡量机器人的末端执行器可达性，但对于视觉引导的操作任务，一个运动学可达的目标可能无法在所需的位姿下被观测到，导致机器人需要额外的感知重定向或身体移动，将感知局限转化为额外的运动。现有的人形机器人在模仿人体形态时继承了这一局限。

Method: 提出VRW(可见可达工作空间)作为设计阶段度量，将可见性条件化在可行的到达配置上，并扩展至空间分离工作区域的同步可见性。构建了一个31自由度人形机器人，配备独立驱动的RGB-D相机，并通过对比实验和硬件测试验证设计。

Result: 相机关节化使可见可达覆盖率从38%提升到97%；在致动相机布局下，第二台相机将成对覆盖率从0.45提升至0.95，第三台仅提升到0.97。双致动设计相比固定相机将平均完成任务时间降低17%，机械能量降低19%。

Conclusion: 当可达性评估与使可达空间可观测的感知配置联合考量时，可达性成为感知驱动的人形机器人操作更具信息量的设计度量。研究结果支持在设计阶段考虑感知-运动协同，以改进人形机器人形态设计。

Abstract: Workspace analysis measures where a robot can place its end effector. For visually guided manipulation, reachability alone is insufficient: a kinematically reachable target may not be visible in the specific pose required to reach it. The robot must then redirect its sensing or move its body to acquire a view, turning a perception limitation into additional motion. Existing humanoids largely inherit this limitation when copying human form factors. We introduce the visible-reachable workspace (VRW), a design-stage measure that conditions visibility on feasible reaching configurations and extends it to concurrent visibility of spatially separated work regions. We apply VRW by building a 31-DoF humanoid with independently actuated RGB-D cameras. On the same robot, camera articulation increases visible-reachable coverage from 38% to 97%. With actuated camera layouts, a second camera raises pairwise coverage from 0.45 to 0.95, while a third changes it only to 0.97. In a controlled two-target reach-and-grasp benchmark, our dual-actuated design reduces mean completion time by 17% and mechanical energy by 19% relative to the same robot with its cameras fixed. Hardware experiments demonstrate simultaneous observation and manipulation of front/back and left/right target pairs without torso reorientation. The results suggest that reachability becomes a more informative design quantity for perception-driven humanoid manipulation when it is evaluated together with the sensing configurations that make the reachable space observable. We will open-source all software and the humanoid hardware design. Our website is https://generalroboticslab.com/DukeHumanoidv2

</details>


### [120] [Remotely Detectable Keyed Communication through Motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08920)
*Benjamin Chang,Michael Amir,Manon Flageat,Amanda Prorok*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究基于运动的机器人通信问题，提出一种将任意短消息（如智能体意图）编码为预先训练策略动作中噪声的方法，使机器人无需额外硬件或直接链路即可通过自身运动传递信息，并在真实50Hz机器人上实现四台机器人联合以0.67 bits/s的速率恢复8位消息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器人通信依赖文本、音频或无线电信号，需要额外硬件或直接链路。机器人运动本身具有丰富的表达力，但尚未被利用作为通信信道。作者希望探索通过运动本身传递消息的物理信道，作为标准无线通信的补充，增强通信的鲁棒性。

Method: 作者将消息编码为预训练策略动作中的噪声，在保证策略性能不下降的前提下传递信息。系统性地刻画了编码方案空间并推导了设计启发式规则，从远程传感（视频或动作捕捉）中解码消息。方法支持短载荷（如智能体当前意图）的任意消息内容编码。

Result: 在模拟环境和真实机器人部署中验证了方法效果。真实机器人以50Hz运行，四台机器人联合恢复一条8位消息，聚合速率达到0.67 bits/s。

Conclusion: 运动本身可以作为机器人通信的有效物理信道，与标准无线信道互补但不依赖其，无需额外硬件或直接链路，为机器人通信提供了一种新的鲁棒性维度。

Abstract: Messages from electronic devices are conventionally received as text, audio, or radio signals. But robots move with rich, articulate motion in the real world, opening up the possibility of transmitting messages through motion itself. In this paper, we consider the problem of motion-based communication, where we seek to modify a robot's movements so as to transmit messages detectable from remote sensing (e.g., video or motion capture), without degrading policy performance. We introduce a method for messaging through motion capable of encoding arbitrary message content over short payloads - such as an agent's current intent - as noise in any pre-trained policy's actions. This brings a new kind of robustness to robot communication: this 'physical' channel complements standard wireless communications channels but does not depend on them, requiring no extra hardware nor the establishment of a direct link to the robot. We systematically characterize the space of encoding schemes and derive design heuristics, then validate them across simulated environments and real-robot deployment; on real robots running at 50 Hz, four robots jointly recover an 8-bit message at an aggregate 0.67 bits/s.

</details>


### [121] [Model Predictive Control of Tensegrity Robots via Contact-Aware Graph Neural Dynamics Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08958)
*Nelson Chen,Patrick Meng,Charles Tang,Angelina Degay,Zachary Brei,Rebecca Kramer-Bottiglio,Kostas E. Bekris,Mridul Aanjaneya*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于三杆张拉整体机器人的模型预测路径积分（MPPI）控制器，该控制器基于学习到的图神经网络（GNN）动力学模型。研究扩展了先前的GNN模型以支持可微接触检测模块，使其能够处理非水平地形、障碍物和自碰撞，并引入混合MPPI策略结合转向运动原语以提升机动性。在MuJoCo五个导航任务中的实验表明，该方法优于A*重规划和纯MPPI变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 张拉整体机器人具有轻量、柔顺的特点，适合在复杂地形上移动，但其接触丰富的动力学和部分可观测性使其难以建模和控制，这构成了研究的核心挑战和动机。

Method: 该方法包括三个关键部分：（1）扩展先前的GNN动力学模型，加入可微接触检测模块以处理非水平地形、障碍物和自碰撞；（2）学习到的动力学模型与MPPI控制器形成闭环数据采集循环，迭代提升模型精度和控制性能；（3）引入混合MPPI策略，将MPPI与转向运动原语结合以提高机动性。

Result: 在MuJoCo中跨五个导航任务（墙体障碍、斜坡、窄通道、低净空结构及复合3D障碍赛道）的实验表明，混合MPPI控制器在学习GNN动力学模型上运行，相对于平坦地面基线模型提升了预测精度，并在导航性能上优于基于A*的重规划和仅MPPI变体。

Conclusion: 接触感知的学习动力学与基于采样的模型预测控制相结合，能够实现张拉整体机器人在复杂接触丰富环境中的稳健导航。

Abstract: Tensegrity robots offer lightweight, compliant mobility over challenging terrain but remain difficult to model and control due to complex contact-rich dynamics and partial observability. This work presents a model predictive path integral (MPPI) controller for a three-bar tensegrity robot driven by a learned graph neural network (GNN) dynamics model. This work first extends prior GNN-based models with a differentiable contact detection module. The extension allows the dynamics model to reason over non-horizontal planar terrains, obstacles, as well as self-collisions. Then, the learned dynamics model and the MPPI controller operate in a closed data-collection loop, iteratively improving model accuracy and control performance. This work further introduces a hybrid MPPI strategy that combines MPPI with turning motion primitives to improve maneuverability. Experiments are performed in MuJoCo across five navigation tasks, which include, wall obstacles, inclines, narrow corridors, low-clearance structures, and a composite 3D obstacle course. The experiments demonstrate that the hybrid MPPI controller operating over the learned GNN dynamics model improves predictive accuracy over a flat-ground baseline model and achieves superior navigation performance compared to $A^*$-based re-planning and MPPI-only variants. Results show that the contact-aware learned dynamics combined with the sampling-based model predictive control enable robust tensegrity navigation in complex, contact-rich environments.

</details>


### [122] [DYAD: A Multimodal Dataset of Co-Located Human Assistance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09023)
*Akhil Ajikumar,Mahya Qorbani,Sakib Reza,Sean Andrist,Mohsen Moghaddam*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DYAD是一个人类-人类协助的多模态数据集，记录了20次齿轮箱组装会话中培训助手与HoloLens 2佩戴者的交互过程，包含528个任务步骤、611个请求和851条协助记录，并提出了三个参考任务评估协助过程的关键能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有程序数据集只能描述单人的执行过程，交互数据集要么覆盖远程口头指导，要么覆盖无差别的协作，缺乏将协助者的言语和物理干预与执行者的请求、任务状态、协助触发原因及结果联合关联的数据。

Method: 构建DYAD数据集：在20个会话中，一名受训助手遵循"指导优先"策略，协助佩戴HoloLens 2的执行者完成齿轮箱组装。数据包含528个任务步骤间隔、611个请求以及851条涵盖言语和物理帮助的协助记录，基于第一视角和工作区传感进行多模态同步采集与标注。提出三个参考任务：因果步骤理解、发病前模式预判、教练响应生成。

Result: 在829个符合条件的模式事件中，最强四种子RGB平均宏F1为0.548±0.007；因果元数据达到0.624，特权触发映射达到0.915，表明仅靠发病前RGB图像无法恢复某些信息。

Conclusion: DYAD的贡献不在于数据规模，而在于其联动的交互结构——将求助识别、干预选择、执行和结果贯穿起来，在自我中心视角和工作区传感下提供协助过程的完整标注。

Abstract: An embodied assistant working beside a person must track task state, recognize help seeking, choose how to intervene, and produce an appropriate response. Existing procedural datasets richly describe individual execution, while interactive datasets capture remote verbal instruction or undifferentiated co-working. They do not jointly link a co-located helper's verbal and physical interventions to performer requests, task state, assistance triggers, and outcomes. We introduce DYAD (DYadic Assistance Dataset), a synchronized multimodal record of human-human assistance during gearbox assembly. Across 20 sessions, one trained helper follows a guidance-first policy while assisting HoloLens 2 wearers. DYAD links 528 task-step intervals and 611 performer requests with 851 valid assistance records spanning verbal and physical help. DYAD's annotations span the assistance process; three reference tasks evaluate selected components rather than an end-to-end system: causal step understanding, pre-onset mode anticipation, and instructor response generation. On 829 eligible mode events, the strongest four-seed RGB mean is 0.548 +/- 0.007 macro-F1; causal metadata reaches 0.624 and a privileged trigger mapping 0.915, revealing information not recovered from pre-onset RGB. DYAD's contribution is not scale, but a linked interaction structure spanning help seeking, intervention choice, execution, and outcome under egocentric and workspace sensing.

</details>


### [123] [A Distributed Consensus Particle Filter for Target Tracking using Autonomous Surface Vessels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09066)
*Carter Noh,Kyle Crandall,Connor Yates,Corbin Wilhelmi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出对经典粒子滤波器的一种增强方法，通过在多智能体海事目标跟踪中缺乏通信信息更新时，策略性地强制粒子扩散，以应对本地估计过度自信的问题，并在湖上USV实验中验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体海事目标跟踪中，各智能体需独立维护估计并在间歇性通信时利用机会通信，但缺乏外部数据时本地估计会过度自信，导致误差累积。

Method: 对经典粒子滤波器进行增强：在缺乏其他传感器节点信息更新时，策略性地强制粒子扩散，以缓解过度自信的本地估计误差。

Result: 在湖上使用USV进行实验，结果表明该增强方法不会降低正常情况下的性能，并在特定边缘情况下提供优势。

Conclusion: 该方法在不牺牲标称性能的前提下，能够有效应对间歇通信场景中本地估计过度自信的问题，对特定边缘情况具有鲁棒性优势。

Abstract: Maritime target tracking over large distances often requires multi-agent teams without centralized coordination, and intermittent communication. Each agent must maintain an independent estimate that can take advantage of opportunistic communications availability when possible. This can lead to overly confident local estimates in the absence of external data. In this work, we propose an augmentation to a classical particle filter implementation that accounts for this potential source of error by forcing particles to spread strategically in the absence of informative updates from other sensor nodes. We demonstrate our method using Unmanned Surface Vessels (USVs) on a lake, and show that our augmentations do not deteriorate nominal performance, and provide an advantage in some specific edge cases.

</details>


### [124] [Rethinking Learned Occupancy in Autonomous Active Mapping with Observation-Gated Filtering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09069)
*Jiahui Zhang,Bonian Han,Gongbo Liang,Yu Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究通过受控闭环基准实验，分析了自主3D主动建图中学习型占用补全对规划的影响，发现占用精度提升并不单调改善闭环覆盖性能，并提出一种观测门控滤波器来抑制不可靠的补全预测，在不重训练的情况下提升规划表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主3D主动建图需要空间机器人自主选择感知位置，学习型占用补全虽然能扩展空间上下文，但同一预测地图同时承担表面增益评分和碰撞约束两个规划角色，导致不可靠的占用预测会同时扭曲机器人"看向哪里"和"认为哪里可行"的决策，需要深入研究这一耦合关系。

Method: 采用受控闭环基准实验：固定主动建图系统不变，仅改变面向规划器的占用状态（仅观测、学习预测、真值修正、地面真值四种条件），通过25个起始点的系统对比进行诊断；基于诊断结果引入观测门控滤波器，在观测不足区域保留补全预测，只有经过重复视锥暴露且缺乏附近RGB-D支持时才抑制预测。

Result: 提升占用精度并不单调改善闭环覆盖：地面真值占用相比学习基线平均提前12.7步达到70%最终覆盖率，但最终覆盖率仅提升0.031。提出的观测门控滤波器无需重训练或地面真值即可改善目标失败频发的起始点表现。

Conclusion: 研究结果证明了在自主通信间隔期间应在线修正面向规划器的几何信息。当前研究假设基准RGB-D观测和足够精确的姿态估计，行星感知条件和累积定位漂移的影响仍需进一步评估。

Abstract: Autonomous 3D active mapping requires a space robot to choose where to sense while building the geometry needed for navigation. Learned occupancy completion extends spatial context beyond the current field of view, but one predicted map often serves two planning roles: it scores expected surface gain and constrains collision-free motion. Unsupported occupancy can therefore distort both where the robot looks and where it believes it can travel. We study this coupled interface in a controlled closed-loop benchmark by holding the active-mapping system fixed and varying only its planner-facing occupancy across observation-only, learned, oracle-corrected, and ground-truth conditions. Improving occupancy accuracy does not monotonically improve closed-loop coverage: across 25 starts, planning with ground-truth occupancy reaches 70% of the learned baseline's final coverage 12.7 steps earlier on average, while increasing final coverage by only 0.031. Guided by this diagnosis, we introduce an observation-gated filter that retains completion in insufficiently observed regions and suppresses predictions only after repeated frustum exposure without nearby RGB-D support. The filter improves both targeted failure-prone starts without retraining or ground truth. These results motivate online revision of planner-facing geometry during autonomous intervals between communication windows. The current study assumes benchmark RGB-D observations and sufficiently accurate pose estimates; planetary sensing conditions and accumulated localization drift remain to be evaluated.

</details>


### [125] [Online, Reachability-Aware, Sampling-Based Motion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09073)
*Brendan Gould,Zhiyuan Zhang,Panagiotis Tsiotras,Samuel Coogan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于区间分析的快速可达集超近似计算方法，为基于采样的模型预测控制（MPC）导航算法提供硬安全性保证，无需昂贵的预计算步骤，在仿真中将安全违规降低了99%以上，并在真实硬件上成功实现无碰撞控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于采样的MPC算法虽然灵活且广泛用于机器人导航，但历史上缺乏硬安全性保证，现有的可达性规划器虽能提供安全保证但需要昂贵的预计算步骤，难以扩展到复杂系统。

Method: 提出一种在线计算可达集超近似的快速区间分析流水线方法，将其集成到基于采样的MPC框架中，以实时提供硬安全性保证，替代了传统离线预计算的可达性方法。

Result: 该方法与最先进的可达性规划器性能相当，但无需预计算步骤；可扩展到现有方法无法处理的系统；在竞速仿真中安全违规减少超过99%；在真实模型赛车上无碰撞地成功实现了硬件控制。

Conclusion: 本文成功填补了基于采样MPC在硬安全性保证方面的空白，证明了在线区间分析流水线既能提供严格的安全保证，又能保持性能并避免预计算瓶颈，在仿真和真实硬件上均验证了有效性。

Abstract: Sampling-Based Model-Predictive Control (MPC) algorithms are a flexible class of controllers used for navigation on a wide range of robotic systems. Historically, such approaches have lacked hard safety guarantees, a shortcoming which we remedy in this work by computing guaranteed reachable-set overapproximations online with a fast, interval-based pipeline. We show that our method achieves similar performance to a state-of-the-art reachability-based planner without the need for the expensive pre-computation step, and can be scaled to systems that are infeasible using existing approaches. Finally, we demonstrate that our technique reduces safety violations by over 99% in a racing simulation and successfully controls a model racecar on real hardware experiments without crashes.

</details>


### [126] [DeCAL: Towards Physically-Grounded Dexterous Vision-Language-Action Models via Contact-Aware Latent Co-Imagination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09119)
*Yankai Fu,Ning Chen,Junkai Zhao,Heng Zhang,Guocai Yao,Pengwei Wang,Zhongyuan Wang,Shanghang Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了DeCAL，一种物理感知的灵巧视-触觉-语言-动作模型（VLA），通过Mixture-of-Transformers架构统一理解、想象与动作生成，引入自适应视触觉融合和视触觉潜在协同想象机制，在接触丰富的灵巧操作任务中达到71%的平均成功率和83.4%的进展成功率，表现超过现有SOTA方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧操作涉及接触丰富且细粒度的物理交互，现有VLA模型面临严重的视觉遮挡和复杂接触动力学挑战。已有工作虽引入触觉传感，但大多依赖同质多模态融合，缺乏自适应的触觉集成和对物理动力学的显式建模。

Method: 提出DeCAL模型，基于Mixture-of-Transformers（MoT）架构，为理解、想象、动作生成分别设置专用专家并实现高效信息流互通；设计了自适应视触觉融合机制，通过接触感知门控策略动态调节触觉信息交互；提出视触觉潜在协同想象，联合建模视觉与触觉动力学，赋予策略隐式物理世界知识。

Result: DeCAL在所有任务上均达到最先进性能，平均成功率为71%，进展成功率为83.4%，并在未见场景中表现出强大的泛化能力。

Conclusion: DeCAL通过物理感知的架构设计、自适应触觉融合以及视触觉协同想象，有效解决了接触丰富灵巧操作中的视觉遮挡与动力学建模难题，证明了该方法的有效性和泛化性。

Abstract: Dexterous manipulation involves contact-rich and fine-grained interactions with the physical world, posing significant challenges for existing vision-language-action (VLA) models due to severe visual occlusions and complex contact dynamics. While recent works have incorporated tactile sensing into robotic manipulation, most approaches still rely on homogeneous multimodal fusion, lacking adaptive tactile integration and explicit modeling of physical dynamics. In this work, we present DeCAL, a physically-grounded dexterous vision-language-action model that unifies understanding, imagination and action generation for contact-rich dexterous manipulation. Built upon a Mixture-of-Transformers (MoT) architecture, DeCAL leverages specialized experts for each capability while enabling efficient information flow among them. To effectively leverage tactile information, we introduce Adaptive Visuo-Tactile Fusion that dynamically regulates tactile interactions via a contact-aware gating strategy. Furthermore, we propose Visuo-Tactile Latent Co-Imagination to jointly model visual and tactile dynamics, equipping the policy with implicit physical world knowledge. Experimental results show that DeCAL consistently achieves state-of-the-art performance across all tasks, attaining a 71% average success rate and an 83.4% progress success rate, while also demonstrating strong generalization to unseen scenarios. The website is available at https://aureleopku.github.io/DeCAL.

</details>


### [127] [Proxy Policy Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09148)
*Chuanruo Ning,Tianrui Wang,Wei-Chiu Ma,Kuan Fang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Proxy Policy Steering (PPS)，一种推理时自适应方法，通过训练两个轻量级代理策略的校准速度空间差异来引导冻结的基础策略采样器，在不修改基础策略的前提下实现任务特化，同时保留其广泛操作能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用机器人策略具备大规模数据带来的广泛操作先验，但将其特化到新任务时存在部署瓶颈——需要从有限演示中引出任务特定行为，同时不损害其泛化能力。

Method: PPS训练两个轻量代理策略：参考代理建模冻结基策略在目标任务观测上的行为，任务代理从参考代理初始化并捕捉任务监督下的行为变化。两者差异形成校准的速度空间残差，在每次去噪步骤中引导冻结基采样器。基础策略从不被直接修改，其广泛能力在推理时保持可用，仅需基础策略的前向速度预测即可训练。

Result: 在8个真实世界和4个仿真操作任务上，PPS将先进的pi 0.5基础策略的平均绝对成功率提升了53%，在基础策略从未解决的任务上实现零到一的突破，同时保留基础策略的广泛能力。性能优于LoRA微调、从零训练专家、残差策略及先前的推理时引导方法。

Conclusion: PPS通过代理策略差异引导冻结基础策略实现任务特化，推理时无需访问基础策略参数，既轻量又有效，为通用策略的快速部署提供了新方案。

Abstract: Generalist robot policies carry broad manipulation priors from large-scale data, but specializing them to a new task remains the deployment bottleneck. This requires eliciting task-specific behavior from limited demonstrations without degrading their broad capabilities. We introduce Proxy Policy Steering (PPS), an inference-time adaptation method that resolves this challenge by training two lightweight proxy policies whose calibrated velocity-space difference steers the frozen base sampler. A reference proxy models the frozen base's behavior on target-task observations, and a task proxy, initialized from the reference, captures how this behavior changes under task supervision. Their difference forms a calibrated velocity-space residual that steers the frozen base sampler at every denoising step. We identify the conditions under which this residual isolates the change induced by task supervision, and validate them empirically. Because the base is never directly modified, its broad capabilities remain available at inference, including behaviors such as recovery from failure that the demonstrations themselves do not exercise. Adaptation requires only forward velocity predictions from the base, making PPS lightweight to train and applicable even without access to the base's parameters. On 8 real-world and 4 simulation manipulation tasks, PPS lifts the state-of-the-art pi 0.5 base policy by 53% absolute success rate on average, with zero-to-one gains on tasks the base never solves, while preserving the base's broad capabilities. PPS outperforms LoRA fine-tuning, from-scratch specialists, residual policies, and prior inference-time steering methods.

</details>


### [128] [TANGO: Humanoid Navigation in Cluttered Environments with a Whole-Body Vision-Language-Action Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09158)
*Anqi Li,Yuxin Chen,Zhaobo Li,Zhuo Cao,Junli Ren,Masayoshi Tomizuka,Dhruv Shah*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出TANGO，首个用于语言条件人形机器人在杂乱环境中通行的全身视觉-语言导航框架。给定自然语言指令和第一视角RGB观测，TANGO直接预测29自由度关节空间动作。模型在仿真中通过全局路径规划、运动学全身运动生成、障碍物感知运动编辑和基于强化学习的跟踪合成数据训练，并零样本部署到Unitree G1人形机器人上，无需真实世界导航数据即实现稳健的语言引导通行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统导航方法将人形机器人运动建模为2D路径规划问题，但人形机器人在杂乱环境中的通行需要连续的几何感知全身适应，包括手臂协调放置、躯干调整和步态调节，以实现复杂三维空间中的无碰撞运动。现有方法无法解决这一全身语言条件导航问题。

Method: TANGO是一个全身视觉-语言导航框架，将语言指令和第一视角RGB观测直接映射到29自由度关节空间动作。训练完全在仿真中完成，通过四个阶段的pipeline生成训练数据：1)全局路径规划合成多样化无碰撞通行行为；2)运动学全身运动生成；3)障碍物感知运动编辑；4)基于强化学习的跟踪，为语言条件全身策略提供动态可行动作监督。

Result: 在广泛的仿真实验中，TANGO在视觉-语言导航任务上达到最先进水平，并在需要障碍物协商的挑战性场景中优于强模块化基线。此外，TANGO在Unitree G1人形机器人上实现零样本部署，在杂乱真实场景中表现出稳健的语言引导通行能力，且未使用任何真实世界导航数据进行训练。

Conclusion: TANGO证明了直接从语言指令和视觉观测端到端预测整全身关节动作的可行性，通过纯仿真训练即可泛化到真实人形机器人，为语言条件下的人形机器人在杂乱环境中的全身导航开辟了新方向。

Abstract: We study the problem of navigating cluttered indoor environments with a humanoid robot. Unlike conventional methods that model navigation as a 2D path planning problem, humanoid traversal in cluttered environments requires continuous geometry-aware whole-body adaptation, including coordinated arm placement, torso adjustment, and gait modulation for collision-free movement through complex 3D spaces. We introduce TANGO, the first whole-body vision-language navigation framework for language-conditioned humanoid traversal in cluttered environments. Given a natural-language instruction and egocentric RGB observations, TANGO directly predicts 29-DoF joint-space actions for downstream whole-body control. We train TANGO entirely in simulation by synthesizing diverse collision-free traversal behaviors via global path planning, kinematic whole-body motion generation, obstacle-aware motion editing, and RL-based tracking. This pipeline provides dynamically feasible action supervision for learning language-conditioned whole-body policies. In extensive simulation experiments, TANGO demonstrates state-of-the-art performance in vision-language navigation, while outperforming strong modular baselines in navigating challenging scenes requiring obstacle negotiation. Lastly, we deploy TANGO zero-shot on a Unitree G1 humanoid robot, and observe robust language-guided traversal in cluttered real-world scenes without training on any real-world navigation data.
